FAPlace operates on a sufficiently large canvas, eliminating the circular dependency by allowing the optimal interposer footprint to emerge as an output of the optimization rather than a pre-specified input. At its core is a novel footprint mask that fuses area compactness with an aspect ratio penalty into a unified spatial cost map. Integrated with wirelength and thermal guidance masks, FAPlace delivers holistic multi-physics optimization in a deterministic, single pass process. Experimental results demonstrate that FAPlace reduces wirelength and footprint area while achieving near-unity aspect ratios, without compromising on thermal performance.

</details>


### [111] [HyNoC: A Hybrid Circuit-Switch/Wormhole Network-on-Chip for Distributed VLIW Computing on FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02729)
*Christophe Clienti*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: HyNoC是一种面向FPGA上VLIW分布式系统的开源混合网络架构，结合电路交换路径建立与虫洞数据传输，通过源路由实现确定性低延迟传输和软件级热点避免，在四主控象限配置下相比单主控实现5倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NoC架构在延迟、面积和拥塞管理之间存在根本性权衡，亟需一种在不引入虚拟通道面积开销的前提下同时实现低延迟传输和拥塞避免的替代方案，特别是面向FPGA上基于VLIW处理器核心的分布式计算系统。

Method: 混合交换模型（电路交换路径建立 + 虫洞数据传输），源路由编码，并行轮询仲裁器（PRRA）实现固定授权延迟，每端口独立时钟域，支持单播和多播路由，编译器辅助的静态路由分配。

Result: 四主控象限配置将Mesh划分为流量隔离象限，零跨象限链路流量，相比单主控实现5倍吞吐量提升；Verilator协同仿真测量了确定性每跳延迟；完整RTL实现以CERN-OHL-P v2许可开源。

Conclusion: 更丰富的拓扑结构结合编译器辅助静态路由是FPGA分布式VLIW系统中虚拟通道方案的有竞争力替代方案，HyNoC在吞吐量和确定性延迟方面表现优异。

Abstract: Network-on-Chip (NoC) architectures have become the standard interconnect fabric for many-core systems, yet most proposals face a fundamental trade-off between latency, area, and congestion management. This paper presents HyNoC (Hybrid Network-on-Chip), an open-source NoC architecture that combines circuit-switch path establishment with wormhole data transfer, targeting distributed computing systems built around VLIW processor cores on FPGA.
  HyNoC employs source routing, where the complete path through the network is encoded in the packet header by the sender or statically at compile time, enabling both deterministic low-latency transfers and software-level hotspot avoidance without the area overhead of virtual channels. The router features a parallel round-robin arbiter (PRRA) with fixed grant latency, per-port independent clock domains, and support for both unicast and multicast routing.
  We discuss the design rationale, the hybrid switching model, and positioning relative to prior NoC art, and argue that a richer topology combined with compiler-assisted static routing is a competitive alternative to virtual channels for FPGA-based distributed VLIW systems. Verilator co-simulation measures the deterministic per-hop latency and benchmarks the design at LLaMA 3 8B FFN up-projection scale: a four-master quadrant configuration partitions the mesh into traffic-isolated quadrants with zero cross-quadrant link traffic, yielding a 5x throughput improvement over a single master. The complete RTL implementation is available as open-source hardware under the CERN-OHL-P v2 license.

</details>


### [112] [AIGOR: A Modular, Event-Driven Neuromorphic Architecture for Configurable SNN Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03191)
*Pierpaolo Perticaroli,Roberto Ammendola,Andrea Biagioni,Ottorino Frezza,Francesca Lo Cicero,Michele Martinelli,Pier Stanislao Paolucci,Elena Pastorelli,Luca Pontisso,Cristian Rossi,Francesco Simula,Piero Vicini,Alessandro Lonardo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AIGOR是一种模块化、事件驱动的神经形态架构，由可参数化的计算、存储和通信IP模块组成，支持跨不同硬件平台（FPGA）的脉冲神经网络推理。它通过声明式配置生成神经核与突触存储映像，并在AMD Versal VPK180上验证了前馈分类器和递归平衡随机网络两种工作负载，前者复现了snnTorch精度，后者在多FPGA多核心上达到了NEST的脉冲级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SNN硬件生态碎片化严重，专用神经形态处理器、FPGA加速器和大规模神经模拟器各自围绕固定的神经元模型、执行策略或工作负载类别构建，缺乏统一的、可配置的模块化架构。

Method: AIGOR将神经元组织为时间步同步的处理核心，通过包交换通信层传输脉冲；从可参数化的IP模块库（计算、存储、通信）构建实例，而非为单一网络定制；使用声明式规范生成核心、神经元核和突触存储映像；采用事件驱动方式实现推理。

Result: 在AMD Versal VPK180上验证了两个工作负载：前馈图像分类器复现snnTorch参考精度；递归平衡随机网络在多FPGA多核心上达到NEST的脉冲级精度；报告了资源利用率；仿真验证了多节点同步方案可扩展至三维网格上的1000个核心；测量了吞吐瓶颈（突触传递数据路径和全局时间步屏障）。

Conclusion: AIGOR证明了可配置的模块化神经形态架构能够统一多种SNN工作负载，同时保持精度和可扩展性；识别出的瓶颈可通过架构的可配置结构在同一核心上进行数据路径优化。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs) run today on a fragmented landscape of hardware: dedicated neuromorphic processors, application-specific FPGA accelerators, and large-scale neuroscience simulators, each typically built around a fixed neuron model, execution strategy, or workload class. We present AIGOR, a modular, event-driven neuromorphic architecture for spiking neural network inference. AIGOR organizes neurons into timestep-synchronized processing cores that exchange spikes as packets over a packet-switched communication layer, and it is assembled from a library of parameterized compute, memory, and communication IP blocks rather than as a one-off design for a single network. The neuron model, numeric precision, the folding of neurons onto hardware, and the partitioning across cores are configured per instance rather than committed at design time; a single declarative specification then generates the cores, neuron kernels, and synaptic-memory images that realize a given network. We validate a first prototype on the AMD Versal VPK180 across two deliberately different workloads mapped onto the same cores: a feedforward image classifier trained in snnTorch and a recurrent bal anced random network modeled in NEST. The classifier reproduces its snnTorch reference accuracy, and the recurrent network matches its NEST reference at spike-level precision across multiple cores spanning two FPGAs. We report post-implementation resource utilization and validate the multi-node synchronization scheme in simulation up to one thousand cores on a three-dimensional torus. The prototype's measured limits localize the throughput bottleneck in the synaptic-delivery datapath and the global timestep barrier, and motivate a set of datapath refinements, now in development, that the configurable structure of the architecture admits as changes to the same cores.

</details>


### [113] [ArchEval: Measuring AI Agents as Computer Architects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03601)
*Chenyu Wang,Zishen Wan,Jeffrey Ma,Shvetank Prakash,Zhenting Qi,Haebin Do,Andy Cheng,Arya Tschand,Jiahe Shi,Yilun Du,Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ArchEval是一个评估LLM智能体在计算机体系结构设计与优化中能力的基准平台，包含20个挑战和8个模拟器。实验发现：在有完整工具支持的L1设置下，所有智能体均能达到或超越基线；但在移除支持后表现显著下降，仅GPT-5.5+Codex在L3设置中保持高于基线（1.21倍性能，65%胜率）。当前智能体更适合作为优化助手而非自主架构师。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机体系结构长期依赖基准测试来衡量进步，但LLM智能体带来了不同的测量问题：成功不仅仅是编写代码或调参，智能体需要理解工作负载、选择机制、使用模拟器、预测性能、满足硬约束并决定评估哪个可行设计。现有缺乏系统评估LLM智能体在体系结构设计任务上能力的基准。

Method: 构建包含20个挑战（覆盖CPU核心机制、系统架构、存储系统、加速器、存内计算）的基准，由8个模拟器支撑。设置三种评估层次：L1完整工具链含模拟器反馈循环；L2提供模拟器源代码但需自主构建工作流；L3纯智能体模式无运行时反馈。使用基线归一化验证器性能和完整轨迹记录进行评估。

Result: L1设置下四个智能体均达到或超过基线，能跨模拟器改进真实设计。移除支持后表现分化：仅GPT-5.5+Codex在L3中保持1.21倍性能（65%胜率），其余三个低于基线。性能建模通过率仅15%。L2中多个智能体无法将源代码转化为有效实验，L3预测与验证结果常不一致。

Conclusion: 当前LLM智能体更适合作为优化助手而非自主架构师。未来亟需提升的能力包括：模拟器工具使用、校准预测、预反馈判断、有用机制发现。

Abstract: Computer architecture has long used benchmarks to make progress measurable. LLM agents create a different measurement problem: success is not merely writing code or tuning parameters. The agent must interpret workloads, choose mechanisms, use simulators, predict performance, satisfy hard constraints, and decide which feasible design is worth evaluating. This paper introduces ArchEval, a benchmark and platform for evaluating LLM agents on computer architecture design and optimization. It contains 20 challenges across CPU core mechanisms, system architecture, memory systems, accelerators, and compute-in-memory, backed by eight simulators. Each challenge is posed under three settings: L1 full harness, with repeated simulator feedback; L2 simulator-code container, where simulator source is available but the agent must assemble its own workflow; and L3 agent-only, with no runnable feedback before submission. Each run reports baseline-normalized verifier performance and records the full trajectory, connecting results to workload analysis, simulator-tool use, prediction, constraint handling, and artifact integrity. Initial results show a sharp boundary in current agents. With L1 support, all four evaluated agents reach or exceed baseline and improve real designs across diverse simulators. Removing support exposes weaknesses: many agents fail to turn simulator source into useful experiments, and L3 predictions often disagree with verifier results. In L3, only GPT-5.5 + Codex remains above baseline, reaching 1.21x geomean performance and a 65% win rate; the other three fall below baseline. Even GPT-5.5 + Codex has only a 15% performance-modeling pass rate. ArchEval frames today's agents as useful optimization assistants rather than autonomous architects, and identifies capabilities needed next: simulator-tool use, calibrated prediction, pre-feedback judgment, and useful mechanism discovery.

</details>


### [114] [ELiTeFormer: An Efficient Transformer for FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03652)
*Victor Agostinelli,Nicolas Bohm Agostini,Antonino Tumeo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: ELiTeFormer首次在FPGA上实现了线性注意力与三元量化联合设计的Transformer架构，通过无乘法位掩码PE微架构实现10倍模型压缩和12.8倍KV缓存压缩，相比LLaMA 3在长上下文场景下取得3.9倍延迟降低和3.2倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer在FPGA部署时面临计算和内存双重挑战，而先前工作通常分别优化注意力机制或前馈网络，缺乏对两者的联合硬件加速协同设计。

Method: 提出ELiTeFormer架构，核心包括：(1) 混合线性注意力与三元线性投影的统一设计；(2) 创新的PE微架构，通过位掩码操作完全消除三元线性投影中的乘法运算，避免使用DSP块；(3) 面向FPGA的算法-架构协同设计流程。

Result: 模型权重压缩10倍，KV缓存压缩12.8倍；MMLU基准达31.9%（与BitNet b1.58差距<3.0%）；FFN操作加速9.6倍，注意力加速4.4倍；相比A100上的LLaMA 3，端到端延迟降低3.9倍，能效提升3.2倍。

Conclusion: 首次实现结合线性注意力与三元量化的FPGA方案，验证了算法-架构协同设计对下一代LLM加速的可行性。

Abstract: Transformer blocks are prevalent in large language model (LLM) but present deployment challenges due to their challenging computational and memory demands. While prior work has typically optimized attention mechanisms or feed-forward networks (FFNs) separately, few hardware (HW) architecture have jointly addressed both components with co-designed hardware acceleration. We present ELiTeFormer (Efficient Linear Ternary Transformer), the first Transformer model architecture that unifies hybrid linear attention with ultra-low-precision (ternary) linear projections, specifically co-designed for field-programmable gate array (FPGA) deployment. ELiTeFormer achieves 10x model weight compression and 12.8x key-value (KV) cache compression compared to LLaMA 3, while maintaining competitive accuracy (31.9% on the MMLU benchmark, within 3.0% of BitNet b1.58). Our key architectural contribution is a novel processing element (PE) micro-architecture that eliminates all multiplications in ternary linear projections through bitmasking operations, significantly reducing resource utilization by completely avoiding dedicated digital signal processing (DSP) blocks. We simulate, synthesize, and deploy ELiTeFormer targeting a Xilinx VCK5000 Versal board using high-level synthesis (HLS) flows. Block-level simulations show 9.6x speedup for FFN operations and 4.4x speedup for attention compared to standard implementations. End-to-end deployment achieves up to 3.9x lower latency and 3.2x better energy efficiency than LLaMA 3 on an NVIDIA A100 graphics processing unit (GPU) at long context lengths. This represents the first FPGA realization combining linear attention with ternary quantization, demonstrating the viability of algorithm-architecture co-design for next-generation LLM acceleration.

</details>


### [115] [SABLE: An NDA-Safe Closed-Loop LLM Framework for Analog Circuit Optimization in Industrial EDA Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03701)
*Xunqi Li,Chris H. Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出SABLE框架，一种NDA安全的闭环系统，允许LLMs通过Cadence工具优化模拟电路，同时通过威胁模型、白名单入口点、PDK/路径/模型脱敏、结构化设置与回写、JSON动作合约及六种停止条件确保知识产权安全。在LC-VCO和两阶段运放任务上测试了11个LLM检查点，分别有7/11和4/11通过验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs可以提出电路优化决策，但工业模拟流程无法将晶圆厂PDK内容、专有原理图、绝对仿真路径或受许可证约束的工具状态暴露给云端点，需要在保护知识产权的前提下利用LLM能力进行模拟电路优化。

Method: 提出了SABLE框架，包含：明确的威胁模型（好奇但被动的云提供商）、28个限定SKILL入口点白名单、每条返回路径上的PDK/路径/模型脱敏、结构化的Maestro设置与回写、严格的JSON动作合约（含六种机器检查的停止条件）、以及最佳状态保存机制。

Result: 在LC-VCO任务上11个模型中7个通过；在更难的运放任务上（每个指标需在最差角落保持、相位裕度门控拒绝不稳定高增益点），11个模型中4个在15次迭代预算内通过。反馈路径消融实验表明移除单个脱敏通道会削弱规格或降低搜索效果。

Conclusion: 不同LLM模型在需要工具纪律、偏置推理和规格修复的任务上表现差异显著，但NDA安全边界仍能提供足够脱敏的反馈来实现成功的模拟电路优化。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can propose circuit-optimization decisions, but industrial analog flows cannot expose foundry PDK content, proprietary schematics, absolute simulation paths, or license-bound tool state to a cloud endpoint. We present SABLE (Safe Analog Boundary for LLM-driven EDA), an NDA-safe closed-loop framework that lets LLMs optimize analog circuits through Cadence Virtuoso, Maestro, and Spectre while returning only scrubbed topology intent, numeric metrics, operating-point summaries, and scoped writeback status. "NDA-safe" denotes enforcement under a stated curious-but-passive cloud-provider threat model, not a formal non-interference proof. The framework combines an explicit threat model, a whitelist of 28 scoped SKILL entry points, PDK/path/model scrubbing on every return path, structured Maestro setup and writeback, a strict JSON action contract with six machine-checked stop conditions, and best-so-far state preservation. We evaluate eleven LLM checkpoints from the same documented reset state on two real closed-loop tasks, both run as process-voltage-temperature (PVT) sign-offs across three corners: a 20 GHz LC-VCO tuning-curve task and a two-stage op-amp task. On the LC-VCO task 7 of 11 models pass; on the harder op-amp task, where every metric must hold at the worst corner and a phase-margin gate rejects unstable high-gain points, 4 of 11 pass within a 15-iteration budget. Feedback-path ablations show that removing individual sanitized channels either silently weakens the specification or degrades the search. Model quality differs sharply once the loop requires tool discipline, bias reasoning, and specification repair, yet an NDA-safe boundary still provides enough sanitized feedback for successful analog circuit optimization.

</details>


### [116] [Weave: Verified Netlist-to-Schematic Conversion via Layered Graph Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03835)
*Senol Gulgonul*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Weave是一个确定性SPICE网表到LTspice原理图的转换器，采用分层图布局算法，通过往返连通性检查为每个输出提供二进制正确性认证。在公共测试集上实现100%编译和100%连通性等价，远超现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于学习的网表转原理图方法缺乏连通性保证，且在大规模电路上精度急剧下降。亟待一种确定性、可验证的转换方法，以弥合EDA工具链中仿真器产生网表与设计者需求原理图之间的鸿沟。

Method: 采用Sugiyama风格分层图布局算法实现确定性转换；内置5093个LTspice符号的引脚表；通过往返连通性检查（重新解析生成原理图为网表，逐网表比较）提供二进制正确性认证，而非相似度分数；以单文件无依赖形式运行。

Result: 在公共Circuits-LTSpice测试集（117个电路）：Weave实现100%编译和100%往返验证连通等价，而SOTA模型Schematos仅76%编译、图编辑距离相似度0.35。在3460个AD公司LTspice演示电路集上：88.4%实现精确连通性验证，失败集中在密集多引脚电源模块类。

Conclusion: Weave通过确定性方法彻底解决了网表转原理图中的连通性保证问题，在现有基准上大幅超越学习方法，并提供可审计的二进制正确性认证，为EDA工具链中的逆向工程和文档化提供了可靠方案。

Abstract: Converting a SPICE netlist into a human-readable schematic is a longstanding problem in electronic design automation: simulators and machine-learning pipelines readily produce netlists, but designers reason about circuits through diagrams. Recent learning-based approaches translate netlists into schematics probabilistically, yet they provide no guarantee that the generated drawing preserves the original connectivity, and their accuracy degrades sharply as circuits grow. We present Weave, a deterministic converter that turns a SPICE netlist into an LTspice .asc schematic using a layered (Sugiyama-style) graph layout, and that certifies every output by a round-trip connectivity check: the generated schematic is re-parsed into a netlist and compared, net for net, against the input. A result is reported as correct only when the two partitions are identical, giving a binary correctness certificate rather than a similarity score. Weave runs entirely client-side as a single dependency-free file and embeds a pin table for 5093 LTspice symbols. On the identical public Circuits-LTSpice test set used by the state-of-the-art LLM converter Schemato (117 circuits, netlisted with LTspice itself), Weave achieves 100% compilation and 100% round-trip-verified connectivity equivalence, compared with Schemato's reported 76% compilation and a graph-edit-distance similarity of 0.35; notably, 73% of that set exceeds the five-component threshold beyond which Schemato reports losing connectivity accuracy. On a larger and harder corpus, the 3460 netlistable circuits of the official Analog Devices LTspice demo collection, Weave verifies exact connectivity for 88.4% of circuits, with the remaining failures concentrated in a single, well-characterized class of dense multi-pin power modules.

</details>


### [117] [TileLens: Efficiently Using Large-Granularity Memory Systems with Transparent Two-Dimensional Memory Layout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04031)
*Jae Hyung Ju,Euijun Chung,Hritvik Taneja,Anish Saxena,Shinnung Jeong,Hyesoon Kim,Moinuddin K. Qureshi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 针对大粒度存储系统（LGMS）在LLM推理中因读取放大导致性能严重下降的问题，提出TileLens方案，通过Tile-Major布局对齐计算瓦片与内存粒度，结合软件扩展（TileLens-SW）和硬件扩展（TileLens-HW），在HBF增强GPU上将性能恢复至接近HBM水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LGMS（如HBF、RoMe）虽提供更高的容量或带宽，但其最小访问粒度达KB级别，导致LLM推理核心操作——分块矩阵乘法出现严重的读取放大问题（性能下降可达一个数量级），根源在于计算瓦片的二维特性与内存一维布局之间的根本性不匹配。

Method: 提出Tile-Major布局，将连续内存块重塑为二维矩形以对齐瓦片边界。同时设计TileLens：TileLens-SW扩展GPU DSL，允许仅通过修改布局描述符采用Tile-Major；TileLens-HW扩展张量内存加速器（TMA），为TMA内核提供透明支持。

Result: 在周期级模拟器上使用Qwen-3 30B和Llama-3.1 70B的矩阵乘法内核评估，TileLens结合自适应硬件预取器在HBF增强GPU上（5us NAND读取延迟）将几何平均性能损失从1.61-6.49倍降低至HBM基线1%以内。

Conclusion: Tile-Major布局能有效消除LGMS中的读取放大问题，TileLens以最小改动实现接近HBM的推理性能，为下一代高容量/高带宽内存系统在LLM推理中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) inference is bottlenecked by the capacity and bandwidth of GPU High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM). Recent proposals, such as High-Bandwidth Flash (HBF) and RoMe, offer higher capacity or bandwidth than HBM, but require a minimum access granularity of kilobytes. We show that these Large-Granularity Memory Systems (LGMS) can degrade the performance of tiled matrix-multiplication, which is the dominant operation in LLM inference, by up to an order of magnitude. The root cause of the slowdown is read amplification, where memory requests fetch far more data than the tile actually needs. This waste stems from a fundamental mismatch between the two-dimensional nature of compute tiles and the one-dimensional memory layout, leading to each request spilling well beyond the tile boundaries.
  To mitigate read amplification, we propose to use tile-major layout for LGMS. Rather than storing data as a one-dimensional strip, tile-major layout reshapes each contiguous memory block into a two-dimensional rectangle, aligning memory granularity with tile boundaries. To ease the adoption of tile-major layout on GPUs, we propose TileLens, lightweight software and hardware extensions that collectively cover major classes of GPU kernels. TileLens-SW extends GPU DSLs so that DSL-based kernels can adopt tile-major in global memory by changing only the layout descriptor. TileLens-HW extends the Tensor Memory Accelerator (TMA) for transparent tile-major support in TMA-based kernels without code changes. We evaluate TileLens on a cycle-level simulator using matrix-multiplication kernels from Qwen-3 30B and Llama-3.1 70B. Combining a tile-major layout with an adaptive hardware prefetcher, TileLens achieves near-HBM performance on HBF-augmented GPUs with a 5us HBF NAND read latency, reducing the geomean slowdown from 1.61-6.49x with conventional layouts to within 1% of an HBM-only baseline.

</details>


### [118] [A Reconfigurable and Representation-Adaptive ISA-Based Architecture for Efficient DNN Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04475)
*Vasilis Sakellariou,Vassilis Paliouras,Ioannis Kouretas,Hani Saleh,Thanos Stouraitis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向机器学习的指令集架构（ISA）和可重构硬件平台，通过结合RNS动态精度、轻量级可编程核与SIMD单元，在保持高能效的同时提升了DNN加速器的可编程性。22nm工艺下实现5.12-10.47 TOPS/W，能效比定点方案高1.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域专用DNN加速器虽效率高但缺乏对不断演化的模型架构的适应性，而基于通用ISA的方案（如RISC-V）可编程性好但效率低。本文旨在弥合效率与可编程性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出了一种面向机器学习的ISA及可重构硬件平台：ISA支持数据移动、动态精度和解耦执行的细粒度控制；架构采用轻量级可编程核与SIMD单元，维持高PE利用率且低控制开销；基于残数系统（RNS）实例化3-8位动态精度。

Result: 22nm工艺下实现5.12-10.47 TOPS/W，典型工作负载下能效比定点方案高1.2倍，同时保持模型精度，优于现有最先进和混合精度加速器。

Conclusion: 该设计有效弥合了现代DNN加速器中效率与可编程性之间的差距。

Abstract: Domain-specific hardware accelerators provide significantly higher performance and energy efficiency for deep neural network (DNN) workloads than general-purpose processors, but often lack adaptability to evolving model architectures. In contrast, general-purpose ISA-based solutions, such as RISC-V-based accelerators, improve programmability at the cost of efficiency. This work addresses this tradeoff by introducing a machine-learning-oriented instruction set architecture (ISA) and a reconfigurable hardware platform that combine high efficiency with flexibility. The proposed ISA enables fine-grained control over data movement, dynamic precision, and decoupled execution across data-fetching, tensor processing, and post-processing domains. The corresponding architecture employs lightweight programmable cores and SIMD units to maintain high processing-element utilization with low control overhead, while remaining independent of the underlying numerical representation. We demonstrate the approach using a Residue Number System (RNS) instantiation supporting 3-8-bit dynamic precision. A 22-nm implementation achieves 5.12-10.47 TOPS/W for a typical workload and up to 1.2x higher energy efficiency than its fixed-point counterpart, while preserving model accuracy. It also outperforms state-of-the-art and mixed-precision accelerators. These results show that the proposed design effectively bridges the gap between efficiency and programmability in modern DNN accelerators.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Optimus: A Generic Operator-Level PyTorch Model Transformation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02945)
*Menglu Yu,Jiaqi Xu,Yuzhen Huang,Yanbo Liang,Jia Liu,Shuai Yang,Jason Ansel,Elias Ellison,Edward Yang,Brian Hirsh,Jia Chen Ren,Will Feng,Oguz Ulgen,Xu Zhao,Daohang Shi,Huaqing Xiong,Quanyu Zhu,Mingming Ding,Junqing Zhou,Ruilin Chen,Yuhang Yang,Chi-Keung Luk*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: Optimus是一个基于PyTorch 2.x编译器构建的通用模型变换框架，通过高效的贪婪搜索算法进行模式匹配与替换，在工业级推荐模型上实现了最高63%的加速、6%的峰值内存降低和超过400秒的编译时间减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模工业推荐系统中，深度学习模型架构复杂多样，且由大型团队持续开发迭代，人工优化不可行。现有的基于PyTorch FX的图优化技术依赖人工设计的模块级重写规则，无法扩展到多样化的模型架构。

Method: 提出Optimus框架，基于PyTorch 2.x机器学习编译器构建。使用一组简洁的预定义模式，通过高效的贪婪搜索算法进行模式匹配与替换，同时保持模型语义不变。框架设计为高度可定制和可扩展，集成在PT2栈中。

Result: 在工业级推荐模型上，与基线相比实现了最高63%的加速、6%的峰值内存降低以及超过400秒的编译时间减少。

Conclusion: Optimus作为一种通用且可扩展的模型变换框架，有效解决了大规模工业推荐模型中手工优化不可行、传统规则式变换不可扩展的问题，并已随PyTorch 2.x开源。

Abstract: In large-scale industrial applications, deep learning models that power recommendation and ranking have complex and diverse model architectures. These models are continuously developed and refined by large teams of machine learning engineers, rendering manual optimization infeasible. Consequently, graph-based optimization techniques have become an industry standard for boosting performance, with PyTorch FX transformations leading the charge. These transformations typically rely on a set of human-engineered module-level rewrite rules which are not scalable to diverse model architectures. To address this limitation, we introduce Optimus, a general-purpose model transformation framework built in the PyTorch 2.x (PT2) machine learning compiler. With a concise set of predefined patterns, Optimus applies an efficient greedy search algorithm for pattern matching and replacement, while preserving model semantic. It is designed and implemented as a highly customizable and extensible framework integrated into the PT2 stack. Our evaluation shows that the framework can achieve up to 63% speedup, 6% peak memory reduction, and over 400 second compile time decrease for our industry-scale recommendation models compared to baselines. Optimus is open-sourced together with PyTorch 2.x as a customizable model transformation layer.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Auditing the Audit: Five Failure Modes in Benchmark-Validity Audits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02586)
*Yanhang Li,Zhichao Fan,Zexin Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出基于扰动的构念效度审计（如安全基准测试）的结论容易被实现细节（即管道故障）所操纵，并在自审计实验中展示了五类管道故障（F1-F5），在六点尽职审查门控下所有测试结果均未达到确认标准。作者将其定位为一种补充性的证据扣留与披露协议，而非替代经典构念效度证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑现有AI治理框架所依赖的扰动式构念效度审计本身具有脆弱性，其结论可能因读者不可见的实现细节而被静默操纵，从而无法提供可靠的保障证据。

Method: 通过自审计实验，在安全基准测试和开放权重指令微调模型上，识别并展示了五类管道故障（F1-F5），并采用统一的六点尽职审查门控对所有实验结果进行验证。

Result: 在六点尽职审查门控下，所有实验单元均落入非确认性桶中，无一达到确认性标准。五类管道故障均被成功演示。

Conclusion: 该门控应作为保证级证据的扣留与披露协议，补充（而非替代）经典构念效度证据，且不应用于得出基准效度裁决。

Abstract: Governance frameworks ask AI providers and auditors for documented evaluation evidence, and perturbation-based construct-validity audits are a common form of that evidence. We argue the audits are themselves fragile: their conclusions can be silently manufactured by implementation details that readers cannot see in the reported numbers. We name five classes of pipeline failure and demonstrate each in a self-audit over safety benchmarks and open-weight instruction-tuned models. Under a unified six-point due-diligence gate, every cell lands in a non-confirmatory bucket, and no cell reaches confirmatory. The evidence here is a single two-model, five-benchmark case study, and F1--F5 is an illustrative, deliberately non-exhaustive starting taxonomy -- not a comprehensive partition of audit failures. We position the gate as a withholding and disclosure protocol for assurance-grade evidence, supplementary to (not a replacement for) classical construct-validity evidence, and not as a route to benchmark-validity verdicts.

</details>


### [121] [Evaluating Time Series Foundation Models for Electricity Price Forecasting: Contamination Risk, Distributional Shifts, and Covariate Dependence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02623)
*Zhenghua Pan,Ahmed Aziz Ezzat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在电价预测（EPF）这一具有挑战性的非平稳、协变量驱动场景中的零样本泛化能力，提出了双数据集基准框架，发现TSFMs表现强劲但依赖协变量支持，且与领域专用方法互补，简单集成效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间序列基础模型在零样本预测中表现优异，但在协变量驱动的非平稳场景（如电价预测）中的泛化能力尚未被充分探索。电价预测因其复杂的时间依赖、分布偏移及对结构/上下文信息的强依赖，是一个理想的测试平台。

Method: 提出双数据集基准框架用于电价预测，以降低数据污染风险并实现TSFMs的公平评估；从点预测、概率预测、尾部行为、价格尖峰等多个维度进行评估，并与领域专用方法对比。

Result: TSFMs在电价预测中表现强劲，通常优于通用基线，但性能高度依赖协变量支持，且未能持续超越领域专用方法；TSFMs与领域专用方法的简单集成具有显著潜力。

Conclusion: TSFMs与领域专用方法捕获互补的预测信息，二者的集成是提升电价预测性能的有效方向，启示未来研究应关注跨范式融合。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) have shown strong zero-shot forecasting performance, but their generalization in covariate-driven, non-stationary settings is underexplored. Electricity price forecasting (EPF) presents a challenging testbed due to complex temporal dependencies, distributional shifts, and strong reliance on structural and contextual information. We propose a two-dataset-benchmarking framework for EPF to mitigate contamination risk and enable fair evaluation of TSFMs. We examine key aspects of EPF including point and probabilistic forecasting performance, tail behavior, price spikes, and comparisons against domain-specific methods. We find that TSFMs are highly competitive and often outperform general-purpose baselines. Yet, their performance depends critically on covariate support, and they do not consistently surpass domain-specific methods tailored to EPF. Interestingly, simple ensembles of TSFMs and domain-specific methods appear to have significant potential, suggesting that the two approaches capture complementary predictive information.

</details>


### [122] [HiFA4: Training-Free 4-bit FlashAttention on Ascend HIF4 NPUs for LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04302)
*Hui Dong,Yanzhao Li,Jie Gao,Chunlu Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Yupeng Sun,Zhenyuan Chen,Zhiqiang Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HiFA4提出了一种针对Ascend NPU的4-bit FlashAttention后训练算子级设计，通过Smooth-QK和P-Reordering两种机制，在保持FP16在线softmax状态的同时，实现了QK^T和PV的4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMM运算，有效降低了量化带来的精度损失和决策漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM推理中，FlashAttention的QK^T和PV计算是主要瓶颈，现有的低精度量化方案（如HIF4）会引入显著的精度损失和决策漂移，尤其是在Ascend NPU上缺乏针对性的4-bit FlashAttention设计。

Method: 提出两种核心机制：(1) Smooth-QK：在RoPE后对Q和K进行校准静态的每通道等价重缩放，将量化困难从K转移到Q，无需推理时的每tile在线调整；(2) P-Reordering：使用与PV GEMM中相同的量化注意力权重P_hat来累积softmax归一化器，而非高精度重建版本，该不一致性会引入连贯的输出缩放误差，同时允许归一化器融合到PV Cube GEMM中。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上恢复37.5%的精度差距，样本加权精度损失从1.12 pp降至0.70 pp，BF16不一致MMLU预测从16.3%降至8.2%，MMLU准确率回归减少57%（1071→465）。Gemma2-9B上保持在BF16 0.7 pp内，回归减少27%。禁用Smooth-QK的LLaMA3.1-8B、Mistral-7B和Phi-4B上，P-Reordering仍使MMLU回归减少41-52%。指令调度分析预计相较于BF16减少35.4%的关键路径延迟。

Conclusion: HiFA4是首个针对Ascend NPU的HIF4 FlashAttention设计，通过Smooth-QK和P-Reordering有效缓解了4-bit量化精度损失，并在多个LLM上验证了其有效性，同时提出了一种可融合softmax归一化器到PV GEMM的指令调度方案以降低延迟。

Abstract: We present HiFA4, a post-training operator-level design that executes both QK^T and PV in FlashAttention as 4-bit HIF4 Cube GEMMs for LLM inference on Ascend NPUs, while maintaining the online softmax state in FP16. To our knowledge, HiFA4 is the first Ascend-HIF4-targeted design of this kind evaluated on standard NLP benchmarks.
  HiFA4 combines two mechanisms. Smooth-QK applies a calibration-static per-channel equivalent rescaling to Q and K after RoPE, transferring quantization difficulty from K to Q without per-tile online reduction at inference. P-Reordering accumulates the softmax normalizer from the same quantized attention weights P_hat used in the PV GEMM, rather than from a higher-precision reconstruction. We show that this inconsistent formulation introduces a coherent output-scaling error, and validate the effect on a Qwen3-8B Layer-0 MMLU trace, where all 3.6M measured attention tiles exhibit net probability-mass loss with median epsilon_bar = -0.064. P-Reordering also allows the normalizer to be fused into the PV Cube GEMM.
  Across five LLMs, HiFA4 reduces quantization-induced decision drift. On Qwen3-8B, it recovers 37.5% of the accuracy gap introduced by direct HIF4 quantization, narrows the sample-weighted accuracy loss from 1.12 pp to 0.70 pp, reduces BF16-inconsistent MMLU predictions from 16.3% to 8.2%, and cuts MMLU accuracy regressions by 57% (1071 to 465). On Gemma2-9B, mild smoothing keeps HiFA4 within 0.7 pp of BF16 while reducing MMLU regressions by 27%. On LLaMA3.1-8B, Mistral-7B, and Phi-4B, where Smooth-QK is disabled, P-Reordering with the adopted Q-Mean auxiliary still reduces full-set MMLU regressions by 41-52%. A preliminary instruction-scheduling analysis projects a 35.4% critical-path latency reduction relative to BF16 by fusing the softmax normalizer into the PV Cube GEMM; on-hardware validation is left to future work.

</details>


### [123] [QuantFlow: A Federated Mamba-Based Post-Transformer Foundation Model for Time-Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02632)
*Shah Nawaz Haider,Steve Austin,Arnab Barua,Sarowar Morshed Shawon,Hadaate Ullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QuantFlow是一个结合倒置序列嵌入、双向Mamba状态空间解码器、分位数回归和联邦学习的概率预测框架，在加密货币、交通、电力等多个领域的时间序列预测任务中取得良好性能，并在非独立同分布联邦场景下保持隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列基础模型依赖集中式数据和Transformer注意力机制，限制了其在长序列、高维度和隐私敏感信号上的应用，亟需一种既能保持跨任务迁移能力又能兼顾隐私保护和计算效率的预测框架。

Method: QuantFlow采用倒置序列嵌入（每个变量在完整观测窗口上嵌入）、双向Mamba状态空间解码器（前向和反向处理）、分位数回归（预测五个条件分位数）以及联邦学习框架。同时提出TSMixup数据增强方法，通过Dirichlet加权插值扩展时间多样性并保持序列结构。

Result: 在ETTm1上取得0.2834的MSE，在Weather上取得0.2218的MSE；20客户端非独立同分布联邦部署在三轮通信后仍保持可用精度，无需集中原始数据。

Conclusion: 选择性状态空间建模是可扩展、不确定性感知且隐私保护的时间序列预测的有前景的基础方案，但在不规则流行病信号和长周期泛化方面仍存在局限性。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting supports decisions in finance, en-ergy, transportation, public health, and industrial monitoring. Recent foundation models improve transfer across forecast-ing tasks, but many depend on centralized data and Trans-former attention, which restricts their use for long, high-di-mensional, and privacy-sensitive signals. This paper presents QuantFlow, a probabilistic forecasting framework that com-bines inverted sequence embedding, bidirectional Mamba state-space decoders, quantile regression, and federated learning. Each variable is embedded over the complete ob-servation window, processed in forward and reverse direc-tions, and projected to five conditional quantiles. TSMixup expands temporal diversity through Dirichlet-weighted inter-polation while preserving sequence structure. Experiments cover cryptocurrency, traffic, electricity, Electricity Trans-former Temperature, influenza, and weather data. QuantFlow obtains mean squared errors of 0.2834 on ETTm1 and 0.2218 on Weather, and a 20-client non-IID deployment retains use-ful accuracy after three communication rounds without cen-tralizing raw records. The results indicate that selective state-space modelling is a promising basis for scalable, uncer-tainty-aware, and privacy-conscious time-series prediction, while also revealing limitations on irregular epidemiological signals and long-horizon generalization.

</details>


### [124] [GRAFT: Grafted Reference Audio for Fine-grained Pronunciation in Zero-shot Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02633)
*Antonis Asonitis,Francesco Verdini,Aref Farhadipour,Vijeta Avijeet,Pierre-Edouard Honnet,Marzieh Razavi,Juan Pablo Zuluaga Gomez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRAFT是一种针对文本到语音神经编解码语言建模的逐词发音控制机制，通过从短语音样本中提取发音提示并将其绑定到文本对应位置，显著提高了罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语的发音准确性，同时保持说话人相似性和自然度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TTS系统虽然达到了高可懂度和自然度，但继承了文本的歧义性，在罕见专有名词、外来词和技术术语上容易发音错误。即使是音素条件模型也无法为逐词发音提供直接的声学控制手段。

Method: GRAFT通过模型的语音分词器编码目标词的短语音样本，将其作为发音提示绑定到提示文本中对应位置。在训练数据构建过程中使用语音转换技术，将提示说话人与目标说话人解耦，使提示可以来自任意语音而输出保持目标说话人声音。

Result: 在盲听英语听力测试中，GRAFT被人类评分者明显评为第一，其困难词汇的呈现最接近该词汇的参考录音。在五语言客观基准上，GRAFT将目标词音素错误率较纯文本基线降低了22-39%，在目标词发音上优于竞争性的开源零样本系统，同时保持了说话人相似性和自然度。

Conclusion: GRAFT通过逐词发音控制机制有效解决了TTS系统中罕见词汇的发音错误问题，在不牺牲说话人相似性和自然度的前提下，显著提升了目标词的发音准确性。

Abstract: We present GRAFT, a per-word pronunciation conditioning mechanism for text-to-speech neural codec language modeling. Existing systems reach high intelligibility and naturalness but inherit the ambiguity of text and mispronounce rare proper nouns, loanwords and technical terms. Even phoneme-conditioned models offer no direct acoustic handle for per-word pronunciation. GRAFT controls the pronunciation of a chosen word from a short spoken sample of it, encoded with the model's own speech tokenizer and bound to the word's position in the prompt. Voice conversion during training-data construction disentangles the hint speaker from the target speaker, so the hint may come from any voice while the output stays in the target voice. In a blind English listening study, human raters rank GRAFT first by a clear margin, judging its rendering of the difficult word closest to a reference recording of that word. On a five-language objective benchmark, GRAFT reduces target-word phoneme error rate by 22-39% over the identical text-only backbone and outperforms competitive open-source zero-shot systems, both phoneme- and text-conditioned, on target-word pronunciation, while preserving speaker similarity and naturalness.

</details>


### [125] [Federated Learning for Object Detection: Enabling Collaborative Drone Learning Without Centralizing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02636)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出在分布式无人机群中应用联邦学习（FL）进行目标检测，使用Sherpa.ai FL平台和KIIT-MiTA数据集，在不集中图像数据的情况下训练共享模型。实验表明，FL方法在mAP指标上接近集中式训练，远超单无人机训练，轻量级YOLO26 nano模型在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别获得52.89%和67.80%的相对提升，实现了可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键的无人机与边缘视觉系统（如灾难响应、安防、基础设施监控等）依赖大规模持续更新的数据集，但集中式训练需要传输航拍图像，面临隐私、监管、存储和带宽等挑战，尤其是在分布式无人机部署场景中，机载数据不便或不宜传输到中心化基础设施。

Method: 在Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台上，基于KIIT-MiTA数据集实现联邦目标检测流水线，采用YOLO26 nano等轻量级模型，并在IoU阈值0.50和0.50:0.95下以平均精度（mAP）为指标，与单无人机训练和集中式训练基线进行比较。

Result: 联邦学习方法的性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单无人机训练。最佳轻量级模型YOLO26 nano在mAP@0.50和mAP@0.50:0.95上分别实现52.89%和67.80%的相对提升。

Conclusion: 联邦学习能够在无需数据集中化的情况下，实现跨分布式无人机群的可扩展、高性能且隐私保护的目标检测。

Abstract: Object detection is a fundamental capability for AI-driven perception in safety-critical drone and edge-vision systems, including disaster response, operational security environments, infrastructure monitoring and defense applications. Robust model performance in such environments depends on large, continuously updated datasets. However, training high-performing detectors typically requires centralizing aerial imagery, which raises privacy, regulatory, storage, and bandwidth challenges. This is especially problematic in distributed drone deployments, where visual data is generated onboard and is often impractical or undesirable to transfer to a centralized infrastructure.
  In this work, we apply Federated Learning (FL) for object detection, enabling drones to improve a shared model while keeping image data local and private. We implement a federated object detection pipeline using the Sherpa.ai FL platform on the KIIT-MiTA dataset, and compare it with Single-drone and Centralized baselines using mean Average Precision (mAP) at IoU thresholds of 0.50 and 0.50-0.95. In our experiments, the proposed FL approach remains close to Centralized training while dramatically improving over Single-drone training, with the best lightweight model (YOLO26 nano), suitable for deployment even on very limited edge infrastructure, achieving relative gains of 52.89% and 67.80% in mAP@0.50 and mAP@0.50:0.95, respectively. These results show that FL enables scalable, high-performing, and privacy-preserving object detection across distributed drone fleets without data centralization.

</details>


### [126] [Post-Generation Curation of Synthetic Images via Homogeneous-Heterogeneous Splitting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02637)
*Disheng Liu,Tuo Liang,Chaoda Song,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成器无关的合成图像选择方法，通过将真实数据分为同质和异质子集，并基于保真度-多样性准则对合成图像评分，仅通过子集选择即可提升下游任务性能，在多个基准上超越现有方法，最多可减少40%的合成样本即可匹配真实数据性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有利用生成模型合成数据的方法要么需要训练/微调生成器，要么依赖提示工程等后处理，这些方法对生成器特定且需要专业知识。作者希望研究一个互补性问题：给定固定的合成图像池，能否仅通过选择信息性子集来提升下游效用？

Method: 1) 将每个真实类别划分为规范同质子集和非冗余异质子集；2) 基于保真度-多样性准则对合成图像评分，该准则奖励语义对齐同时惩罚规范冗余；3) 选择高分合成子集用于下游任务。该方法与生成器无关，无需重新训练。

Result: 在多个基准上，该方法一致优于现有数据选择基线，最多可用40%更少的合成样本匹配真实数据性能。在更强的任务调优生成器上依然有效，在分类和分割任务上均有增益。

Conclusion: 后生成选择不是更好生成器的替代品，而是提升合成数据效用的互补机制。现代生成器存在过度生成规范模态、低估类内变化的系统性偏差，有效选择必须对抗这一偏差。

Abstract: Recent generative models can produce high-quality synthetic images, offering scalable training training data for data-hungry models. Existing approaches to exploiting this potential typically involve 1) training or fine-tuning generators, or 2) using lightweight post-hoc adaptation like prompt engineering or inference-time guidance, making them generator-specific and expertise-intensive. We study a complementary question: given a fixed pool of generated images, can downstream utility be improved purely by selecting an informative subset? The answer is yes. We show that effective selection must counter a structural bias of modern generators: they tend to over-produce canonical modes of each class while underrepresenting intra-class variation. Building on this insight, we split each real class into a canonical Homogeneous (HO) subset and a non-redundant Heterogeneous (HE) subset, then score synthetic images by a fidelity-diversity criterion that rewards semantic alignment while penalizing canonical redundancy. The method is generator-agnostic and requires no retraining. Across multiple benchmarks, it consistently outperforms state-of-the-art data selection baselines and matches the real-data performance with up to 40% fewer synthetic samples. The same criterion remains effective when applied on top of stronger task-tuned generators, with gains on both classification and segmentation tasks. Post-generation selection is therefore not a substitute for better generators, but a complementary mechanism for improving the utility of synthetic data.

</details>


### [127] [Adaptive Inference Batching using Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05272)
*Ruslan Sharifullin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习在推理服务系统中的自适应批处理和路由策略。结果表明：在单GPU场景下，RL相比人工调优的静态策略提升有限（+0.1%~1.0%）；但在多GPU异构路由场景下，RL发现的工作负载隔离策略消除了队头阻塞，相较轮询提升348%，相较最短队列提升48%，吞吐量提高60%，延迟降低25%，且具备良好的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理服务系统在突发性、异构性工作负载下需要平衡吞吐量和延迟，但业界标准的静态批处理策略需要手动调优且无法适应动态流量变化，因此探索强化学习是否能学习出优于启发式方法的自适应批处理和路由策略。

Method: 将问题建模为关于队列状态、请求类型和GPU可用性的马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），在经排队论和生产轨迹验证的离散事件模拟器上训练REINFORCE和PPO智能体，采用注意力增强策略网络，并在标准泊松流量、极端突发、真实世界轨迹和异构多GPU路由等多种场景下评估。

Result: 单GPU场景下RL提升有限（+0.1%~1.0%）；多GPU异构路由场景下，RL发现的工作负载隔离策略相较轮询提升348%、相较最短队列提升48%，吞吐量提高60%、延迟降低25%且满足SLA约束，注意力网络相比MLP收敛快约20%，策略能泛化到未见过的突发和真实流量。

Conclusion: 强化学习的优势集中在组合性的多资源决策问题（如多GPU异构路由）而非单资源的时间调度问题（如单GPU批处理），这为生产推理基础设施中何时值得采用学习策略提供了实用性的判定依据。

Abstract: Inference serving systems must balance throughput and latency under bursty, heterogeneous workloads, yet the industry standard remains static batching policies that require manual tuning and cannot adapt to shifting traffic. We investigate whether reinforcement learning (RL) can learn adaptive batching and routing policies that outperform these heuristics, training REINFORCE and PPO agents on a discrete-event simulator validated against queuing theory and production traces (Azure Functions, BurstGPT). We formulate the problem as an MDP over queue state, request type and GPU availability, evaluating across standard Poisson traffic, extreme bursts, real-world traces and heterogeneous multi-GPU routing.
  Our central finding is a clear boundary condition for RL's value in systems problems. In single-GPU settings, a well-tuned static batching policy is already near-optimal under Poisson-like arrivals and RL offers only marginal gains (+0.1% to +1.0%). In multi-GPU heterogeneous routing, however, where fast and slow requests compete for shared resources, the agent discovers a workload-segregation policy that eliminates Head-of-Line blocking, yielding a 3.5x (348%) improvement over Round-Robin and a 48% improvement over the strongest heuristic baseline (Shortest-Queue), with 60% higher throughput and 25% lower latency while respecting SLA constraints. The policy generalizes to unseen bursty and real-world traffic despite training only on synthetic Poisson arrivals and an attention-augmented policy network converges roughly 20% faster than an MLP baseline.
  These results suggest RL's advantage over engineered heuristics concentrates in combinatorial, multi-resource decisions rather than single-resource temporal scheduling, a practical distinction for deciding where learned policies justify their engineering cost in production inference infrastructure.

</details>


### [128] [A Granularity-Aware EEG Feature Framework for Psychopathology Dimension Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02670)
*Haofan Cheng,Jingjing Hu,Jingrong Pei,Shuaiqi Fu,Meilun Shen,Shuai Fang,Meng Wang,Dan Guo,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种粒度感知的EEG特征管线，将多尺度描述符组织为全局、区域和通道三个层级，在HBN队列中评估对四种精神病理学维度的预测能力。基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择在特定条件下优于传统方法，但效应量适中。选定的特征呈现维度特异性的时空模式，与现有神经生理学知识一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG提供了一种非侵入性方法研究维度精神病理学的神经生理相关性，但跨EEG范式和特征粒度的系统证据仍然有限，现有研究缺乏对多尺度特征的系统组织与评估。

Method: 开发粒度感知的EEG特征管线，将特征组织为全局、区域和通道三个层级；使用Healthy Brain Network (HBN)队列，评估四种精神病理学维度（p因子、内化、外化和注意力问题）在四种EEG范式下的预测；采用基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择策略。

Result: 基于树的模型和粒度平衡特征选择在特定条件下优于传统方法，但效应量适中。选定的特征呈现出维度特异性的空间和频谱模式，与现有神经生理学知识基本一致。在独立PEARL队列中的探索性交叉数据集验证表明该选择原则在协议转换下仍具技术可行性。

Conclusion: 多尺度EEG特征包含与维度精神病理学相关的微弱但可检测的信号，粒度感知的特征选择可作为未来基于EEG的表型研究中一种有用的特征降维策略。该设置为可行性测试而非临床筛查场景。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) offers a noninvasive approach for examining neurophysiological correlates of dimensional psychopathology, yet systematic evidence across EEG paradigms and feature granularities remains limited. Here, we develop a granularity-aware EEG feature pipeline that organizes multi-scale descriptors into global, regional, and channel levels. Using the Healthy Brain Network (HBN) cohort, we evaluate the prediction of four psychopathology dimensions: p-factor, internalizing, externalizing, and attention problems, across four EEG paradigms. Given the heterogeneity of pediatric psychopathology and the moderate reliability of questionnaire-derived scores, this setting represents a challenging feasibility test rather than a clinical screening scenario. Tree-based models and granularity-balanced feature selection showed promising improvements over conventional approaches in selected conditions, although effect sizes remained modest. Visualization of selected markers revealed dimension-specific spatial and spectral patterns that were broadly aligned with existing neurophysiological knowledge. An exploratory cross-dataset sanity check on the independent PEARL cohort suggested that the proposed selection principle remains technically feasible under protocol shifts, without claiming cross-dataset generalizability. Overall, multi-scale EEG features contain weak but detectable signals related to dimensional psychopathology, and granularity-aware selection may serve as a useful feature-reduction strategy for future EEG-based phenotyping studies.

</details>


### [129] [LiNO: Lifting based multiresolution neural operator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02715)
*Himanshu Pandey,Subham Patel,Ratikanta Behera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于第二代小波提升格式的多分辨率神经算子LiNO，通过可逆的自适应提升变换从数据中直接学习多尺度分解，实现信息无损的多尺度算子学习，在Darcy流、Poisson方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程和Gray-Scott反应扩散系统等基准测试中表现优于现有神经算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子在同时捕捉全局动力学和精细尺度结构方面存在困难，缺乏能够表征多尺度特征的分层多尺度分解框架。

Method: 基于第二代小波提升方案构建多分辨率算子LiNO，通过参数化提升变换从数据中自适应学习多分辨率分解；该提升变换可逆且适应底层解函数；在提升后的多分辨率空间中分别演化粗尺度系数和方向细节系数，实现尺度感知的物理建模。

Result: 在Darcy流、Poisson方程、Allen-Cahn方程、可压缩Navier-Stokes方程和Gray-Scott反应扩散系统等多个涵盖多尺度现象、输运主导动力学和混沌系统的基准测试中，LiNO相比现有最先进神经算子展现出更强的性能。

Conclusion: 自适应多分辨率算子为科学机器学习提供了一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Recently, neural operators have shown promising outcomes for learning solution operators of differential equations directly from data. This framework learns a functional mapping from the parameter field to the solution field, enabling the prediction of an entire class of solutions rather than a specific instance. However, existing operators often struggle to capture both global dynamics and fine-scale structure simultaneously. To design an effective operator capable of representing multiscale features, a hierarchical multiscale decomposition framework is required. In this study, we develop the Lifting Neural Operator (LiNO), a multiresolution operator built on the second-generation wavelet lifting scheme. LiNO learns a multiresolution decomposition directly from data by parameterizing the lifting transform. This lifting transformation is adaptive to the underlying solution function and exactly invertible by construction, enabling information-preserving multiscale operator learning. In the lifted multiresolution space, the operator evolves coarse and directional detail coefficients separately, resulting in scale-aware modeling of the underlying physics. We evaluate LiNO on several benchmarks, including Darcy flow, the Poisson equation, the Allen-Cahn equation, the compressible Navier-Stokes equation, and the Gray-Scott reaction-diffusion system. Together, these benchmarks cover a wide range of physical behaviors, including multiscale phenomena, transport-dominated dynamics, and chaotic systems. LiNO demonstrates strong performance on these challenging benchmarks compared with state-of-the-art neural operators. These results suggest that adaptive multiresolution operators provide a promising direction for scientific machine learning.

</details>


### [130] [Weighted Conformal Prediction for Lab-to-Track Thermal Transfer in EV Motorsport Powertrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02722)
*Varshith Roy Kotla*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究电动汽车动力系统热预测中的实验室到赛道迁移问题，使用共形预测方法（EnbPI）在真实协变量偏移下进行评估，并引入加权EnbPI改进，但提升有限；同时在F1遥测数据上作为无监督OOD诊断应用，指出共形域适应虽有潜力但尚未完全解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能电动汽车动力系统的内部热预测在真实赛道负载下非常困难，因为内部温度在实验室外几乎不可观测，且基于实验室循环标定的模型在真实场景下失效。

Method: 采用EnbPI（留一法自助法集成共形预测）处理自相关时间序列；在此基础上引入密度比加权的加权EnbPI（结合集成残差和Tibshirani等人的密度比估计，通过概率域分类器实现）。在CALCE锂离子循环数据（FUDS分布内，US06@45°C真实偏移）上标定和评估，并在F1遥测数据（Monza/Silverstone）上作为无监督OOD诊断。

Result: 未加权EnbPI在分布内达到95.00%名义覆盖率，在真实协变量偏移下降至70.13%；加权EnbPI恢复至72.42%，提升有限。F1数据上无监督标记率分别为65.6%（Monza）和58.0%（Silverstone），远高于分布内基准5%，但与制动/DRS区域关联不一致。

Conclusion: 共形域适应是解决实验室到赛道热预测迁移问题的有前景但尚未完全解决的工具，论文坦诚指出了其具体不足之处。

Abstract: Predicting thermal volatility in high-performance EV powertrains is difficult as internal temperatures are rarely observable outside the lab, and models calibrated on lab drive cycles fail when deployed against real-world loads. We study this lab-to-track transfer problem using conformal prediction, offering distribution-free uncertainty bounds. We implement Ensemble Batch Prediction Intervals (EnbPI; Xu & Xie, 2021), a leave-one-out bootstrap-ensemble conformal method for autocorrelated time series, and calibrate it on real CALCE lithium-ion cycler data (A123 SP20 cells, FUDS profile). We evaluate it under a genuine, measured covariate shift: a second real CALCE test condition (US06 Highway Driving Schedule at 45°C). The unweighted EnbPI bound, achieving its nominal 95% coverage in-distribution (measured: 95.00%), degrades to 70.13% empirical coverage under this real shift. We introduce a weighted EnbPI procedure combining EnbPI's ensemble residuals with density-ratio weighting (Tibshirani et al., 2019), estimating the density ratio via a probabilistic domain classifier. This recovers coverage to 72.42%, a modest, honestly-reported improvement, not a complete fix. We additionally apply the calibrated model to real 2023 Formula 1 telemetry (Monza and Silverstone, driver VER) as an unsupervised out-of-distribution diagnostic. Because no internal thermal channel exists in public trackside telemetry, we report only unsupervised flag rates (65.6% at Monza, 58.0% at Silverstone, well above the 5% in-distribution base rate) and note inconsistent associations between flags and braking/DRS zones. We conclude that conformal domain adaptation is a promising but only partially solved tool for this problem, detailing exactly where it falls short.

</details>


### [131] [Out-of-Distribution Generalization of Risk Aversion in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02755)
*Kristina Zhang,Junior Chinomso Okoroafor,Benjamin Maltbie,Andrew Lin,Abhitej Bokka,Elliott Thornley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究低风险场景下训练的AI风险厌恶行为能否泛化到天文级别的高风险场景。通过在Qwen3-8B等模型上使用SFT、DPO、激活控制等方法训练低风险厌恶，发现模型的风险厌恶在跨越98个数量级的高风险场景下仍能部分泛化（合作率从基线2%提升至39%-70%），但尚不足以作为可靠的安全保险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AI可能misaligned的情况下，训练AI对资源持有风险厌恶态度可作为一种安全保险机制——misaligned但风险厌恶的AI倾向于选择低风险低回报的合作策略而非高风险高回报的反叛策略，从而限制潜在危害。然而现实只能训练低风险场景下的风险厌恶，安全有效性取决于这种能力能否泛化到天文级别的高风险场景。

Method: 构建RiskAverseOOD基准测试，使用SFT、DPO、Tie Training、激活控制(Activation Steering)等多种方法在低风险场景下训练Qwen3-8B等模型的风险厌恶行为，然后测试其在跨越98个数量级的天文高风险场景下是否仍表现出风险厌恶。此外还训练了奖励模型来评估风险厌恶推理的质量。

Result: 模型学会的风险厌恶至少部分泛化到98个数量级外：合作率从基线2%提升至SFT和Tie Training约70%、DPO约52%、激活控制约39%。微调的奖励模型能可靠地区分风险厌恶推理与风险中性或过度风险厌恶推理（成对准确率99.6%）。效果在不同规模（Qwen3-1.7B、14B）和模型家族（Gemma-3-12B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct）上复现。

Conclusion: 低风险场景下学习的风险厌恶可以部分泛化到天文级别的高风险场景，但一致性尚不足以作为可靠的安全保险机制。实现足够水平的泛化一致性仍是一个未解决的开放问题。

Abstract: Training AIs to be risk-averse in resources could offer a failsafe in the event that AIs turn out misaligned. Misaligned but risk-averse AIs would tend to prefer low-risk, low-reward strategies like cooperation over high-risk, high-reward strategies like rebellion, limiting the downsides of any misalignment. But we can only feasibly train AIs to be risk-averse on low-stakes gambles, and we will only be safe if their risk aversion generalizes to astronomically-high-stakes gambles. Will it? To shed light on this question, we introduce RiskAverseOOD: a benchmark for measuring how well risk aversion generalizes out of distribution. We then offer some initial results. Using a variety of methods to make Qwen3-8B choose risk-aversely when the stakes are low, we find that we can induce substantial risk aversion when the stakes are astronomically high. Our models' learned risk aversion generalizes at least partially across 98 orders of magnitude. From a baseline 2% rate of choosing a safe `Cooperate' option, we see rates around 70% (SFT and tie training), 52% (DPO), and 39% (activation steering). In another experiment, our fine-tuned reward model reliably scores risk-averse reasoning above risk-neutral or excessively risk-averse alternatives (99.6% pairwise accuracy). We replicate these effects at different scales (Qwen3-1.7B and Qwen3-14B) and across model families (Gemma-3-12B-IT and Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct). Overall, we find that risk aversion learned at low stakes can generalize OOD to astronomically high stakes, though not yet consistently enough to serve as a reliable failsafe. Achieving that level of consistency is an open problem.

</details>


### [132] [Safe Inference-Time Alignment via Lagrangian Reward Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02781)
*Yaswanth Chittepu,Ativ Joshi,Sohini Chintala,Scott Niekum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LARA是一种在推理时进行安全约束对齐的通用框架，通过将带约束优化问题对偶化为一维凸问题，计算增广奖励信号，可即插即用地集成到现有推理时对齐方法中，在帮助性与无害性之间取得更好的权衡，其中Best-of-N方法性能接近微调对齐基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法通常只优化单一标量分数，显式安全约束要么被忽略，要么通过手动调参的惩罚项编码，缺乏原则性的约束处理机制。

Method: 从KL正则化的带约束目标（含奖励模型和成本模型）出发，将约束对偶化，转化为关于非负对偶变量的一维凸优化问题；在小型校准集上估计该对偶变量，得到增广奖励，作为即插即用的评分信号集成到现有推理时对齐方法中。

Result: LARA改善了帮助性-无害性权衡，其中Best-of-N在推理时方法中表现最佳，性能接近基于微调的直接对齐基线。

Conclusion: LARA提供了一种原则性的推理时安全约束对齐框架，通过对偶校准的增广奖励信号，在不进行权重更新的情况下实现了有效的约束满足，并且序列级方法可获得精确的约束保证，而词元级方法提供原则性的启发式近似。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment steers a frozen language model during decoding using auxiliary reward signals, avoiding the cost of repeated weight updates. However, existing inference-time alignment methods typically optimize a single scalar score, so explicit safety constraints must either be ignored or encoded through manually tuned penalties. We propose Lagrangian Reward Augmentation (LARA), a general inference-time alignment framework under safety constraints. Starting from a KL-regularized constrained objective with a reward model and a cost model, LARA dualizes the constraint and reduces the optimization problem to a one-dimensional convex problem over a nonnegative dual variable. Estimated on a small calibration set, this dual variable defines an augmented reward that can be used as a drop-in scoring signal within existing inference-time alignment methods. For sequence-level sampling methods, such as Best-of-N reranking, the calibrated dual variable corresponds to the solution of the expected-cost constrained problem. For token-level reward-guided decoding methods, the same construction yields a principled dual-calibrated heuristic rather than an exact constrained-policy guarantee. We evaluate LARA on both sequence-level and token-level inference-time alignment methods, and find that LARA improves the helpfulness-harmlessness tradeoff, with Best-of-N achieving the best performance among inference-time methods, approaching finetuning-based direct alignment baselines.

</details>


### [133] [Induction Heads Interpolate N-Grams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02800)
*Francesco D'Angelo,Oguz Kaan Yuksel,Swathi Shree Narashiman,Nicolas Flammarion*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Transformer中的归纳头（induction heads）在阶-k马尔可夫链上的工作机制，发现其实现了两种互补的平滑机制：软上下文匹配估计和BOS token触发的伪计数平滑，分别对应于Jelinek-Mercer平滑和Dirichlet平滑。训练后的Transformer能够恢复预期的注意力模式，并在多种设置下匹配或超越传统基于计数的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管归纳头被认为是Transformer中上下文学习的核心机制，但对其所实现的统计估计器缺乏精确刻画。本文旨在桥接归纳头的机制可解释性与经典统计平滑理论，揭示Transformer如何通过平滑而非简单计数来实现上下文学习中的正则化。

Method: 在阶-k马尔可夫链上训练Transformer，理论分析归纳头的两种平滑机制：(1) 有限注意力权重尺度下的软上下文匹配估计，加权聚合精确和部分上下文匹配；(2) BOS token引入的加性伪计数，实现Dirichlet风格平滑。构建了解耦的Transformer来验证这两种机制，并通过与经典计数基线比较进行评估。

Result: 训练后的Transformer恢复了预测的注意力模式；在伪计数平滑最优或低阶上下文提供结构化证据的场景中，Transformer匹配或超越了传统计数基线方法。

Conclusion: Transformer学习的是对上下文估计进行正则化（平滑），而非简单计数；归纳头机制与Jelinek-Mercer平滑和Dirichlet平滑之间存在精确对应关系，为理解上下文学习提供了统计理论基础。

Abstract: Induction heads are attention circuits believed to underlie in-context learning in transformers, yet a precise characterization of the estimators they implement remains elusive. We study transformers trained on order-$k$ Markov chains and identify two complementary smoothing mechanisms. First, at finite attention-weight scale, the circuit implements a soft context-matching estimator: it aggregates contributions from exact and partial context matches, weighted exponentially by their overlap, and induces a data-dependent interpolation across context orders analogous to Jelinek-Mercer smoothing. Second, a beginning-of-sequence (BOS) token induces additive pseudo-counts, recovering Dirichlet-style smoothing. We construct a disentangled transformer implementing both mechanisms and show that trained transformers recover the predicted attention patterns. Across settings where pseudo-count smoothing is optimal or lower-order contexts provide structured evidence, trained transformers match or outperform classical count-based baselines. Our results bridge mechanistic interpretability of induction heads with classical statistical smoothing, revealing that transformers learn to regularize in-context estimation rather than simply count.

</details>


### [134] [SMART: A Machine Learning and Monte Carlo Framework for Rapid Analysis of Stochastic Transistor Aging and Process Variation in Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05187)
*Arash Esshaghi,Siavash Es'haghi,Gholamreza Shahabadi,Alireza Moradi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SMART框架，结合机器学习（随机森林回归）和蒙特卡洛仿真，通过贝叶斯优化自动调参，在保持平均精度误差仅1.63%的同时，将可靠性分析时间减少94.54%，解决了纳米尺度下BTI和PV导致的数字电路可靠性分析瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着CMOS技术进入深纳米时代，BTI和PV的随机效应威胁数字电路可靠性，传统基于密集仿真或查找表的分析方法在大规模设计中无法扩展，形成设计空间探索的关键瓶颈。

Method: 提出SMART框架：采用随机森林回归直接预测门延迟分布，绕过耗时的原子模型参数提取；结合蒙特卡洛仿真；使用贝叶斯优化进行自动化超参数调优，确保跨不同工艺库的预测鲁棒性。

Result: 在ISCAS85基准电路上验证，相比最先进方法，分析时间减少94.54%，平均精度误差仅为1.63%。

Conclusion: 通过将计算复杂度转移到离线训练阶段，SMART为设计可靠性感知的数字系统提供了可扩展、高精度的解决方案。

Abstract: As CMOS technology scales into the deep nanometer regime, digital circuit reliability is increasingly threatened by the combined stochastic effects of Bias Temperature Instability (BTI) and Process Variation (PV). Traditional reliability analysis methods, which rely on computationally intensive simulations or extensive lookup tables, fail to scale efficiently for large designs, creating a critical bottleneck in design space exploration. To address this, we propose SMART, a novel framework that integrates Machine Learning (ML) with Monte Carlo simulation to enable rapid, high-fidelity reliability analysis. SMART employs Random Forest regression to predict gate delay distributions directly, bypassing time-consuming atomic model parameter extractions. Crucially, the model utilizes Bayesian Optimization for automated hyperparameter tuning, ensuring maximum predictive robustness across diverse libraries. Experimental validation on ISCAS85 benchmark circuits demonstrates that SMART achieves a 94.54% reduction in analysis time compared to state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining a remarkable average accuracy error of just 1.63%. By shifting computational complexity to an offline training phase, the proposed framework offers a scalable, accurate solution for designing resilient, reliability-aware digital systems.

</details>


### [135] [Training Hybrid Block Diffusion Language Models with Partial Bidirectionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02805)
*Pranshu Chaturvedi,Parth Shroff,Tarun Suresh,Hangoo Kang,Kaiyue Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BDLM Mamba-H，一种Mamba-注意力混合架构，通过将逆向Mamba扫描限制在活跃去噪块内实现精确缓存，在长上下文生成中相比全序列DiffuMamba-H提升19.7倍吞吐量（65K token），相比BDLM注意力提升3.7倍吞吐量（262K token）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文生成中，传统自回归解码每个步骤都需要从内存中流式读取完整的KV缓存，导致生成过程受内存带宽限制而非计算限制。先前工作尝试通过高效注意力变体/线性时间混合器（如Mamba）减少内存访问，或通过扩散模型一次生成多个token来增加并行计算，但两者结合存在技术挑战：DiffuMamba等混合扩散模型使用双向Mamba混合，其中的逆向扫描需要扫描整个序列，其状态不是前缀专用的，即使按块扩散也无法精确缓存复用。

Method: 提出BDLM Mamba-H架构，将逆向Mamba扫描的范围限制在当前活跃的去噪块内，而不是扫描整个序列。这样每个块内的状态是自包含的，可以在跨块扩散时实现精确的缓存复用。该架构混合了Mamba和注意力机制。

Result: 在87M参数DCLM实验中，BDLM Mamba-H相比BDLM注意力和全序列基线取得了最优的C4-en验证困惑度。在350M参数下，与BDLM注意力性能相当。长上下文推理方面，在65K token长度下达到全序列DiffuMamba-H的19.7倍吞吐量，在262K token下达到BDLM注意力的3.7倍吞吐量。

Conclusion: Mamba混合架构（BDLM Mamba-H）是长上下文扩散模型的一种有潜力的架构选择，在保持生成质量的同时显著提升了长上下文推理的吞吐量。

Abstract: High-throughput long-context generation is one of the central challenges for large language models. Generation is typically memory-bandwidth-bound rather than compute-bound: each decoding step must stream the accumulated key/value (KV) cache from memory, so bandwidth demand grows with context length while only one token is emitted. Two parallel approaches have therefore emerged: reducing memory access with efficient attention variants and linear-time mixers such as Mamba, or increasing parallel computation by generating blocks of tokens at once. However, technical challenges arise when combining these two ideas. Earlier hybrid diffusion models such as DiffuMamba use bidirectional Mamba mixing, including a reverse-direction scan relative to causal generation. This reverse scan needs to scan the entire sequence, so its states are not prefix-only and cannot be precisely reused as a cache even when diffusion is performed block by block. We propose a BDLM Mamba--attention hybrid that addresses this challenge by restricting the reverse Mamba scan to the active denoising block, which enables exact caching across blocks. In an 87M-parameter DCLM sweep, BDLM Mamba-H achieves the best C4-en validation perplexity compared to BDLM attention and full-sequence baselines. At 350M parameters, it remains competitive with BDLM attention. For long-context inference, BDLM Mamba-H reaches 19.7x the throughput of full-sequence DiffuMamba-H at 65K tokens and 3.7x the throughput of BDLM attention at 262K, showing that Mamba hybrids are a potential long-context diffusion architecture.

</details>


### [136] [Less Tokens, Better Forecasts: Sparse Residual Routing for Efficient Weather Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02829)
*Janet Wang,Yunbei Zhang,Lin Zhao,Xi Xiao,Jihun Hamm,Xiao Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse-Reslim，一种无参数、即插即用的路由模块，通过仅将25%的空间token送入昂贵的Transformer中间块并作为残差更新处理，使得稀疏token处理兼容天气预报中固定网格的稠密预测需求，在提升精度的同时实现了约2.5-3.18倍的训练加速和2.2倍以上的峰值内存降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于ViT的天气预报模型对所有空间token进行统一计算，但邻近网格点数值相似且大区域随时间平滑演变，导致大量中间token计算冗余。标准的token效率方法（如剪枝、融合）不适用于天气预报，因为该任务是时空稠密预测，需要在原始经纬度网格上保留每个网格单元的物理意义。

Method: Sparse-Reslim是一种无参数、即插即用的路由模块，仅将25%的空间token路由到昂贵的Transformer中间块，并将这些块视为残差更新：计算被路由token的变化量，然后将该增量散射回完整序列。未选中的token保持路由前的表示不变，不引入掩码token、融合层或额外参数。

Result: 在ERA5数据上（最高0.25°分辨率）和两种模型族（确定性Transformer和扩散模型）上，Sparse-Reslim在所有评估变量上提升了预测精度；训练加速约2.5倍（主要设置），在0.25°分辨率下达3.18倍加速，峰值内存降低超过2.2倍。

Conclusion: 稀疏路由本身带来了精度提升，随机token选择在不增加选择器开销的情况下提供了额外的正则化收益，表明Sparse-Reslim是一种有效的稀疏token处理方法，适用于天气预报这一稠密预测任务。

Abstract: Existing ViT-based weather forecasting models apply uniform computation across all spatial tokens, even though nearby atmospheric grid points often contain similar values and large regions evolve smoothly over time. This makes much of the intermediate per-token computation redundant. Standard token-efficiency methods, such as pruning or merging, reduce cost by removing or fusing tokens. However, weather forecasting is a spatiotemporal dense prediction problem in which a history of atmospheric states must be mapped to future values on the original latitude-longitude grid. Thus, every grid cell must retain a physically meaningful representation, especially under autoregressive rollout. We introduce Sparse-Reslim, a parameter-free plug-in routing module that makes sparse token processing compatible with this fixed-grid requirement. Sparse-Reslim routes only 25% of spatial tokens through the expensive middle transformer blocks and treats those blocks as residual updates: it computes the change produced for the routed tokens and scatters only this delta back to the full sequence. Unselected tokens keep their pre-routing representations exactly, so no grid cell is dropped or replaced by a mask token, and no fusion layer or additional parameters are introduced. Across ERA5 resolutions up to the operational 0.25\textdegree{} standard and two model families, a deterministic Transformer and a diffusion model, Sparse-Reslim improves forecast accuracy on every evaluated variable while substantially reducing cost: training is about 2.5x faster in the main settings and reaches 3.18x speedup at 0.25\textdegree{}, with over 2.2x lower peak memory. A controlled decomposition shows that the accuracy gain comes primarily from sparse routing itself, while random token selection provides an additional regularization benefit without selector overhead.

</details>


### [137] [On the Design Space of Discrete Diffusion Online Adaptation for Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02834)
*Trevor Chen,Ariel Dai,Jason Yang,Riccardo De Santi,Daniel Khalil,Wenda Chu,Nate Gruver,Pranav Murugan,Alexander F. G. Goldberg,Maruan Al-Shedivat,Yisong Yue*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对离散扩散模型，研究了在线自适应分子优化中多种策略（获取函数、奖励塑形、模型去偏、重放、有效性控制）的相互作用，并提出了一种综合配方，在有限oracle预算下优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常从预训练生成模型开始，但测试时目标是从该先验采样，而是利用有限的oracle预算将生成导向任务特定的高奖励分子。之前各策略（候选评估、奖励反馈更新、反馈重用、偏离先验程度）被孤立研究，不清楚它们在完整在线自适应循环中是否互补、冗余或相互干扰。

Method: 在6个小分子结合亲和力任务和3个蛋白质适应性任务上进行受控实验，系统研究获取函数、奖励塑形、模型去偏、重放和有效性控制在在线微调中的组合效果。

Result: 获取函数、奖励塑形和模型去偏提供了互补的提升路线（尤其对小分子）；重放稳定学习；有效性惩罚使小分子探索保持在有效分子流形上；综合配方在匹配的oracle预算和GPU时间下优于离线微调和推理时搜索基线；高奖励候选需要更大先验偏移时增益最大。

Conclusion: 在线微调结合获取函数、奖励塑形、去偏、重放和有效性控制是一种反馈高效的分子优化实用配方。

Abstract: Molecular optimization often starts from a pretrained generative model that captures a broad prior over valid molecular structures. At test time, however, the goal is not to sample from this prior, but to use a limited oracle budget to shift generation toward task-specific high-reward molecules. We study this adaptation problem for discrete diffusion models. Each online round couples several choices. The loop must decide which candidates to evaluate, how rewards become model updates, which feedback to reuse, and how far to move beyond the pretrained prior. These choices have mostly been studied in isolation, leaving open whether they complement one another, become redundant, or interfere inside a full online adaptation loop. We conduct controlled studies across six small-molecule binding-affinity tasks and three protein-fitness tasks. We find that acquisition, reward shaping, and model debiasing provide complementary routes to higher reward, especially for small molecules. Replay further stabilizes learning, while validity penalties keep small-molecule exploration on the valid molecular manifold. Together, these findings point to a practical recipe for feedback-efficient molecular optimization: online fine-tuning with acquisition, reward shaping, debiasing, replay, and validity control. This recipe outperforms offline fine-tuning and inference-time search baselines under matched oracle-call budgets and GPU-hour accounting. The gains are largest when high-reward candidates require larger shifts from the pretrained prior.

</details>


### [138] [Labeled-Data-Free Meta-Learning: Efficient Task Generation Using Pre-trained Models and Unlabeled Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02850)
*Lei Sun,Yusuke Tanaka,Tomoharu Iwata*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种无需模型反转的数据无关元学习新范式，通过利用预训练模型为无标签数据生成软标签来构建元训练任务，并引入基于任务置信度和类别分布均衡的任务加权机制，相比现有方法实现最高104倍加速和8.4%-36.4%的小样本分类准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据无关元学习（DFML）依赖模型反转技术生成训练数据，这一过程计算开销大且难以生成高维数据以匹配原始分布，阻碍了DFML的实际应用。

Method: 提出无需模型反转的新设置，利用预训练模型为无标签数据分配软标签来生成元训练任务；针对任务质量差异，设计基于任务置信度和类别分布平衡的任务加权机制。

Result: 相比SOTA DFML方法，实现高达104倍的速度提升和8.4%-36.4%的小样本分类准确率提升。

Conclusion: 该方法大幅降低了计算成本并提升了泛化性能，为数据受限场景下的元学习提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Meta-learning without labeled data is crucial for real-world applications, where obtaining labeled datasets can be expensive or restricted due to privacy concerns. Data-Free Meta-Learning (DFML) addresses this challenge by leveraging pre-trained models without access to training data. However, existing DFML methods rely on model inversion to generate training data, a process that is generally difficult and computationally expensive due to the need to generate high-dimensional data matching the original distribution. To address this limitation, we propose a novel meta-learning setting that avoids model inversion by jointly leveraging pre-trained models and unlabeled data. Our method generates meta-training tasks by assigning soft labels from pre-trained models to unlabeled data. Since the quality of these tasks can vary, we introduce a task-weighting mechanism based on task confidence and class distribution balance to ensure effective meta-learning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach substantially reduces computational cost and improves generalization, achieving up to 104-fold speedup and 8.4 percent to 36.4 percent improvements in few-shot classification accuracy compared to state-of-the-art DFML methods.

</details>


### [139] [Trading Confidence: Comprehensive Uncertainty Estimation in Algorithmic Trading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02864)
*Lin Li,Li Rong Wang,Hsuan Fu,Xiuyi Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个集成分布、认知和偶然三种不确定性估计的强化学习框架，通过SHAP加权重建不确定性、MC Dropout和基于LSTM的技术指标共识机制，在五个美国主要股指上显著优于传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融市场高度不确定，价格波动受随机波动、模型局限和制度转变影响；传统RL模型难以适应突如其来的市场波动，导致次优交易决策。

Method: 提出不确定性感知的RL框架，集成三类不确定性估计：SHAP加权重建不确定性、MC Dropout、LSTM-based技术指标共识机制。

Result: 在五个美国主要股指上，配备不确定性估计的RL智能体在回报和风险管理方面显著优于传统模型。

Conclusion: 不确定性估计能有效提升RL在金融交易中的表现，未来可拓展至其他资产类别和替代RL架构。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful approach in financial trading, enabling agents to learn optimal strategies through direct market interaction. However, financial markets are highly uncertain, with price fluctuations driven by stochastic volatility, model limitations, and regime shifts. Traditional RL models struggle in dynamic environments, often failing to adapt to sudden market disruptions, leading to suboptimal trading decisions. To address this challenge, we propose an uncertainty-aware RL framework that integrates distributional, epistemic, and aleatoric uncertainty estimations. Our approach enhances uncertainty estimation using SHAP-weighted reconstruction uncertainty, MC Dropout, and an LSTM-based technical indicator consensus mechanism. Experimental results on five major U.S. stock indices demonstrate that RL agents equipped with uncertainty estimation significantly outperform traditional models in return and risk management. This study advances uncertainty estimation in RL-based financial trading, with future research extending its application to other asset classes and alternative RL architectures for greater adaptability.

</details>


### [140] [Reward Granularity in RLVR: Comparing Process and Outcome Reward Structures for Mathematical Reasoning in Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02869)
*Anagha Radhakrishna Palandye,Rebecca Glick,Osheen Kaul*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了五种奖励条件（无RL、仅过程奖励、仅结果奖励、三种混合权重）对Qwen2.5-0.5B模型在GSM8K数学推理任务上的影响，发现过程监督显著优于结果监督（63.73% vs 53.75%），混合奖励与过程权重正相关，但低过程权重配置出现异常退化，表明奖励粒度是RLVR的一阶设计决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大多数RLVR工作仅奖励最终答案（基于结果的奖励），而步骤级过程监督（过程奖励）的影响尚未充分探索，特别是对于缺乏在稀疏反馈下自我纠正能力的小模型。

Method: 对Qwen2.5-0.5B模型使用GRPO在GSM8K上微调，比较五种奖励条件：无RL基线、仅过程奖励、仅结果奖励、以及三种混合权重（过程权重λ∈{0.9, 0.5, 0.1}）。使用GPT-4o作为评判者进行错误分析。

Result: 仅过程监督准确率63.73% vs 仅结果监督53.75%，差距近10个百分点；过程模型生成结构不一致但算术扎实的推理链，结果模型生成简洁但易出推导错误的链；低过程权重（λ=0.1）性能低于纯结果监督，表明存在冲突的优化信号。

Conclusion: 奖励粒度是RLVR的一阶设计决策，过程级监督能显著提升小语言模型的准确率和推理链保真度。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as a promising paradigm for improving mathematical reasoning in language models. Yet most RLVR work rewards only the final answer (outcome-based rewards), leaving the impact of step-level process supervision (process rewards) underexplored especially for small models that lack the capacity to self-correct under sparse feedback. We systematically compare five reward conditions applied to Qwen2.5-0.5B fine-tuned with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) on GSM8K: a no-RL baseline, process-only, outcome-only, and three hybrid weightings ($λ\in \{0.9, 0.5, 0.1\}$ process weight). Process-only supervision achieves 63.73% test accuracy versus 53.75% for outcome-only, a nearly 10-percentage point gap while yielding reasoning traces with higher step validity and lower deviation from ground-truth chain length. Hybrid rewards generally correlate positively with process weight, with one notable anomaly: the low-process / high-outcome configuration ($λ=0.1$) underperforms pure outcome supervision, suggesting conflicting optimization signals. Error analysis using GPT-4o as a judge reveals distinct failure mode distributions: process models generate structurally inconsistent but arithmetically grounded traces, while outcome models produce concise but derivation-error-prone chains. Our results demonstrate that reward granularity is a first-order design decision for RLVR, with process-level supervision substantially improving both accuracy and trace fidelity in small language models.

</details>


### [141] [Poisson-Gamma Modeling of Inter-Relational Dependencies in Dynamic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02872)
*Nan Fang,Yijun Wang,Hao Liao,Sikun Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PGRE模型，利用Poisson-Gamma公式和Gamma马尔可夫过程对动态知识图谱中的时序和关系依赖进行概率建模，在稀疏场景下的链接预测任务中取得竞争性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态知识图谱随时间演化且常存在噪声和不完整性问题，现有方法难以有效建模其时序依赖和跨关系依赖，亟需一种能够同时捕获这两种依赖关系的概率模型。

Method: 采用Poisson-Bernoulli公式表示多关系时序链接；引入Gamma分布潜变量捕获实体-因子关联及由共享潜社区介导的跨关系依赖；使用Gamma马尔可夫过程建模潜变量的时序演化。

Result: 在基准数据集上的链接预测任务中达到竞争性性能，尤其在稀疏设定下表现突出，并能揭示动态知识图谱中有意义的关系演化模式。

Conclusion: PGRE能够有效建模动态知识图谱中的时序和关系依赖，在稀疏场景下优势明显，并具有可解释的关系演化分析能力。

Abstract: Dynamic knowledge graphs are ubiquitous in today's AI applications, as we represent molecular structures, social relationships, and language information using these graph models. As knowledge graphs evolve over time and are often noisy and incomplete, modeling their temporal and relational dependencies becomes crucial for downstream tasks. To address these challenges, this paper proposes PGRE (Poisson-Gamma Relational Evolution), a probabilistic model for modeling inter-relational dependencies in dynamic knowledge graphs. PGRE represents multi-relational temporal links via a Poisson-Bernoulli formulation. It introduces Gamma-distributed latent variables to capture entity-factor associations and cross-relation dependencies mediated by shared latent communities. A Gamma Markov process further models the temporal evolution of these latent variables, enabling principled characterization of relational dynamics. Experiments on benchmark datasets show that PGRE achieves competitive performance in link prediction, particularly in sparse settings, while revealing meaningful relational evolution patterns in dynamic knowledge graphs.

</details>


### [142] [Dynamic Regret for Non-Stationary Linear Bandits via Misspecification Reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02891)
*Zihao Hu,Yuan Yao,Jiheng Zhang,Zhengyuan Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非平稳线性赌博机问题，其中每轮的可行决策集随时间变化，且奖励参数也沿路径漂移。通过将非平稳性统一视为误规范问题，采用分块重启策略，在无需正交结构假设的前提下，对一般紧致决策集和K臂情境线性赌博机构达到了最优的\(\widetilde O(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3})\)动态遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优算法依赖正交结构假设，这在上下文相关的实际应用中（如广告投放、动态定价、个性化治疗）难以满足。作者旨在移除这一限制，提出更通用的方法。

Method: 将时间轴分块，每个块内的参数漂移视为有界误规范；利用对误规范具有鲁棒性的线性赌博机算法，在每个块内求解固定参数基准下的遗憾；通过重启机制整合各块，导出动态遗憾上界。

Result: 对一般紧致决策集的线性赌博机和K臂情境线性赌博机，均获得了\(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3}\)的最优动态遗憾依赖（忽略对数因子），无需正交结构假设。

Conclusion: 提出的误规范统一视角有效，拓展了非平稳线性赌博机理论到更实际的设定中，为上下文相关的在线决策提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Many online decision-making problems involve both round-specific feasible actions and drifting reward models: eligible ad impressions, feasible prices, and available treatments can change over time, while user preferences, demand curves, and patient responses may evolve. Motivated by these applications, we study non-stationary linear bandits with round-specific feasible decision sets. Existing methods that obtain the optimal \(\widetilde O(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3})\) dependence, where \(P_T\) is the path length of the reward-parameter sequence, impose an orthogonal-structure assumption on round-specific decision sets, which can be restrictive in contextual applications. We address this gap through a unified misspecification-reduction viewpoint: after partitioning the horizon into blocks, we relate each block's dynamic regret to regret against a fixed-parameter linear bandit benchmark, with the within-block parameter drift entering as bounded misspecification. Restarting algorithms with misspecification-dependent regret guarantees then yields the optimal \(T^{2/3}P_T^{1/3}\) dynamic-regret dependence for both linear bandits with general compact decision sets and \(K\)-armed contextual linear bandits.

</details>


### [143] [Variable Bit-width Quantization: Learning Per-Group Precision for "Bigger-but-Smaller" Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02893)
*Hamish Ogilvy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出可变比特宽度量化(VBQ)方法，让每组64个权重通过Gumbel-Softmax学习独立的{1,2,4,8}比特精度。VBQ发现高度异质的精度分配（69%组坍缩到1比特，LM头仅1.09比特，首个MLP块保持~2.5比特）。该模式可冻结为固定配方复用。131M模型在1.82平均比特下PPL 4.2超越55M FP16模型(PPL 4.4)，存储减少3.8倍；1.46B模型匹配593M FP16基线，存储减少~3.7倍。质量-字节效率比FP16高3.9-8.4倍。分布分析揭示深层逐步自愈浅层注入的量化误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低比特量化将精度视为单一的全局超参数，每个权重使用相同比特宽度，无法自适应分配精度资源。本文旨在将精度变为可学习的非均匀资源，通过不均匀分配固定比特预算来超越均匀分配的性能。

Method: 提出VBQ方法，对每组连续64个权重通过Gumbel-Softmax松弛从{1,2,4,8}比特中学习独立分辨率；使用交替优化联合训练精度logits；发现稳定异质分配模式后冻结为固定配方复用；实现自定义融合反量化-乘法的内核加速推理。

Result: 131M模型1.82平均比特PPL 4.2优于55M FP16的PPL 4.4，存储减少3.8倍；1.46B模型匹配593M FP16，存储减少~3.7倍，参数多2.5倍。质量-字节效率比FP16高3.9-8.4倍。推理加速随规模增长：131M持平，1.0B加速1.9倍，9B加速4.7倍。69%权重坍缩至1比特，LM头1.09比特，首个MLP块~2.5比特。

Conclusion: VBQ将精度重新定义为可学习的非均匀资源，证明不均匀分配固定比特预算优于均匀分配；深层可自愈浅层量化误差是关键机制；该方法是从零开始的训练时现象，但扩展到1.5B以上参数仍具挑战。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization shrinks language models but treats precision as a single global hyper-parameter: every weight uses the same bit-width. We introduce Variable Bit-width Quantization (VBQ), a training-time method in which each contiguous group of 64 weights learns its own resolution from {1,2,4,8} bits via a Gumbel-Softmax relaxation, trained jointly by an alternating optimization that gives the precision logits a clean, task-aligned signal. VBQ discovers a consistent, strongly heterogeneous allocation within individual projection types, not merely across layers, impossible to express with per-layer methods: 69% of groups collapse to 1 bit, the LM head averages 1.09 bits, while the first MLP block keeps ~2.5 bits. This pattern is stable enough to freeze into a fixed recipe and reuse without further search. The recipe yields a "bigger-but-smaller" regime: a 131M model at 1.82 mean bits reaches perplexity 4.2 on TinyStories, beating a 55M FP16 model (PPL 4.4) at 3.8x less storage, and lets a 1.46B model on FineWeb-Edu match a 593M FP16 control at ~3.7x less storage with 2.5x more parameters. As quality-per-byte, VBQ is 3.9-8.4x more efficient than FP16.
