Motivation: 现有概率流预测方法局限于集总式模型，而确定性LSTM径流模型正向分布式发展。将概率预测从集总式迁移到分布式模型时，需要关注上游径流不确定性的空间联合结构这一新需求。

Method: 在日本案例中训练两个概率流域尺度径流LSTM，通过Hayami汇流方案进行汇流。对比随机匹配上游集合成员与分位数匹配策略两种采样方式对下游集合离散度的影响。

Result: 随机匹配上游集合成员导致下游集合严重欠离散，而分位数匹配策略能恢复大部分与直接流域尺度参考相当的离散度。

Conclusion: 从集总式向分布式概率水文模型的转变需要明确关注径流不确定性的空间联合结构，简单独立采样无法保持下游概率预测的合理离散度。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification of hydrological predictions is necessary to inform operational decisions. Recent generative machine-learning methods have advanced probabilistic streamflow prediction, but have remained confined to lumped models that predict a basin outlet directly. At the same time, deterministic LSTM runoff models are increasingly applied at grid or catchment scale and routed through river networks to produce spatially continuous, physically consistent discharge fields. This technical note argues that moving probabilistic prediction from lumped to distributed models introduces a specific new requirement: the joint distribution of upstream runoff generation must be sampled jointly. In lumped inference, the model predicts the outlet distribution directly and can modulate spread from basin attributes. In distributed inference, downstream discharge is obtained by routing many upstream runoff predictions, so independent local sampling averages uncertainty away. Using Japan as a case study, we train two probabilistic basin-scale runoff LSTMs and route their runoff through a Hayami routing scheme. Randomly matching upstream ensemble members produces severely under-dispersed downstream ensembles, whereas a simple quantile matching strategy restores much of the spread of the direct basin-scale reference. The shift from lumped to distributed probabilistic hydrology therefore requires explicit attention to the spatial joint structure of runoff uncertainty.

</details>


### [180] [PhenoNEST: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Ontology-Aware Multimodal Plant Phenotyping and Trait Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03245)
*Jayant Ghadge,Soumyashree Kar,Surya S. Durbha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个多模态细粒度知识图谱框架，通过整合非结构化文本、RGB图像和标准化本体，实现小麦基因型-表型交互的时序监测与空间定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高通量植物表型数据常以非结构化文本和孤立RGB图像形式存在，缺乏语义关联，难以有效监测基因型-表型随时间及实验条件的交互关系。

Method: 1) 从噪声田间笔记中抽取实体和关系，通过RDF-typing构建层次化类实体的知识图谱；2) 使用PlantDeBERTa将图节点与标准化本体（PO, RO, WTO）对齐；3) 利用视觉-语言模型结合小麦分割ViT生成注意力软图，将KG实体映射到图像像素；4) 引入Plant_Obs_Id节点实现多模态子图时序连接。

Result: 在500个WisWheat样本上，采用Pointing Game准确率、VWSD和排序指标评估，成功将复杂田间观测映射为结构化图，实现田间笔记自动审核、时序胁迫监测和空间性状定位。

Conclusion: 该神经符号方法有效弥合了非结构化文本与图像之间的语义鸿沟，为小麦育种提供了结构化的多模态知识表示工具。

Abstract: High-throughput plant phenotyping generates valuable data that often remains trapped in unstructured text and isolated RGB images. To bridge this semantic gap, we propose a framework for constructing a multimodal granular Knowledge Graph (KG) to monitor genotype-phenotype interactions across time and experiments. In this work, we focus on wheat Triticum aestivum as a representative target crop to validate our methodology across complex canopy environments. Our pipeline first distills noisy field notes to extract entities and relations, dynamically constructing the KG by converting unique instances into hierarchical class entities via RDF-typing. These graph nodes are then aligned with standardized ontologies (PO, RO, WTO) using PlantDeBERTa. To visually ground the constructed graph, a Vision-Language Model paired with a wheat-segmentation ViT generates attention-based softmaps, linking specific KG entities directly to image pixels. We introduce a central observation node Plant_Obs_Id to connect these multimodal subgraphs temporally. Evaluated on 500 curated WisWheat samples using Pointing Game accuracy, Visual Word Sense Disambiguation (VWSD), and rank-based metrics, our neuro-symbolic approach successfully maps complex field observations to a structured graph. This enables automated field note auditing, temporal stress monitoring, and precise spatial trait localization for wheat breeders.

</details>


### [181] [Unbiased Alignment for Large Language Models with Noisy Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03248)
*Jialiang Wang,Xianming Liu,Xiong Zhou,Hui Liu,Haoliang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对偏好数据集中的噪声问题，提出了无偏奖励模型（URM）和无偏直接偏好优化（UDPO）损失函数，通过数学校正实现从噪声数据中无偏训练模型，理论证明具有噪声容忍、参数向下兼容和分类校准特性，实验优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RLHF和DPO方法在真实偏好数据集中存在显著噪声，导致模型对齐效果受损，亟需一种能够从噪声数据中实现无偏对齐的理论框架。

Method: 提出URM损失和UDPO损失，通过数学纠正偏好噪声引起的分布扭曲，使模型可直接从带噪声数据集中无偏训练，无需干净标注作为监督信号。

Result: 理论证明方法具有噪声容忍性、参数向下兼容性和分类校准性；实验在多个数据集上超越现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 所提出的无偏对齐框架能有效解决偏好噪声问题，为实际场景中的大模型对齐提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: The alignment of large language models with human preferences is commonly achieved through Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback or Direct Preference Optimization. However, these methods are vulnerable to the significant noise prevalent in real-world preference datasets. To address this critical issue, we present a theoretical framework for unbiased alignment, introducing the Unbiased Reward Model (URM) loss and the Unbiased Direct Preference Optimization (UDPO) loss. By mathematically correcting the distortion induced by preference noise, our novel objectives enable unbiased model training directly from noisy datasets, without requiring clean ground-truth supervision. We provide rigorous theoretical analyses demonstrating that our methods are noise-tolerant, parameter downward compatible, and classification-calibrated. Comprehensive experiments across diverse datasets demonstrate that our approaches outperform state-of-the-art baselines. Code available at: https://github.com/cswjl/unbiased-alignment.

</details>


### [182] [From Global to Local: Efficient Regional Weather Downscaling with Global Weather Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03279)
*Wiktor Kamzela,Jakub Kubiak,Adam Dobosz,Jędrzej Miczke,Anatol Kaczmarek,Piotr Wyrwiński,Wojciech Stefaniak,Wojciech Kotłowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预训练天气基础模型的降尺度框架，通过学习全局预报的区域细化，在潜空间中使用轻量级多尺度预测头，实现两数量级网格分辨率提升，无需重新训练，在计算成本极低的情况下优于传统区域数值天气预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限区域模型计算成本高，而现有学习方法多将问题简化为超分辨率，忽略了跨尺度的统计和物理不匹配问题。

Method: 基于预训练天气骨干模型，在其潜空间中添加轻量级多尺度预测头，学习全局预报的区域细化；使用区域数值模拟作为训练目标，并利用地面气象站观测评估系统偏差。

Result: 在大部分指标上以极低的计算成本优于传统数值天气预报；基于全局预训练天气基础模型的潜空间方法优于标准图像超分辨率方法。

Conclusion: 该框架能有效实现跨尺度的区域天气降尺度，无需重新训练即可大幅提升分辨率，具备高效、准确的优点。

Abstract: Accurate regional weather prediction requires resolving fine-scale structure while remaining consistent with global dynamics. Traditional limited area models rely on computationally expensive simulations, while many learning-based approaches frame the problem as super-resolution, overlooking statistical and physical mismatches across scales. We propose a foundation-model-driven downscaling framework that learns regional refinements of global forecasts by augmenting a pretrained weather model backbone with lightweight, multi-scale prediction heads operating directly in its latent space. Despite being trained on substantially coarser inputs, the pretrained backbone supports regional adaptation at resolutions corresponding to a two-order-of-magnitude increase in grid-cell resolution, without the need for retraining. The proposed approach uses regional numerical simulations as training targets and is evaluated not only against gridded datasets but also against ground-based weather station observations, enabling analysis of systematic biases between global reanalysis, regional simulations, and in-situ weather station observations. Our experiments show improved accuracy in comparison to NWP on most of the metrics at the fraction of computational cost. Moreover, we observe that building on a latent space of globally pre-trained weather foundation model offers better downscaling capabilities than the standard image-based super-resolution approaches.

</details>


### [183] [A harmonised dataset for Earth system foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03298)
*Carlos Rodriguez-Pardo,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WorldTensor是一个将数百个环境和社会经济变量统一到标准化0.25°空间网格和年时间框架下的全球协调数据集，旨在为训练多模态地球系统基础模型提供支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球系统基础模型主要关注物理气候和天气数据，缺乏对驱动和响应环境变化的人类系统的表征，且缺乏统一的气候、土地、海洋、冰冻圈、基础设施、灾害和社会经济数据全球训练资源。

Method: 将再分析产品、遥感、排放清单、土地利用重建、水文观测、基础设施和灾害数据集以及社会经济指标，通过重网格化、栅格化点/矢量数据、协调不同时间覆盖范围等方式，统一到0.25°空间网格和年时间框架下，以NetCDF格式输出并遵循CF元数据约定。

Result: 生成了一个标准化、可复现的全球数据集，包含数百个变量，覆盖环境与人类系统，适用于机器学习工作流。

Conclusion: WorldTensor为训练和评估学习环境与人类系统耦合动力学的地球系统基础模型提供了可复现的资源，有望推动真正多模态地球系统基础模型的发展。

Abstract: Foundation models for Earth systems have so far been trained primarily on physical climate and weather data, with limited representation of the human systems that both drive and respond to environmental change. The lack of a unified global training resource that combines climate, land, ocean, cryosphere, infrastructure, hazards, and socioeconomic data on a common grid hinders progress toward truly multimodal Earth system foundation models. We present WorldTensor, a harmonised global dataset that aligns hundreds of environmental and socioeconomic variables to a standardised 0.25$^\circ$ spatial grid and annual temporal framework. WorldTensor integrates reanalysis products, remote sensing, emissions inventories, land use reconstructions, hydrological observations, infrastructure and hazard datasets, and socioeconomic indicators within a single representation designed for machine learning workflows. To build the dataset, we regridded inputs across heterogeneous native resolutions and projections, rasterised point and vector datasets into spatially meaningful gridded fields, and reconciled temporal coverages ranging from daily observations to sparse multiyear socioeconomic snapshots. All outputs are distributed as NetCDF files with standardised coordinates, variable metadata, and a common CF metadata convention. WorldTensor provides a reproducible resource for training and evaluating foundation models that learn coupled dynamics across environmental and human systems at planetary scale.

</details>


### [184] [Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG) Beyond the Euclidean Paradigm, with Application to Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03329)
*Bing Cheng,Yi-Shuai Niu,Howell Tong,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出统计有意义的几何（SMG）框架，将过参数化模型提升到无限维Orlicz统计流形，通过Ehresmann联络1-形式ω作为几何滤波器分离纵向规范噪声与横向学习方向，证明预训练后分布外预测方差受可识别商基流形有限直径严格上界约束，从而硬性遏制生成幻觉；并通过SMG序列自适应流形投影下游更新到历史横向承载的正交补上，实现灾难性遗忘的非渐近完全消除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统均匀收敛界和经验风险最小化在极度过参数化模型（如大型语言变换器、生物序列网络）中失效，优化景观出现平坦纵向规范谷，导致经典泛化度量无效，并引发生成幻觉和灾难性遗忘等严重病理现象。

Method: 将确定型参数模型提升为无限维非参数Orlicz统计流形，将总状态空间建模为微分纤维丛(ℳ,ℬ,π,𝒱,ℋ,ω)，建立Ehresmann联络1-形式ω作为动态几何滤波器，定义双重回溯范式：预训练阶段滤除纵向规范噪声（结构内部方向SID）并沿严格非退化横向分布（统计变分方向SVDχ）隔离学习轨迹，微调阶段通过SMG序列自适应流形将下游更新投影到历史横向承载的正交补上。

Result: 证明在联络滤波预训练下，分布外预测方差严格受可识别商基流形ℬ的有限直径上界约束，实现生成幻觉的硬几何遏制；通过正交补投影，证明灾难性遗忘的总非渐近消除。

Conclusion: SMG用无坐标的拓扑约束替代经验微调启发式，将高级微分几何与AI结构可靠性相连接，为过参数化模型提供了具有统计意义的几何学习范式。

Abstract: Conventional uniform convergence bounds and empirical risk minimization break down in massive over-parameterized models, such as large language transformers and biological sequence networks. With near-infinite unconstrained internal degrees of freedom, their optimization landscapes develop flat vertical gauge valleys, rendering classical generalization metrics vacuous and inducing severe pathologies, specifically generative hallucination and catastrophic forgetting. We introduce the Statistically Meaningful Geometry (SMG) framework, an information-geometric paradigm lifting deterministic parametric models into infinite-dimensional non-parametric Orlicz statistical manifolds. Modeling the total state space as a differential fiber bundle ($\mathcal{M}, \mathcal{B}, π, \mathcal{V}, \mathcal{H}, ω$), we establish a Two-Fold Inference Paradigm. We formalize an Ehresmann connection 1-form $ω$ as a dynamic geometric filter that strips away vertical gauge noise (Structural Internal Directions, or SID) and isolates learning trajectories along the strictly non-degenerate horizontal distribution (Statistical Variational Directions, or SVD$χ$). We prove that under connection-filtered pre-training, out-of-distribution predictive variance is strictly upper-bounded by the finite diameter of the identifiable quotient base manifold $\mathcal{B}$, establishing a hard geometric containment of generative hallucinations. By projecting downstream updates onto the orthogonal complement of the historical horizontal carriage, we formalize the SMG Sequential Adaptation Flow, proving the total non-asymptotic elimination of catastrophic forgetting. SMG replaces empirical fine-tuning heuristics with coordinate-free topological constraints, bridging advanced differential geometry with structural reliability in AI.

</details>


### [185] [FedAvg for HAR: Exploring the Tradeoff Between Personalized and Generalization Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03334)
*Andrea De Luna,Susanna Peretti,Chiara Contoli,Alessandro Bogliolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在人类活动识别（HAR）领域中的个性化与泛化能力权衡，通过设计集中式、本地和联邦学习三种范式的测试场景以及模拟客户端数据变化场景，实验表明FedAvg算法在保持较高泛化能力的同时具有更好的个性化能力，但在客户端类别分布变化等压力条件下这一优势不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习范式虽能优化数据利用并缓解隐私问题，但需要在模型个性化与泛化能力之间找到平衡点，当前缺乏对二者在压力条件下权衡关系的系统评估。

Method: 设计并实现了集中式学习、本地学习和联邦学习三种范式的对比测试场景，以及模拟客户端数据变化的场景，在HAR领域对FedAvg算法进行实验评估。

Result: FedAvg相比传统集中式学习在保持高泛化能力的同时具有更高的个性化程度，但在客户端类别分布变化等压力条件下这一优势变得不明显。

Conclusion: 联邦学习在HAR任务中确实实现了个性化与泛化之间的良好权衡，但其鲁棒性在数据分布非独立同分布（non-IID）等压力场景下会显著下降，需要进一步优化。

Abstract: The federated learning (FL) paradigm fosters distributed pervasive computing combined with artificial intelligence techniques, allowing for optimized data usage and improved mitigation of privacy concerns. Indeed, model training occurs on the client's local devices, and model parameters are subsequently shared with a centralized server. However, there is a need to find a tradeoff between models' personalization and generalization capabilities. In this paper, we design and implement several testing scenarios devoted to evaluating and comparing the centralized, local, and federated paradigm performances. We also design and implement a scenario that emulates a change in clients' data. We then present experimental results of the FedAvg algorithm applied to the Human Activity Recognition (HAR) domain to understand the trade-off between personalized and generalized accuracy. Results show that, although FedAvg confirms a higher degree of personalization capabilities while keeping a high degree of generalization with respect to the traditional centralized learning, this result is not so obvious under stressful conditions, such as when varying class distribution over clients.

</details>


### [186] [CSympNet-ID: conformal-symplectic map learning for linearly damped Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03339)
*Jiale Gong,Pengzhan Jin,Dongyang Kuang,Lu Li,Yifa Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CSympNet-ID框架，通过构造性强制满足离散共形辛结构来学习线性阻尼哈密顿系统的单步流映射，在数据稀缺、收缩律恢复及高维测试中表现优于非结构化基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从离散观测中学习耗散动力学对于可靠的长期预测和物理有意义的参数辨识至关重要，而现有方法难以同时保证长时可靠性和耗散因子的可解释性。

Method: 构建共形辛网络（CSympNet-ID）：将严格辛神经核心与显式对角缩放层组合，缩放因子通过标量阻尼率参数的指数形式参数化；推导了共形辛映射的缩放共轭分解，并证明了逐点密度结果。

Result: 在不规则步长阻尼振子、阻尼弹簧质量链、阻尼非线性三次振子及高维扩展测试中，CSympNet-ID在数据稀缺场景、目标收缩律恢复和高维测试中取得最优结果。

Conclusion: CSympNet-ID通过构造性强制共形辛结构，实现了耗散因子的可解释性和正性保证，在长时预测和物理参数辨识方面优于非结构化基线。

Abstract: Learning dissipative dynamics from discrete observations is essential for reliable long-horizon prediction and physically meaningful parameter identification. For linearly damped Hamiltonian systems, the exact flow is generally not symplectic but conformally symplectic, contracting the canonical symplectic form by a scalar factor that reflects the net dissipation. We propose Conformal Symplectic Networks with damping identification (CSympNet-ID), a discrete-time map-learning framework that learns the one-step flow map directly from snapshot pairs while enforcing exact discrete conformal symplecticity by construction, without penalty terms or projection. The architecture composes an exact symplectic neural core with explicit diagonal scaling layers whose factors are parameterized exponentially by a scalar damping-rate parameter, thereby guaranteeing positivity and interpretability of the learned dissipation factor. We establish a scaling-conjugacy factorization for conformal symplectic maps and derive a pointwise-in-step density result for CSympNet-ID. We evaluate an irregular-step damped oscillator, a damped spring-mass chain, a damped nonlinear cubic oscillator, and additional high-dimensional extensions. CSympNet-ID gives the most favorable overall results among the compared models in the reported experiments, particularly in data-scarce regimes, target contraction-law recovery, and high-dimensional tests where unstructured baselines degrade rapidly.

</details>


### [187] [The Multiscale Single-Index Model: A Stylized Model for Hierarchical Feature Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03347)
*Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对多尺度单指标模型（MSIM）进行精细的Wiener混沌分析，利用Edgeworth展开揭示其与张量PCA的深层联系，证明在线SGD能以与线性模型相同的样本复杂度实现高概率恢复，并量化了深度带来的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MSIM的谱方法分析（基于张量展开）足以实现高效恢复，但不足以分析基于梯度的反向传播方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过更精细的Wiener混沌分析来理解深层架构如何学习多尺度表示，并证明梯度方法在该模型下的理论保证。

Method: 使用Edgeworth展开对Wiener混沌进行精细分析：在一阶混沌中得到尺度d^{-q/2}的有限秩层次结构；在高阶混沌中，在自然非抵消条件下，平衡展开呈现出阶梯状奇异值平台。在此基础上，利用慢Hermite能量尾条件，推导浅层网络逼近下界，并分析在线SGD在相关目标上的收敛性。

Result: 证明了在线SGD（所有层在同一时间尺度演化）能以n = Õ(d^{K-1})个样本实现1 - o_d(1)的恢复概率，样本复杂度与线性对应模型相同。同时建立了浅层网络的逼近下界，量化了深度带来的好处。

Conclusion: MSIM是非线性张量PCA的自然类比，其精细的混沌结构使得梯度方法（如在线SGD）能以与线性模型相同的样本效率恢复目标特征，为理解深度学习在多尺度表示学习中的优势提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We consider the Multiscale Single-Index Model (MSIM), first introduced in \cite{oymak2021learning}, as a stylized model for hierarchical learning with \emph{scale separation}. Each layer extracts a shared single-index feature at one physical scale and passes it to the next, thus defining a tractable setting in which to study how deep architectures learn multiscale representations. Under non-degeneracy and delocalization assumptions on the link function and planted features respectively, for fixed depth $K$ and local scale $d$, the first Wiener chaos of the target behaves as a perturbed spiked tensor, where the perturbation of order $d^{-1/2}$ comes from the non-linearity -- revealing the MSIM as a natural non-linear analogue of the Tensor PCA model \cite{montanari2014statistical}. While this perturbative picture is sufficient to enable efficient spectral recovery based on Tensor unfolding (as already observed in \cite{oymak2021learning}), it is not precise enough for the analysis of backpropagation gradient-based methods. In this work, we address this limitation by performing a fine-grained analysis of the Wiener chaos using Edgeworth expansions. In the first chaos, this gives a finite-rank hierarchy at scales $d^{-q/2}$. In higher chaoses, balanced flattenings exhibit staircase singular-value plateaus of size $d^{-ρ/2}$ and multiplicity $d^ρ$ under a natural higher-chaos non-cancellation condition. Using this higher-chaos structure, and under an additional slow Hermite-energy tail condition, we first establish shallow-network approximation lower bounds, quantifying the benefit of depth in this model. Next, and most importantly, we prove that online SGD on the correlation objective, where all layers evolve in the same timescale, achieves $1 - o_d(1)$ recovery with $n = \widetilde{O}( d^{K-1})$ samples, recovering the same sample complexity as in the linear counterpart.

</details>


### [188] [PedestrianDiffusion: Multimodal Generative Denoising and Dense State Estimation for Inertial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03349)
*I-Hao Lu,Dongsoo Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PedestrianDiffusion，一种多模态频域生成框架，通过将6D状态估计重构为连续条件去噪过程，利用频域谱协方差约束稳定反向扩散轨迹，并引入零样本语义条件（视觉-语言嵌入）适应异构传感器噪声，在边缘硬件上通过单步确定性概率流ODE求解器实现异步批量轨迹精化，在多个基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统确定性神经架构在消费级MEMS惯性导航中因随机噪声导致“估计抖动”，牺牲高频运动保真度以换取数值稳定性，亟需一种能够兼顾高频精度与鲁棒性的新型框架。

Method: 1) 将6D状态估计重构为频域中的连续条件去噪扩散过程；2) 通过频域谱协方差约束稳定反向扩散轨迹；3) 引入零样本语义条件机制，利用视觉-语言嵌入提供类别先验，泛化至异构传感器噪声；4) 部署单步确定性概率流ODE求解器(T=1)，实现边缘硬件上的异步批量轨迹精化。

Result: 在OxIOD、RIDI、RoNIN、TLIO基准上达到SOTA性能，对脉冲扰动和耦合6D运动学漂移展现出前所未有的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 验证了生成式架构在边缘硬件上异步批量轨迹精化的可行性，为下一代神经惯性测量单元(N-IMU)提供了算法蓝图。

Abstract: The accuracy of consumer-grade inertial navigation is bottlenecked by the stochastic noise of Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems (MEMS). Traditional deterministic neural architectures often succumb to ``estimation jittering,'' sacrificing high-frequency kinematic fidelity for numerical stability. We propose PedestrianDiffusion, a multimodal spectral-domain generative framework reformulating dense 6D state estimation as a continuous conditional denoising process. By operating in the frequency domain, our formulation bounds the spectral covariance, acting as a mathematical preconditioner to stabilize the reverse diffusion trajectory. Furthermore, we introduce a zero-shot semantic conditioning mechanism leveraging vision-language embeddings as categorical priors to generalize across heterogeneous sensor noise profiles. To address the computational intractability of generative tracking, we deploy a single-step deterministic probability flow ODE solver ($T=1$). This yields high-capacity asynchronous batch trajectory refinement, establishing the viability of generative architectures for asynchronous batch trajectory refinement on edge hardware. Extensive evaluations on the OxIOD, RIDI, RoNIN, and TLIO benchmarks demonstrate that PedestrianDiffusion achieves state-of-the-art performance, exhibiting unprecedented robustness to impulse perturbations and coupled 6D kinematic drift. This work provides a rigorous algorithmic blueprint for next-generation Neural Inertial Measurement Units (N-IMUs).

</details>


### [189] [Amortising Bayesian Experimental Design for Sequential Information Gathering in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03426)
*Jakob Hartmann,James Harvey,Jhonathan Navott,Erik Y. Wang,Luckeciano C. Melo,Flaviu Cipcigan,Cheng Zhang,Alessandro Abate*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ASIG方法，通过多轮GRPO结合期望信息增益奖励，将贝叶斯实验设计摊销到LLM策略中，在20 Questions任务上使7B基座模型成功率翻倍且推理成本降低25倍以上，并在医疗诊断任务上展现出分布外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要多轮交互的序列决策场景中，难以有效收集信息，现有推理时贝叶斯实验设计方法计算成本高。

Method: 提出ASIG（摊销式序列信息收集），采用多轮扩展的GRPO算法，以期望信息增益为奖励信号，将贝叶斯实验设计直接摊销到LLM策略的微调过程中。

Result: 在20 Questions任务上，7B基座模型成功率翻倍；推理成本相比BED-LLM基线降低超25倍；在未见过的MediQ医疗诊断基准上也提升了信息收集性能。

Conclusion: 将贝叶斯实验设计摊销到LLM策略中，是实现高效、计算代价低的序列信息收集的有效途径，且学到的策略具备跨分布泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit strong reasoning and world-knowledge capabilities, yet often struggle to gather information effectively across the multi-turn interactions required in sequential decision-making settings. We introduce Amortised Sequential Information Gathering (ASIG), a fine-tuning approach that amortises Bayesian Experimental Design (BED) into LLM policies via a multi-turn extension of Group Relative Policy Optimisation with an Expected Information Gain reward. Evaluated on the 20 Questions task, ASIG more than doubles the success rate of the 7B base model and reduces inference cost by over $25\times$ relative to BED-LLM, a competitive inference-time baseline. Applied to MediQ, a medical diagnosis benchmark unseen during training, ASIG improves information-seeking performance at the 7B scale, suggesting that the learned strategies can transfer out of distribution. Our findings show that amortising BED into LLM policies provides an effective and computationally efficient approach to sequential information gathering.

</details>


### [190] [How Much of the Routing Gap Is Real? Decomposing the Router-to-Oracle Gap into Reproducible Specialist Advantage and Single-Draw Label Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03436)
*Teng-Ruei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM路由中"每实例最优模型"的随机性本质：由于解码随机性，单次抽样的正确性标签只是一个伯努利样本，而非确定性属性。作者将期望最优分解为可复现的单一模型优势(O^{repro})和不可被任何单一决策路由器恢复的选择噪声(Δ)，证明后者只能通过测试时采样（在选定模型上进行best-of-K）来恢复。在LLMRouterBench上的实验表明，单次抽样噪声占据了路由差距的重要部分，尤其在困难查询上接近一半。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM路由研究假设存在一个"每实例最优模型"，但该最优性基于单次随机解码的正确性标签，导致该指标本身具有随机性。作者旨在厘清路由性能上限的统计本质，区分哪些是路由器可以捕获的稳定差异，哪些是随机噪声。

Method: 提出期望每实例最优的分解：O^{exp}=O^{repro}+Δ，其中O^{repro}是可复现的单一模型最优优势，Δ是单次选择噪声下限。理论证明Δ无法被任何单次决策路由器消除，但可通过测试时采样（best-of-K）恢复。实验上采用k≥20的重采样估计噪声份额，并使用单侧、依赖校正和猜测下限校正的边界估计。

Result: 在LLMRouterBench（33模型，391,645实例）上，单次抽样噪声是路由差距的重要部分：在未饱和基准上更大，在最困难查询上接近一半。但大部分差距仍然是可恢复的专业化模型优势。

Conclusion: 路由器的性能上限受限于随机解码噪声，测试时采样（非新路由策略）是恢复这一差距的有效手段。建议未来路由基准采用多抽样评估协议。

Abstract: Routing among large language models (LLMs) promises better quality at lower cost, motivated by the reported gap between learned routers and a per-instance oracle. But that oracle is computed from a single correctness label per (query, model), so under stochastic decoding it is one Bernoulli draw, not a reproducible property. We recast the question structurally: the expected per-instance oracle decomposes as $O^{\exp}=O^{\mathrm{repro}}+Δ$, into reproducible single-commit headroom $O^{\mathrm{repro}}$ and a non-negative single-commit selection floor $Δ$. Our main result is a recoverability asymmetry: this floor is closed by no single-commit router, yet is recovered by test-time sampling -- best-of-$K$ on the committed model, at the oracle's own budget, dominates the independent-pool single-draw oracle. The cap needs no cross-model independence; we prove it with the exact decomposition and noise-share bounds that shrink as the budget grows. The procedure adds no new router, only resampling. The floor's magnitude is a prospective, conservative localization, not an audit: our primary target LLMRouterBench (33 models, 391,645 instances) defines its oracle as a per-query union over single $T=0.2$ generations -- by construction a union of stochastic single draws. Since $O^{\mathrm{repro}}$ is non-identifiable from the released $k=1$ matrix, we estimate the noise share by fresh $k\ge20$ resampling under one-sided, dependence- and guessing-floor-corrected bounds, recasting 'model-recall failure' as thin-support union inflation. On a controlled open-model re-generation, single-draw noise is a substantial minority of the gap -- larger on an unsaturated benchmark, approaching half on the hardest queries where no model is reliable -- while the majority remains recoverable specialist advantage. We release a multi-sample oracle evaluation protocol for routing benchmarks.

</details>


### [191] [No Time Like the Present: Agentic Test-Time Training for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03441)
*Yanbo Wang,Jinhua Hao,Yuze Shi,Kun Yuan,Ming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT)，一种在LLM智能体多轮交互中通过token级重加权缓解训练漂移的方法，在ALFWorld和SWE-bench Lite上分别提升5.0和4.9个成功率点，且开销仅为无TTT的1.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在长轨迹任务中会出现退化现象（重复探索已访问状态、重复失败动作、丢失有效策略），现有测试时训练（TTT）方法大多只做单次静态适应，无法处理多轮智能体场景中自训练循环带来的漂移问题。

Method: aTTT提出token级重加权方法：检测来自先前更新的重复n-gram，降低这些重复token的损失权重，同时保留新颖token的完整权重；基于vLLM的运行时LoRA API构建并发服务系统，在实时回合中执行更新。

Result: 在ALFWorld上提升5.0个成功率点，在SWE-bench Lite上提升4.9个成功率点，开销仅为无TTT的1.9倍。增益主要集中在模型已具备任务能力但会在长轨迹中漂移的场景。

Conclusion: aTTT主要通过保留模型已有能力（而非教授新能力）来提升性能，验证了控制更新文本重复是缓解多轮智能体自训练漂移的关键机制。

Abstract: LLM agents often degrade over long episodes: as trajectories grow, they revisit explored states, repeat failed actions, and lose strategies that previously worked. Test-time training (TTT) offers a way to adapt model weights to the evolving task state, but existing LLM TTT methods largely adapt once to a fixed input. We study continuous TTT in multi-turn agent episodes, where each update changes the policy that generates later training text. This creates a self-training loop that helps when new trajectory information appears, but can amplify drift when the agent gets stuck and repeatedly trains on similar text. We find that update-text repetition distinguishes these regimes and introduce Agentic Test-Time Training (aTTT), a token-level reweighting method that downweights the loss on tokens appearing in repeated $n$-grams from prior updates while leaving novel tokens fully weighted. To run such updates inside live episodes, we build a concurrent serving system using vLLM's runtime LoRA API, limiting overhead to 1.9$\times$ the no-TTT cost. aTTT improves success by up to 5.0 points on ALFWorld and 4.9 points on SWE-bench Lite. The gains concentrate where models already have task competence but drift over long trajectories, suggesting that aTTT mainly preserves existing competence rather than teaching new abilities.

</details>


### [192] [Best-of-Better-$N$: Generating Pre-Aligned Responses with In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03453)
*Eric Lei,Hsiang Hsu,Chun-Fu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Best-of-Better-N（BoBN）方法，通过检索高奖励样本并进行风格重写后作为上下文示例，引导参考LLM的生成分布向高奖励区域偏移，从而在固定采样数N下提升推理时对齐效果，并在安全对齐和数学推理任务上取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统推理时对齐方法（如Best-of-N）受限于参考LLM对高奖励响应的生成概率过低，导致即使通过择优策略也无法选出对齐的输出。本文旨在解决这一根本性瓶颈，提升参考模型生成高质量响应的能力。

Method: 提出BoBN框架：首先从高奖励示例库中检索与输入查询和任务相关的样例，然后通过参考LLM对检索到的响应进行风格重写（restyling），使其与目标任务格式和风格一致；最后将这些重写后的示例作为上下文（in-context learning）输入，引导参考LLM的采样分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Result: 在安全对齐和数学推理基准上验证了BoBN的有效性：在固定采样数N下，BoBN生成的响应质量更高，从而取得更好的对齐性能；同时，达到目标性能所需的N更小。论文还从理论上刻画了上下文学习如何使预训练Transformer的输出分布向高奖励区域偏移。

Conclusion: BoBN通过检索-重写-上下文引导的范式，有效突破了参考模型生成能力的上限，为推理时对齐方法提供了可证明的性能提升，并在多个任务和模型上验证了其通用性和有效性。

Abstract: Inference-time alignment methods, such as Best-of-$N$, offer a flexible alternative to training-based alignment by using reward models to select high-quality responses generated by a reference LLM. However, the efficacy of these methods is inherently limited by the response quality: if the reference LLM assigns negligible probability to high-reward responses, no selection strategy will succeed in finding aligned outputs. In this work, we propose Best-of-Better-$N$ (BoBN), an in context learning-based generation framework to address this challenge. Our method utilizes retrieval from high-reward examples relevant to the input query and task. Crucially, we introduce a restyling step where retrieved responses are rewritten by the reference LLM to align with the target task's format and style. These restyled examples are used in-context to shift the sampling distribution toward the high-reward region. We analytically characterize how in-context learning shifts the output distribution of pretrained transformers toward the high-reward region, resulting in provable benefits on the target task. We then evaluate BoBN on safety alignment and mathematical reasoning benchmarks across several reference LLMs. BoBN's higher-quality responses enable better performance to be achieved when the number of responses $N$ is fixed, and smaller $N$ required to achieve a target performance.

</details>


### [193] [Towards Diverse and Comprehensive Benchmarks for Mutual Information Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03487)
*Alberto Foresti,Ivan Butakov,Alexander Tolmachev,Giulio Franzese,Alexey Frolov,Pietro Michiardi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于copula理论的互信息估计统一基准框架，包含两类测试族，系统评估了非参数、判别式和生成式三类估计器，发现不存在通用最优方法，并识别了不同估计器的根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有互信息估计基准仅在简化的低维分布上评估，缺乏对复杂真实数据场景的性能探索。

Method: 基于copula理论构建统一基准框架，提出两类互补测试：copula-first族（通过合成和流变换系统变化真实MI、维度和边际复杂度）和marginals-first族（将真实图像数据与可控依赖结构结合，扩展同类别配对范式）。

Result: 不存在通用最优估计器；每类估计器在特定设置下可系统性优于所有其他估计器。

Conclusion: 通过分析不同场景下的表现差异，识别了基本估计障碍，并提出了更有效针对这些局限性的新测试。

Abstract: Mutual information (MI) estimation is a central problem in machine learning and statistics; however, existing benchmarks typically evaluate estimators on simplified, low-dimensional distributions, leaving their performance on complex, realistic data largely unexplored. We address this gap with a comprehensive benchmarking framework grounded in a unified copula-theoretic perspective that subsumes existing benchmarks as special cases. Within this framework, we propose two complementary families of tests: a copula-first family that systematically varies ground-truth MI, dimensionality, and marginal complexity using synthetic and flow-based transformations; and a marginals-first family that couples real-world image data with controlled dependency structures, extending the classic same-class-pairing paradigm. We use this suite to extensively evaluate three classes of estimators: non-parametric, discriminative, and generative. Contrary to prevailing assumptions, our results indicate that there is no universal winner: each category can systematically outperform all other estimators under specific setups. By analyzing these cases, we identify fundamental estimation barriers and propose new tests that more effectively stress these specific limitations. We share the open source code at https://github.com/VanessB/mutinfo.

</details>


### [194] [A Near-Linear-Time Solver for Graph $p$-Laplacian Semi-Supervised Learning via Continuation in $p$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03503)
*Oren E. Livne*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图半监督学习中p=2能量退化的问题，提出基于p-Laplacian流的高效近线性求解器，通过阻尼弦牛顿延拓法保持系统良态，实现大规模图上1.5-14倍加速，内存O(m)远优于直接分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准p=2的图半监督学习在标签稀缺且维度d≥2时，增加无标签数据会导致估计退化为近常函数，模型失效。需要采用p>d的非线性p-Laplacian能量以保证适定性，但现有求解器在大型图中存在计算瓶颈：直接分解内存爆炸，线性化系统在大p时因权重退化而近奇异，无法直接套用近线性Laplacian求解器。

Method: 将p-Laplacian半监督学习重写为源形式非线性Laplacian流Bρ_p(B^T x)=b，采用阻尼弦牛顿延拓法在p上连续迭代求解。每步线性化系统保持良态，可委托给近线性Laplacian求解引擎。该方法将非线性问题转化为一系列良态的加权Laplacian求解。

Result: 在规模缩放图族上，经验壁钟时间为m^0.96-m^1.02（近似Cholesky默认）；跨228个SuiteSparse图池化拟合为m^1.19 vs 直接分解的m^1.45。可处理6.8×10^7边社交网络。Cholesky填充达图非零元的10-280倍，而本方法内存O(m)。对比FCL求解器，在匹配精度下快1.5-14倍。MNIST 10-NN上，p=3每类1标签时准确率64% vs p=2的36%。

Conclusion: 提出的阻尼弦牛顿延拓法有效解决了p-Laplacian半监督学习在大规模图上的计算瓶颈，每步线性化系统保持良态，可兼容近线性Laplacian求解器，在计算效率、内存开销和分类精度上均显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Graph-based semi-supervised learning (SSL) propagates a few labels over a similarity graph by minimizing a Dirichlet-type energy. The standard quadratic ($p=2$) energy reduces to a single graph-Laplacian solve, but it degenerates exactly where SSL is most useful when labels are scarce: gathering more unlabeled data drives the $p=2$ estimate to a near-constant function whenever $d\ge2$ (Nadler-Srebro-Zhou). Well-posedness requires the nonlinear $p$-Laplacian energy with $p>d$. Existing solvers reduce this to a sequence of weighted Laplacian solves, but their reference implementations use a direct sparse factorization or ichol-preconditioned CG instead. Plugging a near-linear Laplacian solver is not straightforward: at large $p$ the conductance weights degenerate near flat-gradient edges, making the system nearly singular and causing stagnation without a damped outer iteration. We close this gap. Recasting $p$-Laplacian SSL as a source-form nonlinear Laplacian flow $Bρ_p(B^\top x)=b$ and solving by damped chord-Newton continuation in $p$, every linearized system stays well-conditioned and can be delegated to a near-linear Laplacian engine. On size-scaled graph families the wall-clock is empirically $m^{0.96}$-$m^{1.02}$ per family (approximate Cholesky default), and a pooled fit across 228 SuiteSparse graphs gives $m^{1.19}$ vs.\ $m^{1.45}$ for direct factorization; the solver handles a $6.8\times10^7$-edge social network in minutes. Memory is the binding constraint: Cholesky fill reaches $10$-$280\times$ the graph nonzeros vs.\ our $O(m)$ hierarchy. Against the released FCL solver we are $1.5$-$14\times$ faster at matched accuracy. On MNIST $10$-NN, $p=3$ scores $64\%$ at one label per class vs.\ $36\%$ for $p=2$. Code: https://github.com/orenlivne/np.

</details>


### [195] [Mixture-of-Gaussians-Guided Schedule Design for Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03517)
*Ron Levi,Michael Elad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对布朗桥扩散模型（BBDM）的调度方案进行了理论分析，基于混合高斯先验导出了封闭形式的理想后验和MMSE去噪器，提出了两种互补的调度设计准则（Wasserstein和MSE），揭示了感知质量与重建保真度之间的固有权衡，并在合成数据和真实图像恢复任务上验证了理论有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BBDM的调度方案通常基于启发式方法选择，缺乏原则性的理论分析框架来指导调度设计。

Method: 在混合高斯（MoG）先验下分析BBDM反向动力学，得到封闭形式的理想后验和MMSE去噪器；通过可处理代理模型解析刻画BBDM诱导的重建律；据此提出Wasserstein准则（优化感知质量）和MSE准则（优化重建保真度）两种互补的调度设计目标。

Result: 证明了感知质量与重建保真度之间存在固有权衡；证明了存在通用的调度方案，该方案独立于退化类型和先验分布；在受控MoG设置上理论与实验完全一致；在FFHQ数据集上的图像修复、去模糊和超分辨率任务中验证了所提调度准则的实用价值。

Conclusion: 建立了BBDM调度设计的理论分析框架，揭示了调度设计中的感知-保真度权衡，并为实际应用提供了可操作的调度准则。

Abstract: Brownian Bridge Diffusion Models (BBDM) offer an appealing framework for image restoration and inverse problems by constructing a stochastic bridge from the clean signal directly to the degraded observation, rather than to pure noise. Despite their promise, the choice of bridge schedule is typically inherited from heuristics, and a principled analytical framework for schedule design has been lacking. In this work, we develop such a framework by offering a novel analysis of BBDM reverse dynamics under a Mixture-of-Gaussians (MoG) prior. This setting yields a closed-form ideal posterior and a corresponding MMSE denoiser, while the BBDM-induced reconstruction law is captured analytically through a tractable surrogate. Building on these expressions, we formulate two complementary schedule-design objectives: a Wasserstein criterion targeting perceptual quality and an MSE criterion targeting reconstruction fidelity. Our work exposes an inherent tradeoff between the two and proves the existence of universal schedules for both that are independent of the degradation and prior. Extensive experiments on controlled MoG settings confirm full alignment between theory and practice, and experiments on the FFHQ dataset across inpainting, deblurring, and super-resolution tasks validate the practical value of our schedule-design criteria.

</details>


### [196] [On the Convergence of Adam, Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03519)
*Steven Heilman,Sampad Mohanty*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明，对于任意动量衰减参数β₁,β₂∈[0,1)，投影Adam在在线优化中可能存在非零的平均遗憾。该结果扩展了Reddi等人2018年的结论（需β₁<√β₂），通过采用斜率为c,-1,-1的三周期线性函数序列（c略大于2），将非零平均遗憾结果推广至AdamW、RMSProp、NAdam、Adan、AdaMax、Muon等变体及i.i.d.变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Reddi等人2018年对Adam优化器遗憾界分析的局限性（需β₁<√β₂条件），证明在更一般的动量衰减参数设置下，Adam及其变体仍可能无法保证收敛到零平均遗憾。

Method: 采用三周期线性函数序列（斜率c,-1,-1）构造反例，其中c略大于2；将分析扩展至AdamW、RMSProp、NAdam、Adan、AdaMax、Muon等变体以及i.i.d.斜率序列变体。

Result: 证明在任意β₁,β₂∈[0,1)下，投影Adam的平均遗憾可以非零，且该结果适用于多种Adam变体及i.i.d.场景。

Conclusion: Adam类优化器在一般参数设置下可能存在非零平均遗憾，其收敛性保证具有更严格的限制条件。

Abstract: We show that projected Adam for online optimization with arbitrary moment decay parameters $β_1,β_2\in[0,1)$ can have average regret bounded away from zero. A similar result of Reddi-Kale-Kumar from 2018 required $β_1<\sqrt{β_2}$. Similar to their result, we use a three-periodic sequence of linear functions on $[-1,1]$ with slopes $c,-1,-1$, though we use $c$ slightly larger than $2$. This nonzero average regret result extends to Adam variants such as AdamW, RMSProp, NAdam, Adan, AdaMax, Muon, and to an i.i.d. variant of the three-periodic sequence of slopes for Adam.

</details>


### [197] [Co-Adaptive Multi-Task LoRA: Transfer-Aware, Label-Free Control of Domain Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03522)
*Wei Zhang,Lin Tang,Ming Zhao,Yuxuan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CoDA是一种面向多任务LoRA微调的自适应控制器，通过无标签探针计算每个域的能力信号和跨域亲和度，动态调整各域的训练参与度（损失权重+采样比例），在异构多域场景下优于均匀混合、梯度手术等方法，且仅需一半数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效微调管线在多域联合训练时默认使用固定的均匀混合策略，忽略了域间正向迁移与负向干扰的问题——每个域应该参与多少训练、哪些域适合共同训练这两个关键问题没有得到解答。

Method: 提出CoDA共适应控制器：1) 对共享LoRA适配器在无标签探针上前向传播，得到每个域的能力信号（水平跟踪剩余空间，轨迹跟踪学习速度）；2) 探针表示的漂移量计算有符号的跨域亲和度，预测两两迁移效果；3) 求解一个熵正则化二次规划问题，为每个域设定参与度（联合损失权重和数据采样比例），奖励高剩余空间、仍在学习、互促的域，抑制干扰的域。

Result: 在五个异构域和两个骨干模型上，CoDA相比均匀混合、学习混合、梯度手术多任务优化器和在线数据选择方法，平均性能更优，且仅使用一半数据，同时降低了跨域梯度冲突。

Conclusion: 理论证明能力信号跟踪域风险、参与度规划存在唯一不动点（由压缩映射达到）、其解实现了迁移感知的水填充；实验、消融和控制实验支持了各项主张。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a single low-rank adapter on many domains at once is multi-task learning: the domains must be co-learned, and how they share the adapter decides whether they help or hurt one another. Most efficient fine-tuning pipelines ignore this and train on a fixed, uniform mixture, leaving two coupled questions unanswered: how much should each domain participate, and which domains should be co-trained given that some transfer positively and others interfere? We show that both answers can be read off cheaply and without labels. A forward pass of the current shared adapter over a small unlabeled probe yields, per domain, a competence signal whose level tracks remaining headroom and whose trajectory tracks learning speed; the drift of these probe representations yields a signed cross-domain affinity that predicts pairwise transfer. We fold both into CoDA, a co-adaptive controller that solves a small entropy-regularized quadratic program on the simplex to set each domain's participation -- jointly its loss weight and its share of the sampled data -- rewarding high-headroom, still-learning, mutually synergistic domains and damping interfering ones. The controller is forward-only, adds no trainable parameters, and wraps any multi-task LoRA pipeline. Across five heterogeneous domains and two backbones, CoDA improves the average over uniform mixing, learned mixtures, gradient-surgery multi-task optimizers, and online data selection while using half the data, and lowers cross-domain gradient conflict. We prove that the competence signal tracks domain risk, that the participation program has a unique fixed point reached by a contraction, and that its solution performs transfer-aware water-filling; analysis, ablations, and controls corroborate each claim.

</details>


### [198] [Aligning Language Models with Selective Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03528)
*Gaoxiang Luo,Yifan Wu,Sinian Zhang,Aryan Deshwal,Ju Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RLSR框架，通过对齐选择性预测性能指标（AURC）来优化大语言模型在后训练对齐阶段的风险-覆盖率权衡，实现更可靠的人机协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在关键决策场景中可靠性不足，现有对齐方法主要关注正确性或校准指标，忽视了选择性预测（风险-覆盖率权衡）这一提升可靠性的重要策略。

Method: 提出RLSR（Reinforcement Learning for Selection Reward）框架，以AURC（风险-覆盖率曲线下面积）作为对齐目标，通过强化学习优化LLM的选择性预测性能。

Result: 在域内和域外任务上，RLSR相比多种对齐基线方法取得了显著更好的风险-覆盖率权衡效果。

Conclusion: 将选择性预测指标纳入LLM后训练对齐阶段是提升模型可靠性的有效途径，RLSR框架能够实现更优的可靠性-覆盖率平衡，促进人机无缝协作。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as critical decision-making components in high-stakes real-world AI systems, rendering LLM reliability a foremost practical concern. In this paper, we focus on enhancing LLM reliability through selective prediction (SP), a strategy that allows an LLM to only predict for inputs where it is likely to be correct (i.e., coverage) and hence reduce the error rate (i.e., risk) on that portion of inputs -- flagging the remaining inputs for future human discretion. In other words, SP improves LLM reliability by balancing the risk-coverage trade-off and enabling seamless human-AI collaboration. To integrate SP into LLMs, we focus on the LLM post-training alignment stage and propose to align LLMs with SP performance metrics, in contrast with existing LLM alignment methods that focus primarily on correctness or calibration metrics. Specifically, we propose a novel alignment framework, Reinforcement Learning for Selection Reward (RLSR), which targets the area under the risk-coverage curve (AURC) -- a popular SP performance metric -- as its alignment objective. RLSR achieves substantially better risk-coverage trade-off compared to multiple alignment baselines on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks.

</details>


### [199] [WeightCLIP: Aligning Datasets and Models for Weight Space Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03551)
*Aron Asefaw,Konstantinos Tzevelekakis,Damian Falk,Léo Meynent,Damian Borth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: WeightCLIP提出了一种通过对比学习对齐神经网络权重表示与对应数据集信息的方法，利用自编码器编码网络权重、数据集编码器编码数据样本，通过对比目标实现两种表示的语义对齐，从而在检索、生成和微调等下游任务中取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有权重空间学习方法虽然能学习神经网络权重的表示，但无法将训练模型所用的数据集信息融入这些表示中，限制了下游应用的能力。

Method: 提出WeightCLIP方法：使用自编码器将神经网络编码为潜在表示，同时使用数据集编码器编码数据集样本，通过对比学习目标对齐两种表示空间，从而将数据集信息诱导到权重空间表示中。

Result: 该方法可应用于多种下游任务，包括将数据集信息映射为权重空间表示并解码生成强模型；引入的潜在表示精炼过程优于标准微调；在检索、生成和精炼任务上均取得改进。

Conclusion: 明确地将数据集信息融入权重空间表示能够显著改善基于权重表示的各种下游任务性能。

Abstract: Weight space learning aims to learn representations of neural network (NN) weights, enabling different downstream tasks. Existing approaches show promising performance, but lacking a way to shape these weight-space representations using information about the datasets the models were trained on, thus limiting downstream applications. We propose WeightCLIP, a method for learning a dataset-aligned latent space for neural networks, where datasets information is induced during training. The NNs are encoded as latent representations using an autoencoder, while dataset samples are encoded using a dataset encoder. The two representations are aligned using a contrastive objective, effectively reshaping the weight-space representations according to the datasets. We demonstrate that such representations can be used for different downstream tasks, including mapping dataset information to a weight-space representation that decode to strong models. In addition, we introduce a latent refinement process for generating models that outperforms standard fine-tuning. Overall, our results demonstrate that explicitly incorporating dataset information improves what can be achieved with weight-space representations across retrieval, generation, and refinement. Code will be available at https://github.com/HSG-AIML/WeightCLIP.

</details>


### [200] [Teacher Supervision over Representation Equivalence Classes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03572)
*Sang Il Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出知识蒸馏应匹配教师模型的等价类（正交各向同性缩放变换下的不变性）而非具体特征，因为预训练表示仅在等价类意义下可识别。实验证明，只有匹配输出函数（logits）才能恢复能力，而匹配隐藏表示只能对齐几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识蒸馏方法默认教师表示具有可匹配的绝对坐标，但预训练表示仅在正交各向同性缩放变换的等价类下可识别，导致绝对特征匹配不适定，需要建立统一的几何框架来理解蒸馏目标的有效性。

Method: 通过群作用商空间的视角，将教师输出函数定义为类不变量的因式分解；提出匹配类不变量（Gram结构、CKA、主子空间）或先对齐坐标再匹配的统一框架；在Qwen2.5和Llama-3.1上通过恢复实验和嫁接实验进行验证。

Result: 恢复实验表明匹配隐藏表示可恢复CKA~0.99的几何对齐但无法恢复模型能力；消融实验定位原因：logit匹配驱动能力恢复；恢复仅限于语料覆盖区域；嫁接实验验证边界重叠是移植成功的必要条件但不充分。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏应聚焦于输出函数匹配而非隐藏特征匹配，统一了特征匹配、关系蒸馏、对齐和嫁接的几何解释；恢复的局限性表明模型能力受限于训练数据覆盖范围。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation is usually framed as a choice of what to match in the teacher - its logits, hidden features, or sample relations - which presupposes that the teacher's representation has absolute coordinates to match. It does not: a pretrained representation is identifiable only up to an orthogonal-and-isotropic-scaling equivalence class, so a student should learn the teacher's equivalence class, not its features. The organizing fact is that capability is the teacher's output function, a class invariant that factors through the quotient by the class action, so an objective recovers capability exactly when it is defined there. This makes absolute feature matching ill-posed, and admissible supervision a matter of targeting class invariants (Gram structure, CKA, principal subspaces) or aligning coordinates first, unifying feature matching, relational distillation, alignment, and grafting in one geometric account. We validate our framework on Qwen2.5 and Llama-3.1. A restoration study recovers a corrupted model's representation (CKA ~ 0.99) but not its capability, and an ablation isolates the cause: output-function (logit) matching drives capability, while matching hidden representations aligns geometry without restoring function. Recovery is confined to the corpus-covered region, and a graft study confirms that boundary overlap predicts transplant success but is necessary, not sufficient.

</details>


### [201] [Differentiate the Evaluator, Not the Program: An Efficient Runtime Representation for Neuro-Symbolic Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03574)
*Lucas Sheneman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NDVM 是一种原生可微分虚拟机运行时，通过将符号结构与可微数值状态分离，实现程序-参数协同搜索中内循环梯度优化的高效批量化执行，相比传统方法达到约 24 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 AI 系统生成可执行科学模型的场景中，参数校准成为程序-参数协同搜索的瓶颈：外循环生成大量候选程序，每个都需要内循环梯度优化才能评估。现有方法中，将每个候选编译为独立可微图牺牲了"程序即数据"特性，而解释器方法则因解释器开销主导计算而效率低下。

Method: NDVM 将符号结构（标签、符号、环境、控制流）保留为原生运行时数据，而将数值载荷组织为密集批处理缓冲区并沿执行迹记录精确反向模式梯度，使得一次解释器遍历即可在大量参数向量上分摊开销。设计基于真实可微自托管 Scheme 解释器的锁定成本模型。

Result: 与参考后端相比，NDVM 在正向和梯度计算上保持等价性，每通道批处理分摊约 60 倍，具有近线性的多核扩展能力，支持两个独立前端。在固定预算的 LLM 提议程序协同搜索中，NDVM 在挂钟时间上约 24 倍更快达到高质量解。

Conclusion: 运行时微分技术可作为科学发现工作流的实用系统基础，NDVM 有效解决了程序-参数协同搜索中内循环优化的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: AI systems increasingly propose executable scientific models whose value depends on both their symbolic structure and their fitted continuous parameters. This makes parameter calibration the bottleneck of program-and-parameter co-search: an outer loop can generate thousands of candidate programs, but each needs an inner gradient-based optimization before it can be assessed. Staging each candidate into its own differentiable graph makes individual models fast but sacrifices the program-as-data property that keeps search fluid; interpreter-based approaches preserve programs as runtime data but pay interpreter overhead that dominates the numerical work.
  We present the Native Differentiable Virtual Machine (NDVM), a runtime representation that differentiates executable programs without compiling each candidate into a separate graph. NDVM separates symbolic structure from differentiable numeric state: tags, symbols, environments, and control remain native runtime data, while numeric payloads live in dense batched buffers with exact reverse-mode gradients recorded along the realized execution trace, so one evaluator walk is amortized across large populations of parameter vectors. A locked cost model of a real differentiable self-hosted Scheme interpreter motivates the design. We realize NDVM as a native runtime with forward and gradient equivalence to the reference backend, about 60x per-lane batch amortization, near-linear multicore scaling, and two independent front ends. In fixed-budget co-search over LLM-proposed programs, NDVM reaches high-quality solutions about 24x sooner in wall-clock time, suggesting runtime differentiation as a practical systems foundation for scientific discovery workflows.

</details>


### [202] [When Geometry Aligns: Dihedral Hidden-State Transformations in UNet, ViT, and DiT Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03580)
*Mojtaba Faramarzi,Alex Lamb,Irina Rish*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过统一框架研究扩散架构（CNN-UNet和Transformer-based DiT）中隐藏状态的几何扰动效应，发现保持几何一致性（如反射变换）能提升特征稳定性，而不一致变换会导致可预测的结构性失败。基于Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net、ViT和DiT的实验验证了几何一致性是空间结构化视觉模型中隐藏状态干预的关键原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散架构（包括卷积UNet和基于Transformer的DiT）中结构化的几何扰动对模型内部表示的影响尚不明确，本文旨在系统研究这一问题。

Method: 提出统一框架，将二面体群的反射基元应用于中间隐藏状态作为受控内部干预，通过自洽偏移（SCS）、激活质量散射（AMS）和漂移（Drift）等激活级诊断指标分析特征稳定性与几何漂移。在Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net上评估七种干预模式（三种种子），并辅以图像级FID、KID、CLIP分数和LPIPS多样性指标，同时进行ViT和受控DiT补充分析。

Result: 一致变换提升稳定性，不一致变换诱发可预测的架构特定失效模式。

Conclusion: 几何一致性是空间结构化视觉模型和扩散模型中稳定隐藏状态干预的关键原则。

Abstract: Diffusion architectures now encompass convolutional UNets as well as transformer-based designs such as Diffusion Transformers (DiTs), inspired by Vision Transformers (ViTs), yet the effects of structured geometric perturbations within these architectures remain poorly understood. We study this question through a unified framework that applies reflection-based elements of the dihedral group to intermediate hidden states as controlled internal interventions, contrasting geometrically consistent and inconsistent variants. Using activation-level diagnostics, including Self-Consistency Shift (SCS), Activation Mass Scatter (AMS), and Drift, we analyze feature stability and geometric drift. We find that consistent transformations improve stability, while inconsistent ones induce predictable, architecture-specific failures. In the main Stable Diffusion 2.1 U-Net study, we evaluate seven intervention modes over three seeds and complement the internal diagnostics with image-level FID, KID, CLIP score, and LPIPS diversity. Taken together with supporting ViT and controlled DiT analyses, these results establish geometric consistency as a key principle for stable hidden-state interventions in spatially structured vision and diffusion models.

</details>


### [203] [Modular Foundation Models for Time-Series Perception in Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03585)
*Quang Hung Pham,Ryad Zemouri,Martin Gagnon,Luc Vouligny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于预训练表示编码器集合的模块化时间序列基础模型，通过自监督学习、门控机制和Transformer自注意力聚合，实现跨PHM任务的通用感知，在ETT基准和工业水轮发电机虚拟传感案例中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列感知方法多为任务特化、数据饥渴且难以集成到可扩展的监控与决策管线中；纯数据驱动模型在变工况下缺乏鲁棒性和可迁移性。

Method: 采用模块化架构，包含一组预训练表示编码器；利用自监督学习在异构数据集上学习可迁移、任务无关的表示；引入门控机制动态选择相关编码器；通过Transformer自注意力模块建模跨编码器交互，将选中的表示投影到共享潜在空间并聚合；下游任务仅需轻量级任务头，预训练编码器保持冻结。

Result: 消融实验验证了自监督预训练、编码器选择、表示对齐和自适应聚合的互补作用；在ETT基准上跨任务表现具有竞争力；工业案例（水轮发电机转子温度虚拟传感）验证了实际应用价值。

Conclusion: 模块化基础模型架构能够有效解决时间序列感知中的通用性和可迁移性问题，支持插补、长期预测和少样本学习等多种下游任务，具有良好的工业实用性。

Abstract: Engineering Digital Twins and Prognostics and Health Management (PHM) systems rely on robust perception modules to extract actionable information from heterogeneous and non-stationary time-series data. However, most existing approaches remain task-specific, data-hungry, and difficult to integrate into scalable monitoring and decision-making pipelines. Moreover, purely data-driven models often lack robustness and transferability across varying operating conditions. To address these challenges, this paper proposes a modular foundation model for time-series perception based on a collection of pretrained representation encoders. The framework leverages self-supervised learning on heterogeneous datasets to learn transferable and task-agnostic representations, which can be reused across multiple PHM tasks. A gating mechanism is introduced to dynamically select relevant encoders for a given target dataset, enabling conditional computation and adaptive model composition. The selected representations are projected into a shared latent space and aggregated using a Transformer-based self-attention module that explicitly models cross-encoder interactions. The resulting architecture supports multiple downstream tasks, including imputation, long-term forecasting, and few-shot learning, through lightweight task-specific heads, while keeping pretrained encoders frozen during adaptation. Extensive ablation studies demonstrate the complementary roles of self-supervised pretraining, encoder selection, representation alignment, and adaptive aggregation. Experimental results on the ETT benchmark show competitive performance across tasks, while a real-world industrial case study on virtual sensing for hydro-generator rotor temperature highlights the practical relevance of the approach.

</details>


### [204] [Graph Classification via Network Usable Information: From Representation Evaluation to Structure Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03587)
*Abdullah Shaik,Anwar Said*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NetinfoGC 提出了一种基于网络可用信息（NUI）范式的图分类框架，利用传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）构建排列不变的图表示，并通过无训练NUI估计和稀疏组LASSO正则化实现高效的特征选择与评估，实验表明经典中心性度量与学习型表示竞争力相当，且NUI与分类准确率高度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图神经网络方法依赖端到端训练的黑盒嵌入，缺乏可解释性和对表示质量的直接评估手段。本文旨在提出一个统一、可解释且无需端到端神经训练的图分类框架，同时建立一种不依赖监督学习的表示质量评估指标。

Method: 1) 构建基于传播机制和经典结构描述符（如图中心性度量）的排列不变图表示族；2) 提出基于聚类一致性的无训练NUI估计方法，用真实标签评估表示质量；3) 采用稀疏组LASSO正则化自动选择信息性结构描述符并抑制冗余特征。

Result: 在基准数据集上，经典中心性度量与学习型传播表示竞争力相当，甚至在某些情况下表现更优。NUI估计值与下游分类准确率之间存在强相关性，验证了NUI作为表示效用度量的有效性。

Conclusion: NetinfoGC 提供了一个统一且可解释的图表示评估与利用框架，无需端到端神经训练即可达到具有竞争力的分类性能，且NUI能够有效预测表示的任务相关性。

Abstract: We propose NetinfoGC, a framework for graph classification that extends the Network Usable Information (NUI) paradigm to graph-level learning. Unlike conventional graph neural network approaches that rely on end-to-end training of black-box embeddings, NetinfoGC constructs a family of permutation-invariant graph representations derived from propagation-based mechanisms and classical structural descriptors, including graph centrality measures.
  To evaluate representation quality, we introduce a training-free NUI estimation procedure based on clustering consistency with ground-truth labels, providing a proxy for task-relevant information without supervised learning. We further exploit the same representations using sparse-group LASSO regularization, enabling automatic selection of informative structural descriptors while suppressing redundant ones.
  Experiments on benchmark datasets show that classical centrality measures are highly competitive with learned propagation-based representations, and in several cases yield superior performance. Moreover, we observe a strong correlation between estimated NUI and downstream classification accuracy, validating NUI as an effective measure of representation utility.
  Overall, NetinfoGC provides a unified and interpretable framework for evaluating and exploiting graph representations without requiring end-to-end neural training.

</details>


### [205] [Implicit Bias of SGD in Multivariate ReLU Networks: Effective Width Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03613)
*Shuang Liang,Tom Jacobs,Guido Montúfar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了噪声随机梯度下降在训练宽两层ReLU网络进行多元回归时的隐式偏差。在平均场机制下，训练动力学由Wasserstein梯度流近似，收敛到唯一平稳测度。尽管网络无限过参数化，学习到的预测器却具有有限表示：输入权重和偏置沿有限方向对齐，导致有效宽度坍缩。函数是连续分段仿射的，其仿射区域由有限超平面排列的单元格决定。学习方向数（即超平面数）以2𝒫-1为上界，其中𝒫是训练输入上可实现线性二分的数量。学习到的每个方向在训练数据上诱导唯一的三元激活模式，因此学习预测器的复杂度受训练数据组合几何的控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解噪声SGD在训练过参数化神经网络时的隐式偏差，特别是网络最终预测器的结构复杂度如何被数据本身的组合几何所约束，从而解释神经网络的泛化能力。

Method: 采用平均场视角，将训练动力学近似为Wasserstein梯度流，分析其收敛到唯一平稳测度的过程。通过研究平稳测度的结构，推导出学习到的权重和偏置的有限方向对齐性质，以及由此产生的分段仿射函数表示。

Result: 1) 网络无限过参数化但有效宽度坍缩，预测器由有限方向表征。2) 学习方向数≤2𝒫-1，𝒫是训练数据上可实现线性二分数。3) 每个学习方向在训练数据上诱导唯一的三元激活模式，保证非冗余性。4) 预测器复杂度由训练数据的组合几何决定。

Conclusion: 噪声SGD的隐式偏差导致网络自动学习一个复杂度受限于数据几何结构的简洁表示，为理解过参数化神经网络的泛化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study the implicit bias of noisy stochastic gradient descent in training wide two-layer ReLU networks for multivariate regression. In a mean-field regime, the training dynamics are approximated by a Wasserstein gradient flow that converges to a unique stationary measure. We characterize the structure of this stationary measure and the predictor it represents. We show that, despite the network being infinitely overparameterized, the learned predictor admits an effectively finite representation: the input weights and biases align along finitely many directions, leading to an effective width collapse. In particular, the solution function is continuous piecewise affine, with affine regions determined by the cells of a finite hyperplane arrangement. The number of learned directions, and hence hyperplanes, is bounded above by $2\mathcal{P}-1$, where $\mathcal{P}$ denotes the number of linear dichotomies realizable on the training inputs. We further establish a non-redundancy property of the learned representation by proving that each learned direction induces a unique ternary activation pattern on the training data. Consequently, the complexity of the learned predictor is governed by the combinatorial geometry of the training data.

</details>


### [206] [Reflected Schrödinger Bridge Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03626)
*Marcus Häggbom,Viktor Nilsson,Pierre Nyquist,Joakim andén*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种部分免模拟的反射薛定谔桥训练框架，利用类似流匹配的新采样方法和回归目标，在保持或略优生成性能的同时，训练和推理的额外时间开销可忽略不计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射薛定谔桥方法依赖前向-后向SDE理论，需要昂贵的高阶导数和完整路径采样，训练复杂度高。本文旨在开发一种部分免模拟的反射SB训练框架，使其能像流匹配一样高效训练。

Method: 提出新型采样方法和回归目标，使得反射SB的训练方式类似于流匹配，实现部分免模拟训练；通过耦合高维图像数据集进行验证。

Result: 反射动力学在训练和推理阶段仅增加可忽略的额外计算时间，同时保持或略微提升生成性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的部分免模拟框架使反射SB能够高效训练，且反射动力学作为模型选择具有内置的样本域内保真度保证，是一种实用且高性能的生成建模方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative modeling have enabled the efficient computation of Schrödinger bridges (SB) in high-dimensional settings by leveraging partially simulation-free training methods inspired by flow matching. However, these have not covered SBs with reflecting dynamics, a useful model choice with built-in guarantees that generated samples stay in the data domain. Existing alternatives for reflected SBs instead rely on more complex training based on forward--backward SDE theory, requiring expensive higher-order derivatives and sampling entire paths during training. In this article, we introduce a partially simulation-free framework that allows reflected SBs to be trained similarly to flow matching, using a new sampling method and regression target. We demonstrate our results by coupling pairs of well-known high-dimensional image datasets. Using reflected dynamics incurs negligible additional wall-clock time during both training and inference while maintaining or slightly improving generative performance.

</details>


### [207] [LLM-Guided Transportation Hub Capacity Planning with Textual Business Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03651)
*Xiaoyue Liu,Zheng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合LLM代理和优化模型的枢纽容量规划框架，通过链式思维推理将商业上下文（自然语言描述）转化为容量决策，在真实货运网络中实现了2.8%的最优性差距，显著优于传统优化模型的11.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统枢纽容量规划模型能有效优化定量输入，但无法消化定性的商业上下文信息（如自然语言描述的业务背景）。

Method: 1) LLM代理通过链式思维推理将每个上下文项映射到具体的容量调整（方向与幅度），构建结构化决策表；2) 新容量决策通过优化模型反馈循环验证，优化模型提供基于路径的绩效指标指导代理选择。

Result: 在美国东南部13枢纽货运网络上，该方法达到2.8%的最优性差距，而传统优化模型（无文本输入）的差距为11.0%。

Conclusion: LLM可以作为上下文桥梁，将定性商业洞察整合到运筹学工作流中，显著提升容量规划决策质量。

Abstract: While traditional hub capacity planning models optimize effectively for quantitative inputs, they often fail to digest qualitative business context. We propose a novel framework where a large language model (LLM) agent iteratively proposes hub capacity decisions guided by natural-language business context descriptions. The key mechanism is a chain-of-thought reasoning protocol: the LLM constructs a structured decision table that maps each contextual item to specific capacity adjustments based on the implied direction and magnitude of changes. The new capacity decision is then validated through a feedback loop with an optimization model, which provides routing-based performance metrics to guide the agent's selection. On a real-world 13-hub freight network in the southeastern US, our framework achieves a 2.8% optimality gap relative to the hidden ground-truth, a significant improvement over the 11.0% gap produced by the traditional optimization model without textual business inputs. This demonstrates that LLMs can serve as a contextual bridge, integrating qualitative business insights into Operations Research workflows.

</details>


### [208] [Phase-Preserving Trimodal Transformer for Tropical Forest Biomass Estimation Using Optical and PolInSAR Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03663)
*Luiz Felipe Parente Santiago,Rosiane Rodrigues de Freitas,Daniel Rodrigues dos Santos,Felipe Ferrari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为TCCT的物理信息深度学习架构，通过融合Landsat-5光学数据与P波段和L波段的极化SAR干涉（PolInSAR）复值数据，结合复值编码器和动态协同注意力机制克服热带成熟林AGB估计中SAR信号饱和与云覆盖问题。在巴西亚马逊Paracou区域，该方法实现了CHM的RMSE为3.78米、R²为0.33，AGB相对RMSE为4.51%，满足ESA BIOMASS任务小于20%的误差要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热带成熟林AGB准确估计面临两大物理局限性：SAR信号在高密度区域饱和以及光学影像受持续云覆盖影响。现有方法无法有效融合多模态数据克服这些限制。

Method: 提出了三模态协同共注意力Transformer（TCCT），核心创新包括：(1) 复值编码器保持空间相位相干性；(2) 动态协同注意力机制作为自适应门控模块，降低云污染光学像素权重，转向微波相位数据；(3) 通过Levenberg-Marquardt优化导出局部空间异速生长校准模型，针对Paracou区域木材密度定制。采用两阶段评估协议：5折交叉验证建立全局权重，随后进行200轮局部空间微调。

Result: 全局5折交叉验证RMSE为4.19米；局部微调后CHM的RMSE达3.78米、R²为0.33，优于随机森林、CNN和Vision Transformer基线。消融实验证实保持相位相干性可缓解深冠层信号饱和。AGB转换后在>50 Mg/ha密林区域相对RMSE为4.51%。

Conclusion: TCCT满足ESA BIOMASS任务小于20%的误差要求，为热带生物群落连续碳储量制图提供了鲁棒框架。

Abstract: The accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) in mature tropical forests remains a critical challenge in remote sensing, primarily due to the saturation of Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) signals in high-density areas and persistent cloud cover affecting optical imagery. To overcome these physical limitations, we propose the Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT), a physics-informed deep learning architecture. The TCCT natively fuses optical surface reflectance (Landsat-5) with complex-valued Polarimetric SAR Interferometry (PolInSAR) data from both P and L bands. Unlike traditional fusion methods, our architecture employs complex-valued encoders to preserve spatial phase coherence, coupled with a dynamic co-attention mechanism that acts as an adaptive gating module, reducing the weight of cloud-corrupted optical pixels and shifting reliance to microwave phase data. We also derived a localized spatial allometric calibration model via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization, tailored to the specific wood density of the Paracou region in the Amazon basin. Evaluated using a two-stage protocol, the TCCT first underwent a rigorous 5-fold cross-validation to establish robust global weights (achieving a global RMSE of 4.19 m). Subsequently, following a localized spatial fine-tuning phase over 200 epochs, the model attained an absolute RMSE of 3.78 m and an $R^2$ of 0.33 for Canopy Height Models (CHM), outperforming standard Random Forest, CNN, and Vision Transformer baselines. Our ablation study confirms that preserving phase coherence mitigates deep-canopy signal saturation. When converted to AGB, the fine-tuned TCCT map yielded a Relative RMSE (rRMSE) of 4.51% in dense forest areas above 50 Mg/ha. By meeting the European Space Agency (ESA) BIOMASS mission requirement of less than 20% error, the TCCT provides a robust framework for continuous carbon stock mapping in tropical biomes.

</details>


### [209] [A Structural Interpretation of GELU and Threshold-Transmission Activations via the First-Order Loss Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03664)
*Roberto Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从高斯互补一阶损失函数的角度重新解释了GELU激活函数，将其视为硬线性门控在随机高斯阈值下的期望盈余信号传输项，并推广到包含ReLU、GELU、SiLU/Swish和hard swish的阈值-传输族。均匀阈值情况恢复了一个类似hard-swish的紧凑分段多项式门控，其阈值宽度参数可固定或学习。在紧凑视觉和语言模型上的实验表明，该门控与GELU等相比具有竞争力，在多数设置中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GELU激活函数通常被解释为输入相关随机伯努利门控的期望输出，但缺乏从损失函数视角的互补理解。本文旨在提供基于高斯损失函数的全新解释，并构建统一的阈值-传输激活函数家族。

Method: 将GELU重新解释为硬线性门控在服从高斯分布随机阈值下的期望盈余信号传输项，利用高斯互补一阶损失函数进行形式化；推广到包含ReLU、GELU、SiLU/Swish和hard swish的阈值-传输家族；均匀阈值情况推导出类似hard-swish的紧凑分段多项式门控，引入可显式调节的阈值宽度参数，并设计了固定宽度和学习宽度两种变体。

Result: 在紧凑视觉和语言模型上的受控实验表明，经过校准或学习得到的均匀阈值门控在大多数测试设置中与GELU、ReLU和SiLU/Swish相比具有竞争力或表现更优，并且模型在有限过渡区域的使用是非平凡的。

Conclusion: 从损失函数视角为GELU提供了互补的理论解释，统一了多种常用激活函数，提出的均匀阈值门控变体在实验中表现优异，验证了该理论框架的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: The Gaussian Error Linear Unit is usually motivated as the expected output of an input-dependent stochastic Bernoulli gate. This work gives a complementary interpretation based on the Gaussian complementary first-order loss function: GELU is the signal-transmission term of the expected surplus of a hard linear gate with a Gaussian random threshold. This view separates loss accounting from forward signal transmission and generalises to a threshold-transmission family that includes ReLU, GELU, SiLU/Swish, and hard swish as special cases. The uniform-threshold case recovers a hard-swish-like compact piecewise-polynomial gate with an explicit threshold-width parameter, yielding fixed- and learned-width variants. Controlled experiments on compact vision and language models show that calibrated or learned uniform-threshold gates are consistently competitive with GELU, ReLU, and SiLU/Swish, improve over them in most tested settings, and use the finite transition region nontrivially.

</details>


### [210] [Validation-Induced Shapley Shifts: How Validation Structure Distorts Data Valuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03675)
*Yinan Shen,Ziao Yang,Hongfu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示Shapley值在数据估值中存在的系统性脆弱性：验证集的微小扰动（如添加噪声）会导致Shapley值分布发生方向性偏移，训练样本的估值向零压缩，动摇了Shapley稳定性的传统假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设训练数据和模型固定后Shapley值是稳定的，但本文发现该假设存在漏洞——验证集的微小变化即可导致Shapley分布的系统性偏移。

Method: 通过KNN-Shapley框架，在合成数据和真实数据上进行实验，揭示噪声诱导的邻域重排效应（扰动改变验证样本与训练样本之间的局部排序，导致估值景观平坦化）。

Result: 噪声添加导致训练样本的Shapley值一致地向零压缩，且这种偏移是可重复、一致的；验证集的微小扰动即可引发方向性分布变化。

Conclusion: 挑战了Shapley值的稳定性假设，揭示了数据估值中新的脆弱性维度，并提出归一化和边界感知验证策略以缓解失真，实现更鲁棒和可解释的机器学习市场估值。

Abstract: Shapley values are widely used to attribute value to training data based on their marginal contribution to performance on a validation set. Existing practice often assumes these values are stable once the training data and model are fixed. In this work, we uncover a systematic vulnerability: even modest changes to the validation set, such as introducing noises, cause directional shifts in Shapley distributions. As noises are added, Shapley values of training samples compress toward zero. We trace this to a noise-induced neighborhood reshuffling effect: perturbations alter the local rank order between validation and training samples, flattening the valuation landscape. Using the KNN-Shapley framework, we show through synthetic and real data that these shifts are consistent and reproducible. Our findings challenge the assumption of Shapley stability and reveal a new axis of fragility in data valuation. We propose normalization and boundary-aware validation strategies to mitigate these distortions and enable more robust, interpretable valuation in machine learning marketplaces.

</details>


### [211] [Rethinking AI-Generated Text Detection: A Strong Baseline and the Distribution-Shift Problem That Remains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03680)
*Zhuoer Shen,Mingyi Wang,Shaofeng Zou,Yuheng Bu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从基线优先的角度重新审视AI生成文本检测领域，发现简单的全微调RoBERTa模型就能匹配甚至超越许多专门设计的检测器。真正的挑战在于分布迁移（主题领域或生成模型变化），此时强基线性能急剧下降。作者识别出关键失败模式：分布迁移下检测器会将高置信度的"机器生成"标签分配给未见领域的人类文本。随后研究了两种轻量级域适应方法（基于LoRA的MAML K-shot适应和置信度加权集成）来缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI生成文本检测工作往往引入新基准和专门检测器，但缺乏对基线模型的系统比较。作者质疑这种做法的有效性，试图验证简单的基线模型是否已经足够强大，并揭示真正的挑战所在。

Method: 1) 使用全微调的RoBERTa作为强基线，在多基准上对比专门检测器。2) 分析分布迁移场景下的性能退化，识别失败模式。3) 提出两种轻量级域适应方法：基于LoRA的MAML K-shot适应和置信度加权集成方法。

Result: RoBERTa基线在分布内检测中匹配或超越专门检测器；分布迁移下性能急剧下降，简单的增加训练数据无法解决；发现了"高置信度误分类"的失败模式；所提域适应方法能部分缓解该问题。

Conclusion: AI生成文本检测领域的进展应同时衡量分布内性能和分布迁移下的鲁棒性，而非仅关注新颖的架构设计。

Abstract: Recent AI-generated text detection work often introduces a new benchmark together with a specialized detector tailored to it. We revisit this practice from a baseline-first perspective. Across several benchmarks, we show that a plain, fully fine-tuned RoBERTa matches or exceeds the specialized detectors those benchmarks are built around. This suggests that much of the recent architectural complexity is not what drives strong in-distribution detection. The remaining challenge is the distribution shift. The same strong baseline degrades sharply when the topic domain or generating model changes at test time, and simply adding more source data does not close the gap. We identify a key failure mode: under distribution shift, the detector can assign high-confidence machine labels to human-written text from unseen domains. We then study two lightweight domain adaptation methods to address this problem: $K$-shot adaptation with first-order MAML over LoRA adapters, and a per-sample confidence-weighted ensemble built on top of the adapted detector. Overall, our results suggest that progress in AI-generated text detection should be measured not only by in-distribution performance, but also by robustness under distribution shift.

</details>


### [212] [PIEFS: Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03692)
*Varvara Nazarenkko,Timur Lidzhiev,Alexander Tarakanov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PIEFS框架，通过可学习度量矩阵（对角缩放+吉文斯旋转）对Dirichlet能量进行修正，在监督学习中训练具有谱归纳偏置的坐标映射，实现任务自适应的谱表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统谱方法依赖固定核或图拉普拉斯，缺乏任务自适应性，难以在监督学习中灵活利用数据的几何结构。

Method: 构建可学习度量A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)，其中Λ(x)控制各向异性缩放，U(x)通过结构化吉文斯旋转乘积参数化正交变换；在经验Gram正交性、监督线性读出和修正Dirichlet惩罚下联合训练标量坐标映射。

Result: 在合成、表格和图像基准上的实验验证了PIEFS相对于经典基线和NeuralEF的有效性，比较了恒等度规、对角度规和旋转-缩放度规的效果。

Conclusion: PIEFS是一种紧凑的监督谱表示方法，主要改进方向包括优化稳定性、显式算子特征值问题验证以及更丰富的度量参数化。

Abstract: Spectral methods are widely used to construct representations from the geometry of data, but they often rely on a fixed kernel, graph Laplacian, or manually selected feature scaling. We propose Physics-Informed Eigenfunction Features with Learnable Scaling (PIEFS), a supervised neural representation-learning framework with a spectral inductive bias, based on a modified Dirichlet energy. In PIEFS, scalar coordinate maps are trained under empirical Gram orthogonality, a supervised linear readout, and a Dirichlet penalty in which the input gradient is transformed by a learnable metric $A(x)=Λ(x)U(x)$. The diagonal factor $Λ(x)$ controls anisotropic scaling, while the orthogonal factor $U(x)$ is parameterized by a structured product of Givens rotations. This construction yields task-adaptive Dirichlet-regularized coordinates rather than eigenfunctions of a fixed supervision-independent operator. Experiments on synthetic, tabular, and image-based benchmarks study the effect of identity, diagonal, and rotation-scaling metrics, and compare the resulting coordinates with classical baselines and NeuralEF. The results support PIEFS as a compact supervised spectral representation method and identify optimization stability, validation on explicit operator eigenproblems, and richer metric parameterizations as the main directions for future work.

</details>


### [213] [Social Networks of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03695)
*Kaixuan Liu,Guojun Xiong,Weinan Zhang,Shengpu Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM智能体在互动中会因注意力有限而导致从众效应（herding），而非真正的知识聚合；本文提出SNLA框架来建模实际影响力，并证明了窄注意力导致样本量有界、从众，宽注意力仅在无向且度正则图上恢复群体智慧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在交互群体中会形成集体信念，但传统社交网络模型假设网络结构决定信念融合，忽略了LLM智能体注意力有限（只接收部分信息）的事实，导致高估信息聚合程度，无法区分真正共识与从众行为。

Method: 提出SNLA（Social Network with Limited Attention）框架，建模每个智能体对其他智能体的实际影响力，该影响力取决于智能体在网络中的位置以及注意力的集中程度。通过可处理的理论代理模型分析注意力宽窄对聚合行为的影响，并设计控制实验验证。

Result: 理论上证明：窄注意力导致从众效应，有效样本量有界而不随群体规模增长；宽注意力仅在曝光图无向且度正则时恢复群体智慧。经验上，控制实验直接验证了预测结果，从众-智慧转变在多智能体LLM基准的变体上复现。

Conclusion: LLM智能体群体的信念聚合受注意力机制限制，窄注意力会导致虚假共识和从众，无法实现真正的知识聚合；系统设计者需考虑网络结构和注意力机制对集体信念的影响。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly deployed in interacting populations, raising the question of what such populations come to believe collectively. Whether a population aggregates genuine knowledge or collapses into a false consensus directly affects how much such systems can be trusted. Classical social-network models assume that the network itself determines how beliefs combine. This assumption breaks down for LLM agents, whose limited attention takes in only part of what they are exposed to, so these models overstate how much information a population actually pools and cannot tell genuine consensus from herding. We introduce SNLA, a framework that models how much each agent actually influences others, rather than merely how the network connects them. This influence depends on each agent's position in the network and on how sharply attention focuses. Theoretically, we show on a tractable proxy that narrow attention causes herding, where the effective sample size stays bounded regardless of population size, while wide attention recovers wisdom-of-crowds behavior only when the exposure graph is undirected and degree-regular. Empirically, a controlled testbed validates these predictions directly, and the herding-wisdom transition reproduces on operator-controlled variants of three multi-agent LLM benchmarks.

</details>


### [214] [SAVER: Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction for Low-Dose Computed Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03761)
*Shunta Nonaga,Koji Tabata,Junya Honda,Hiroyuki Kudo,Wataru Yashiro,Tamiki Komatsuzaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAVER是一个自适应CT数据采集框架，通过基于实时数据统计方差的Softmax随机调度方案动态选择投影角度，在保持探索的同时优先采集高结构信息方向，从而在降低辐射剂量的同时提升重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统低剂量CT协议采用固定的均匀角度采样，忽略了不同患者器官的结构复杂性差异，难以在辐射剂量和图像质量之间取得最优平衡。因此需要一种能够根据个体患者数据特点动态调整采样策略的方法。

Method: 提出SAVER框架，利用实时采集数据的统计方差评估各方向的结构信息量，采用基于Softmax的随机调度方案结合模拟退火策略，在优先选择高信息量投影方向的同时保持必要的探索性，实现自适应角度选择。

Result: 在8个不同模体上的数值实验表明，SAVER在重建保真度上始终优于传统随机采样方法，尤其对于结构各向异性强的物体效果显著；同时在显著测量噪声下表现出稳健的性能。

Conclusion: SAVER通过将辐射剂量动态重新分配到信息量最高的投影方向，提供了一种最大化单位辐射剂量诊断质量的数学严谨方法，标志着CT采集向样本依赖、数据驱动范式的转变。

Abstract: Computed Tomography (CT) is indispensable in clinical diagnostics, yet minimizing radiation dose without compromising image quality remains a critical challenge. Conventional low-dose protocols often rely on fixed, uniform angular sampling, independent of the underlying structural complexity of organs of individual patients. We propose ``Stochastic Adaptive Variance-Driven Exploration and Reconstruction'' (SAVER), an adaptive data acquisition framework that selects projection angles in real-time based on the statistical variance of acquired data. Utilizing a Softmax-based stochastic scheduling scheme with simulated annealing, SAVER prioritizes directions with high structural information while maintaining necessary exploration. Numerical experiments across 8 diverse phantoms demonstrate that SAVER achieves consistently higher reconstruction fidelity than conventional random sampling, particularly for objects with high structural anisotropy. Furthermore, the proposed method exhibits robust performance under significant measurement noise. By dynamically reallocating radiation dose to the most informative projections, SAVER provides a mathematically-grounded approach to maximize diagnostic quality per unit of radiation dose, marking a shift toward sample-dependent, data-driven CT acquisition.

</details>


### [215] [FedACT: Federated Adaptive Coordinate Trust Modulation for Robust Transformer Training under Data Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03763)
*Shuai Li,Qinglin Wang,Ping Luo,Jiahuan Wang,Hongyang Hu,Haotian Mo,Yigui Feng,Ziang Liu,Qisong Xiao,Jie Liu,Tao Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedACT，一种用于联邦AdamW训练的全局感知坐标信任调节方法，通过坐标级别的信任分数重新分配更新幅度，在异构数据下提升Transformer等模型的训练效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦Transformer训练中，局部AdamW的自适应更新在客户端数据异构下会导致坐标级别的信任不匹配（coordinate trust mismatch），即全局校正后的AdamW更新在各坐标上的可靠性高度不均匀。现有联邦自适应优化器主要在客户端更新或通信轮级别处理不匹配问题，但仍然对所有坐标统一稠密地应用校正后的自适应方向。

Method: FedACT首先构建全局校正的自适应方向，然后根据坐标级别的信任分数重新分配更新幅度：对同时受局部梯度与全局校正支持的坐标分配较大步长，其余坐标保留较小的非零更新。

Result: 在联邦视觉Transformer、CNN、LLM预训练和LLM微调上的大量实验表明，FedACT持续优于强联邦自适应基线，在更强的数据异构性下对Transformer模型提升最大。机制分析表明FedACT改善了跨客户端的更新方向一致性。

Conclusion: 坐标级别的信任分配有效补充了轮级别的全局-局部校正，为联邦AdamW训练提供了一种更精细的优化策略。

Abstract: Federated Transformer training increasingly relies on local AdamW, whose adaptive updates can provide much stronger local progress than SGD-based training. However, under heterogeneous client data, even globally corrected AdamW updates may remain highly uneven in coordinate-wise reliability. We refer to this phenomenon as coordinate trust mismatch. Existing federated adaptive optimizers mainly address mismatch at the client-update or communication-round level, but still apply the corrected adaptive direction densely and uniformly across coordinates. In this paper, we propose FedACT, a global-aware coordinate trust modulation method for federated AdamW training. FedACT first forms a globally corrected adaptive direction and then reallocates update magnitudes according to a coordinate-wise trust score, assigning larger steps to coordinates jointly supported by local gradients and global correction, while preserving smaller non-zero updates on the remaining coordinates.
