  This paper studies fixed-step gradient descent as a discrete dynamical system in a hierarchy of exactly solvable models retaining basic structures of deep learning: depth, factorization, width, data coupling, activation, and stochasticity. The starting point is the balanced scalar reduction of a deep linear chain, giving a quartic loss and a cubic gradient map whose post-edge behavior is explicit. Under the natural large-depth scaling, this dynamics converges to a universal Ricker-type map. The edge of stability is therefore not a breakdown of optimization, but the first bifurcation of the training map.
  Embedding the scalar dynamics back into factored models turns these regimes into learning phenomena. Finite steps break conservation laws of gradient flow and contract factorization imbalance; residual oscillations move parameters toward flatter, more balanced representations. Wider linear networks produce a ladder of spectral edges, so the optimal learning rate can lie beyond the first edge. Data coupling, nonlinear activations, and stochastic targets preserve the same organizing principle: finite-step oscillations drive alignment, balancing, and representation selection. Thus the learning rate is not merely a numerical stability parameter. It is a structural parameter of the training dynamics, determining its attractors and shaping the representations gradient descent selects.

</details>


### [322] [ImputeECG: Deep Learning Reconstruction of Complete 12-Lead Electrocardiograms from Incomplete Recordings for Cardiac Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05009)
*Xiaocheng Fang,Haoyu Wang,Jieyi Cai,Qinghao Zhao,Jun Li,Shanwei Zhang,Guangkun Nie,Yujie Xiao,Shun Huang,Jiarui Jin,Hongmin Liu,Guodong Wang,Shuohua Chen,Liming Lin,Shouling Wu,Hongyan Li,Shenda Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了ImputeECG，一种基于掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，用于补全不完整的12导联10秒心电图信号。在PTB-XL、CPSC2018和Kailuan临床队列中，ImputeECG显著降低了缺失区域的MAE和MSE，恢复了下游诊断分类性能，并在真实世界心电图像数字化场景中验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床心电图记录常因显示格式短、波形数字化不完整、导联丢失或信号损坏而缺失导联或时段，限制了AI心血管评估的可用性。现有补全方法性能不足，亟需一种能保留观测样本同时准确补全缺失信号的模型。

Method: 提出ImputeECG，一个掩码条件的一维Transformer自编码器，采用mask-conditioned机制，在补全缺失导联/时段的同时保留所有观测样本。模型在PTB-XL上训练，并在模拟不完整设置及真实世界心电图像数字化场景中评估。

Result: PTB-XL上MAE降低41.7-51.0%、MSE降低54.0-63.7%；CPSC2018上MAE降低49.7-51.9%。下游分类任务中，最不完整设置下AUROC达92.28%、AUPRC达33.88%，接近完整ECG性能。Kailuan队列中，零样本性别预测AUROC从82.6%提升至85.8%，年龄预测MAE从10.72降至9.87年。

Conclusion: ECG补全是将不完整心电图记录转化为AI可用数字信号的有效策略，可扩展心电档案在数字心脏评估中的可用范围。

Abstract: Complete digital 12-lead electrocardiograms (ECGs) are essential for AI-enabled cardiovascular assessment, yet many clinical ECG records, particularly those digitized from ECG images, remain incomplete because of short display formats, incomplete waveform digitization, lead loss, or signal corruption. We developed ImputeECG, a mask-conditioned one-dimensional Transformer autoencoder that completes 12-lead, 10-s ECGs while retaining all observed samples. The model was trained on PTB-XL and evaluated on PTB-XL and CPSC2018 under simulated incomplete settings, with additional real-world validation in a 43,633-record Kailuan clinical cohort after ECG image digitization. Metrics were computed over originally missing regions, with analyses of morphology and downstream diagnostic utility. On PTB-XL, ImputeECG reduced missing-region MAE by 41.7-51.0% and MSE by 54.0-63.7% versus the strongest baseline, with lower errors in R-peak timing, RR interval, QRS duration, QT interval, and P-wave, QRS-complex, and T-wave reconstruction. On CPSC2018, ImputeECG reduced MAE by 49.7-51.9%, supporting external generalization. In downstream multi-label classification, ImputeECG restored performance to 92.28% AUROC and 33.88% AUPRC in the most incomplete PTB-XL setting, approaching complete-ECG performance. On CPSC2018, completed ECGs achieved 94.75-95.89% AUROC and 78.83-81.86% AUPRC across settings. In Kailuan, ECG completion improved zero-shot sex prediction AUROC from 82.6% to 85.8% and reduced age prediction MAE from 10.72 to 9.87 years after image-based ECG digitization. These findings support ECG completion as a practical strategy for converting incomplete ECG records into AI-ready 12-lead, 10-s digital signals and extending the usable scope of ECG archives for digital cardiac assessment.

</details>


### [323] [Hyperparameter Transfer in Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05017)
*Gage DeZoort,Boris Hanin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了图神经网络（GNN）中超参数迁移的参数化方法，使得在不同模型规模下（特别是宽度和深度增加时）能保持近最优超参数设置的一致性。通过理论缩放分析和控制实验，为SGD、Adam和AdamW优化器分别提出了迁移参数化方案，包括图相关的第一层修正因子、消息传递归一化策略以及权重衰减与学习率的联合迁移，为GNN的可扩展训练提供了实用指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习的超参数（学习率、初始化尺度、权重衰减等）对模型性能至关重要，超参数迁移旨在使这些设置在不同模型规模间保持一致，从而通过较小的代理模型调优来优化大型模型。尽管在稠密神经网络（语言和视觉任务）中已有充分研究，但在图神经网络中仍相对缺乏探索。

Method: 通过理论缩放分析和控制实验，针对SGD、Adam和AdamW三种优化器分别设计了GNN的迁移参数化方案：1) SGD中提出图依赖的第一层修正因子，加速稀疏词袋输入图的早期训练；2) Adam中探索不同消息传递归一化对早期和后期训练迁移行为的影响，强调归一化的重要性并推荐相关超参数；3) AdamW中适配允许权重衰减和学习率联合迁移的参数化方案。

Result: 提出的参数化方案在宽度和深度增加时能够保持稳定的特征更新、实现学习率迁移，并提升模型性能。

Conclusion: 本文为跨多种学习任务和训练场景的GNN缩放提供了实用的技术方案，填补了图神经网络中超参数迁移研究的空白。

Abstract: The performance of deep learning models crucially depends on the settings of hyperparameters like learning rate, initialization scale, and weight decay. Hyperparameter transfer aims to make near-optimal hyperparameter settings consistent across model scale, so that large models can be optimized by proxy tuning their smaller, cheaper-to-optimize counterparts. While transfer principles are well-studied in the context of dense neural networks in language and vision tasks, they remain comparatively under-explored for graph neural networks (GNNs). We develop and validate a transfer parameterization for GNNs trained with SGD, Adam, and AdamW. Through theoretical scaling analyses and controlled experiments, we show that the proposed parameterization yields stable feature updates, learning rate transfer, and improved performance as width and depth increase. For SGD, we identify graph-dependent first-layer correction factors and show that their use can accelerate early training in graphs with sparse bag-of-words inputs. For Adam, we explore how different message passing normalizations affect early- and late-training transfer behavior, illustrating the importance of message passing normalization and advocating for an associated hyperparameter. For AdamW, we adapt a parameterization that allows for the joint transfer of weight decay and learning rate. Together, these results provide a practical recipe for scaling GNNs across a variety of learning tasks and training scenarios.

</details>


### [324] [Beyond Modality Fusion: Deep Ensembles for Multimodal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05019)
*Ilya Burenko,Dmitry Vetrov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在多模态分类中，无需显式融合，仅使用单模态网络的深度集成即可有效分类。与同等参数量的晚期融合网络相比，集成方法始终优于最先进的晚期融合方法，并且在极端模态不平衡下，集成方法倾向于先依赖强模态，随着规模扩大再引入弱模态模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有晚期融合方法在面对模态不平衡时表现不佳，需依赖各种正则化技术来平衡学习过程。本文探索是否可以在无需显式模态融合的情况下，仅通过单模态网络的集成来实现有效的多模态分类。

Method: 系统比较深度集成与晚期融合网络（同等参数量）；提出启发式方法选择每模态模型数量，避免穷举搜索；提出合成多模态框架以系统控制模态数量和预测强度；对双模态数据集拟合缩放律以估计集成的渐近性能。

Result: 深度集成方法始终优于最先进的晚期融合和中间融合方法；在极端模态不平衡和小集成规模下，仅使用强模态训练的集成更优；随着集成规模扩大，引入弱模态模型变得有益；合成框架与真实数据上的结论一致。

Conclusion: 无需显式模态融合，深度集成方法即可实现有效的多模态分类，为多模态学习提供了一种简洁而强大的替代方案。

Abstract: In multimodal classification, late-fusion approaches classify concatenated modality-specific features extracted by unimodal neural networks.
  When modality imbalance is pronounced, various regularization techniques have been proposed to balance the learning process and overcome the inferior performance of late-fusion networks.
  In contrast, this work demonstrates that multimodal data can be effectively classified without any explicit modality fusion, using deep ensembles of unimodal networks.
  We systematically compare deep ensembles to late-fusion networks at equal parameter count and show that ensembles consistently outperform state-of-the-art late-fusion methods designed to address modality imbalance.
  This advantage also holds over intermediate-fusion techniques we evaluated and over hybrid methods that combine unimodal and multimodal predictions.
  We propose and empirically validate a method for selecting the number of models per modality in an ensemble, avoiding computationally expensive exhaustive search.
  Under extreme modality imbalance and small ensemble sizes, the heuristic indicates that ensembles of unimodal models trained solely on the stronger modality are preferable; as the ensemble scales up, incorporating models from the weaker modality becomes beneficial.
  Both predictions align with our empirical findings.
  To systematically explore the challenges of optimizing multimodal models, we propose a synthetic multimodal framework that allows control over both the number of modalities and their predictive strength; our findings are consistent across synthetic and real-world datasets.
  Finally, by fitting scaling laws to bimodal datasets, we estimate the asymptotic performance of ensembles.

</details>


### [325] [Uncertainty-aware damage identification in short-span bridges via physics-informed variational autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05025)
*Ana Fernandez Navamuel,A. Javier Omella,Diego Zamora-Sanchez,David Pardo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种物理信息高斯Copula变分自编码器（PI-GCVAE）框架，将可微分的数值特征值求解器嵌入VAE架构，并用高斯Copula替代潜变量的独立假设，从而在结构健康监测中实现具有不确定性量化的损伤识别，在合成桥梁数据集上达到77.2%的覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于振动的土木基础设施损伤识别是一个病态逆问题，受测量噪声、稀疏传感器阵列和环境变异性影响。现有的确定性深度学习方法缺乏可靠的不确定性量化且可能产生物理不一致的结果。

Method: 提出PI-GCVAE框架：1）将可微分数值特征值求解器嵌入VAE架构，确保潜变量满足结构动力学控制方程；2）用高斯Copula替代独立假设，建模相邻结构单元间物理相关的空间互相关性；3）利用Copula提供高维强相关潜空间的高效分布模型。

Result: 在合成简支桥梁数据集上验证，PI-GCVAE准确恢复了真实后验分布，达到77.2%的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 该框架为运行中桥梁的早期损伤诊断提供了可靠、可扩展的工具。

Abstract: Vibration-based damage identification in civil infrastructure is a challenging, ill-posed inverse problem due to measurement noise, sparse sensor arrays, and environmental variability. While deep learning is powerful for system identification, deterministic approaches lack reliable uncertainty quantification and can yield physically inconsistent results. This work proposes a robust probabilistic Scientific Machine Learning (SciML) framework: a physics-informed Gaussian copula variational autoencoder (PI-GCVAE) for structural health monitoring (SHM). First, we eliminate the need for data-driven surrogates by embedding a differentiable numerical eigenvalue solver directly into the VAE architecture. This ensures that latent space samples satisfy the governing equations of structural dynamics, reducing the trainable parameter space and improving generalization. Second, we replace the conventional independence assumption of latent variables with a Gaussian copula. This model captures complex, physics-dependent spatial cross-correlations between adjacent structural elements, defining feasible solutions while accounting for inherent system variability and measurement errors. Third, compared with alternatives such as Gaussian mixtures, our copula-based VAE provides an efficient distributional model for high-dimensional, strongly correlated latent spaces. We validate the approach using a synthetic dataset of a simply supported bridge subjected to various damage scenarios and corrupted with stochastic Gaussian noise. Synthetic data enables exhaustive validation against ground-truth stiffness values unavailable in practice. Results demonstrate that the PI-GCVAE accurately recovers the true posterior distribution, achieving 77.2% coverage. The proposed framework provides a reliable, scalable tool for early-stage damage diagnosis in operating bridges.

</details>


### [326] [CollabEval: Statistically Efficient Collaborative Model Evaluation via Matrix Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05046)
*Adam Fisch,Daniel Deutsch,Joshua Maynez,Alekh Agarwal,Jonathan Berant,William Cohen,Amir Globerson,Jacob Eisenstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CollabEval 提出了一种利用历史评估数据依赖关系的协同评估方法，将模型评估建模为矩阵补全问题，结合预测驱动推断（prediction-powered inference）构建低秩近似分数矩阵，并使用控制变量法保证无偏估计和有效置信区间，在相同标注预算下显著降低了置信区间宽度和点估计均方误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI模型的评估虽然在模型开发过程中反复进行，但资源消耗巨大（即需要大量人工标注），现有方法未能充分利用不同模型在相同任务上历史运行结果之间的依赖关系来提升统计效率。

Method: 将模型评估建模为 M×N 的分数矩阵补全问题（M为模型数，N为评估提示数），对目标模型仅标注小比例 p 的提示；利用预测驱动推断（prediction-powered inference）构建该矩阵的低秩近似，将重构值作为控制变量（control variates）来保证评估指标均值的无偏估计以及统计有效的置信区间。

Result: 在多种数据集、模型和稀疏度 p 条件下，CollabEval 相比基线方法，在相同标注预算下显著降低了置信区间大小和点估计的均方误差。

Conclusion: 利用模型间历史评估数据的结构化依赖关系可以有效提升评估的统计效率，CollabEval 是一种简单、有效且有理论保证的评估方法。

Abstract: Evaluating generative AI models is a routine, but resource-intensive, process that is conducted over and over again during the course of model development. In this work, we propose Collaborative Evaluation (CollabEval), a simple, effective, and principled method for exploiting dependencies between historical runs of different models on the same tasks to improve statistical efficiency. Specifically, our approach treats model evaluation as a matrix completion problem over an $M \times N$ matrix of evaluation scores, where $M$ is the total number of models and $N$ is the total number of evaluation prompts. We assume that a subset of these $M$ models are targeted for evaluation. For these target models only a small fraction, $p$, of prompts has been annotated with evaluation scores. Leveraging recent results in prediction-powered inference, we build a low-rank approximation of the score matrix, and use the reconstructed values as control variates in a manner that guarantees unbiased estimates of the true evaluation metric mean, in addition to statistically valid confidence intervals. Empirically, across a wide range of datasets, models, and sparsity levels $p$, we find that CollabEval substantially reduces the mean confidence interval size, and the mean squared error of the point estimate, compared to baseline methods at the same annotation budget.

</details>


### [327] [KVpop -- Key-Value Cache Compression with Predictive Online Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05061)
*Lukas Hauzenberger,Niklas Schmidinger,Anamaria-Roberta Hartl,David Stap,Thomas Schmied,Sebastian Böck,Günter Klambauer,Sepp Hochreiter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVpop提出通过直接监督keep-or-drop决策学习固定预算的KV缓存驱逐策略，使用新颖的未来注意力目标训练评分器，并引入延迟记忆评分器。在AIME和HMMT数学推理任务上，Qwen3-4B在75%压缩率下保持98%的全注意力性能，88%压缩率下保持97%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存增长是自回归解码的主要瓶颈，内存和带宽随上下文长度线性扩展。现有驱逐方法依赖静态启发式或代理分数，难以跟踪未来token效用，导致驱逐不稳定。

Method: 学习固定预算的KV驱逐策略，通过直接监督keep-or-drop决策；训练评分器使用新颖的未来注意力目标，无需物化密集注意力图即可高效计算；引入延迟记忆评分器，将评分推迟固定步数以利用近期上下文。

Result: 在AIME和HMMT数学推理上，Qwen3-4B在75% KV缓存压缩率下保留98%全注意力性能，88%压缩率下保留97%；Qwen3-8B达到接近全教师性能。

Conclusion: 使用未来注意力信号监督驱逐可以在保持质量的同时削减内存成本，KVpop持续优于现有驱逐基线。

Abstract: Key-value (KV) cache growth is a major bottleneck in autoregressive decoding, as memory and bandwidth scale linearly with context length. Existing KV eviction methods often rely on static heuristics or proxy scores, which poorly track future token utility and cause brittle eviction as relevance shifts. To address this, we introduce KVpop, which learns a fixed-budget KV eviction policy by directly supervising the keep-or-drop decision. The scorer is trained against a novel future-attention target, computed efficiently without materializing dense attention maps. We further introduce a delayed memory-based scorer that, uniquely among learned eviction methods, defers scoring for a fixed number of steps to exploit near-future context. On AIME and HMMT mathematical reasoning, KVpop retains 98% of full-attention performance on Qwen3-4B at 75% KV cache compression and 97% at 88% compression, consistently outperforming established eviction baselines. Qwen3-8B shows even stronger results, reaching near-full teacher performance. These results show that supervising eviction with future-attention signals cuts memory costs while maintaining quality.

</details>


### [328] [Computing Monetary Risk Measures in Linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05078)
*Palash Agrawal,Gersi Doko,Maeve Burwell,Marek Petrik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuickVaR和QuickDivergence两种新算法，用于在期望线性时间内计算离散随机变量的VaR和φ-散度风险度量（包括CVaR），在大规模问题上实现了数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 货币风险度量（如VaR和CVaR）在表达决策者风险厌恶中广泛应用，但现有计算效率不足，特别是对于大规模离散随机变量的域。

Method: QuickVaR算法改编自Quickselect算法；QuickDivergence算法基于多拟阵优化算法，并利用QuickVaR作为子程序。

Result: 新算法在大规模域上实现了数量级的加速，代码已开源在GitHub上。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种高效算法显著提升了离散随机变量风险度量的计算效率，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: Monetary risk measures have gained popularity for expressing decision-makers' risk aversion. Value-at-Risk (VaR) and Conditional-Value-at-Risk (CVaR), in particular, are used commonly for this purpose. This paper proposes new efficient algorithms to compute these risk measures for a discrete random variable in expected linear time with respect to the size of its domain. First, we propose a QuickVaR algorithm that computes the VaR of a discrete random variable. Then, we leverage QuickVaR to propose QuickDivergence, an algorithm for computing a class of $\varphi$-divergence risk measures, including the popular CVaR risk measure. The QuickVaR algorithm adapts the well-known Quickselect algorithm, while QuickDivergence builds on polymatroid optimization algorithms. Numerical results show that our new algorithms offer an order-of-magnitude speedup for large domains, and a library implementation of the algorithms is available at https://github.com/RiskAverseRL/RiskMeasures.jl.

</details>


### [329] [FAST: A Holistic Framework for Optimizing Memory-I/O, Computation, and Sampling in Temporal GNN Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05095)
*Yushu Cai,Qingrui Zhu,Lei Liu,Kai Sheng,Hao Chen,Xin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAST是一个全栈加速框架，通过联合优化采样、内存I/O和计算，实现TGNN训练端到端2.1倍（最高4.7倍）加速，且不损失模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TGNN训练系统因内存I/O、不规则计算和时序邻居采样三个瓶颈相互交织，且这些阶段常被孤立优化，导致在大规模动态图上性能不佳，存在显著的优化空间。

Method: 1) SlimCache：利用批次内压缩和跨批次缓存减少GPU内存有限下的主机-设备数据传输；2) 线程高效的图算子：针对稀疏时序子图优化GPU缓存局部性，降低聚合和边softmax延迟；3) 拓扑感知采样策略：提升CPU缓存局部性，加速时序邻居采样。

Result: 在真实大规模动态图上，FAST相比现有最优系统实现平均2.1倍（最高4.7倍）加速，且不牺牲模型精度。

Conclusion: 通过对采样、内存I/O和计算三个环节的联合协同优化，FAST有效解决了TGNN训练的可扩展性瓶颈，为大规模动态图学习提供了高效的端到端解决方案。

Abstract: Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) are widely used for learning from dynamic graphs in applications such as recommendation, social network analysis, and traffic forecasting. However, scaling TGNN training to large dynamic graphs remains challenging due to three intertwined bottlenecks: memory I/O, irregular computation, and temporal neighbor sampling. Existing systems often optimize these stages in isolation, leaving substantial performance headroom on the table. We present FAST, a holistic framework that accelerates end-to-end TGNN training by jointly optimizing sampling, memory I/O, and computation. FAST introduces SlimCache, which exploits within-batch compression and cross-batch caching to reduce host-device data movement under limited GPU memory budgets. It further designs thread-efficient graph operators tailored to sparse temporal subgraphs, improving GPU cache locality and reducing the latency of aggregation and edge softmax. In addition, FAST employs a topology-aware sampling strategy that improves CPU cache locality and accelerates temporal neighbor sampling. Extensive experiments on real-world large dynamic graphs show that FAST achieves an average of 2.1x (up to 4.7x) speedup over state-of-the-art systems without sacrificing model accuracy.

</details>


### [330] [Functional Bilevel Optimization for Predictive Fairness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05098)
*Ieva Petrulionyte,Julien Mairal,Michael Arbel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对连续高维敏感属性的公平性-准确性权衡，提出基于均值条件方差DPVar的泛函双层优化方法，两种算法在60个表格回归数据集上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性松弛方法（如HSIC、对抗方案、线性依赖等）无法直接优化公平性-准确性权衡，且对连续高维敏感属性（如人口统计得分、年龄/收入分布）的全统计独立约束过于严格。

Method: 定义均值条件方差DPVar作为公平性度量，构建泛函双层优化问题；提出FBO算法（利用平方损失下闭形式伴随矩阵计算精确超梯度）和ITD算法（通过展开内层步骤进行微分，可推广至非平方损失）。

Result: 在合成数据和基于60个表格回归数据集构建的半合成基准上，两种方法达到最低或接近最低的聚合公平性-准确性遗憾，一致优于或匹配HSIC、对抗、线性依赖和广义DP基线。

Conclusion: DPVar与泛函双层优化框架能有效直接优化公平性-准确性权衡，为连续高维敏感属性下的公平性学习提供了可扩展且性能优越的解决方案。

Abstract: When sensitive attributes are continuous and high-dimensional $-$ demographic score vectors, posteriors over attributes, age or income profiles $-$ enforcing full statistical independence is often too restrictive, and existing relaxations rely on indirect dependence penalties or adversarial schemes that do not directly target the fairness-accuracy trade-off. We instead consider mean demographic parity through DPVar, the variance of the conditional-mean prediction given the sensitive attribute, and show that optimizing it yields a functional bilevel problem. We propose two algorithms for this problem: FBO, which uses a closed-form adjoint we derive for the squared-loss case to obtain an exact hypergradient, and ITD, which differentiates through unrolled inner steps and extends beyond squared loss. On synthetic data and a new semi-synthetic benchmark built from 60 tabular regression datasets, both methods achieve the lowest or near-lowest aggregate fairness-accuracy regret, and consistently match or outperform strong HSIC, adversarial, linear-dependence, and generalized-DP baselines.

</details>


### [331] [Counterfactual Methods for Detecting Unfairness in Anti-Money Laundering Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05101)
*Lea Multerer,Michele Inchingolo,David Kletz,Adrian Cosma,Alessandro Antonucci,Martina Gogova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了反洗钱（AML）机器学习模型中引入账户国家与平均行为特征后，预测性能提升与公平性之间的权衡。通过反事实路径效应分析发现，性能提升越大的模型，其公平性违规越严重，凸显了在AML领域进行系统性公平性分析的紧迫性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AML检测系统通常使用敏感客户信息训练算法，可能存在公平性问题，但该领域在公平性视角下的研究仍十分匮乏。尽管基于反事实的深度分析方法已可用于分解敏感特征对模型预测的直接和间接影响，但尚未被应用于AML场景。

Method: 使用合成IBM AMLSim交易数据集，构建账户国家和平均行为等额外特征，训练从基线决策树到最先进的图神经网络等多种机器学习模型。通过反事实路径特定效应分析（path-specific effect analysis）评估与这些特征相关的潜在不公平性。

Result: 引入额外特征提升了各模型的预测性能，但公平性分析发现，预测性能提升越大的模型，其公平性违规也越显著，呈现出明确的预测准确性与公平性之间的权衡关系。

Conclusion: 在AML应用中存在预测准确性与公平性之间的具体权衡，亟需在该关键领域进行系统性的公平性分析。

Abstract: The application of machine learning-based predictive algorithms to Anti-Money Laundering (AML) has grown rapidly, driven by the vast volume of financial transaction data available to banks. These algorithms are typically trained not only on transactional data but also on sensitive client information, which may raise fairness concerns. Despite this, AML detection systems remain largely underexplored from a fairness perspective, even though deeper analytical methods based on counterfactuals are now available. Such techniques enable the decomposition of the direct and indirect effects of potentially sensitive features on model predictions, thereby supporting the evaluation of whether their influence is acceptable from a fairness perspective. Closing this gap, we consider the synthetic IBM AMLSim transaction dataset and construct additional features of the country of an account and its average behaviour. This improves the predictive performance of diverse machine learning models, ranging from baseline decision trees to state-of-the-art graph neural networks. We assess the potential unfairness associated with these features through a counterfactual, path-specific effect analysis. This reveals that fairness violations tend to be more pronounced for models whose predictive performance benefits the most from the extended features. Such a finding highlights a concrete instance of the trade-off between predictive accuracy and fairness in AML applications, thus underscoring the urgency of a systematic fairness analysis in such critical domains.

</details>


### [332] [Choosing a parallel heterogeneous ensemble method for tabular classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05103)
*Vassili Maillet,Gustavo,Angulo,Pierre Jouvelot*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在56个小型到中型表格分类任务上比较了并行集成方法，得出了最佳实践建议，并在28个额外任务上验证。主要发现：Blending和Stacking的不一致性相互独立；Robust Soft Voting在概率分类上表现优异，尤其在多分类场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统比较并行集成方法在不同规模表格分类任务上的表现，提炼出可操作的最佳实践建议，填补现有研究缺乏统一指导的空白。

Method: 在OpenML CC18的56个任务上进行并行集成方法对比实验，基于观察提炼最佳实践建议；随后在TabArena的28个额外任务上使用预计算数据验证建议的有效性。

Result: 1) 推荐的最佳实践集显著优于Single Best，且匹配或超过单一集成方法；2) Blending和Stacking的不一致性相互独立，发生在不同任务上；3) Hard Voting的概率分类较弱，而Robust Soft Voting的概率分类非常成功，在多分类场景下尤为突出。

Conclusion: 并行集成方法研究中，Blending和Stacking的失败模式是独立的，推荐使用Robust Soft Voting替代Hard Voting以获得更好的概率分类性能，尤其是在多分类任务中。

Abstract: Parallel ensemble methods were compared on $56$ small-to-medium tabular classification tasks drawn from OpenML CC18. A set of ``best practice'' recommendations on the use of ensemble methods was derived from these observations. It was later validated on 28 additional tasks using TabArena's precomputed data, where the recommendation set significantly outperformed Single Best and matched or exceeded individual ensemble methods.
  Two key observations were made. First, Blending and Stacking are inconsistent, but their inconsistencies are independent and happen on different tasks. Second, while Hard Voting's probabilistic classification is rather weak, a consequence of using vote proportions as posterior estimates, Robust Soft Voting's probabilistic classification is particularly successful, especially in the multiclass case.

</details>


### [333] [Grokking Is Conditional and Fragile: A Fully-Tractable, Multi-Seed Study at 12K Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05104)
*Yoshiyuki Ootani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过一个仅约11,856参数的Llama风格Transformer（Glimmer-1-Base）研究模算术上的“grokking”（延迟泛化）现象，发现该现象是条件性的脆弱相变，受训练集覆盖率门控、数值环境（CPU线程数、CPU vs GPU）敏感，且采用多种子率度量可消除单次运行中的种子混杂效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前grokking研究多使用过大的模型导致无法完整读取，且常基于单次训练运行报告，缺乏可重复性和可解释性。本文旨在通过一个完全可追踪的小型模型，系统性地度量grokking的多种子率，揭示其相变性质及数值敏感性。

Method: 使用约11,856参数的Llama风格Transformer（Glimmer-1-Base）在模算术任务上训练；枚举所有权重、注意力及完整输入-输出映射；采用多种子（multi-seed）率度量而非单次运行；控制训练集覆盖率、权重衰减、浮点环境（CPU线程数、CPU vs GPU）等变量；进行子任务专家分解分析。

Result: Grokking是条件性脆弱相变，门控因素为训练集覆盖率，其阈值主要跟踪输出基数（模数）而非任务结构；权重衰减在12K参数下复现Omnigrok倒U形曲线；浮点环境的微小扰动（CPU线程数、CPU vs GPU）可翻转少数同种子结果但不改变聚合率；多种子控制可推翻单个种子中的显著假象。

Conclusion: Grokking的证据单位必须是固定数值环境下的多种子率，并在可能的情况下通过直接读取模型进行验证。单次运行结果不可靠，数值环境的细微差异即可导致结果翻转。

Abstract: Grokking -- the delayed onset of generalization long after a network has fit its training set - -is usually studied in models too large to read completely and reported from single training runs. We instead study a publicly released ~11,856-parameter Llama-style transformer (Glimmer-1-Base) on modular arithmetic, small enough to enumerate its weights, attention, and full input-output map, and we measure grokking as a multi-seed rate rather than a single outcome. In this fully-tractable regime grokking is a conditional, fragile phase transition. It is gated by training-set coverage, whose threshold tracks output cardinality (the modulus) more than task structure, an ordering that holds above the transition and across a ten-fold change in domain size. Weight decay reproduces the Omnigrok inverted-U at 12K parameters, a positive control on the rate measurement. Grokking also sits on a numerical knife-edge: two perturbations of the floating-point environment -- CPU thread count (reduction order) and CPU-versus-GPU execution -- each flip a minority of same-seed outcomes without a detectable shift in the aggregate rate. Decomposition into sub-task specialists helps chiefly by making coverage cheap rather than by adding supervision. Methodologically, multi-seed control under a fixed numerical environment overturns three dramatic single-run narratives in our own data, each a seed confound. The unit of evidence for grokking must therefore be a multi-seed rate under a pinned numerical environment, checked where possible against a direct reading of the model.

</details>


### [334] [PDEFlow: Autonomous Agentic PDE Pipelines for Neural Operator Learning and Solver-Free Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05134)
*Akshat Jani,Prathamesh Gadekar,Sakhinana Sagar Srinivas,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present PDEFlow, an autonomous agentic framework that turns user-level ODE and PDE descriptions into solver-backed neural-operator pipelines. The workflow links problem specification, data generation, operator training, and checkpoint-based inference. A stateful input graph converts multi-turn natural-language input and user edits into validated problem specifications. The data-generation module then samples parameters, solves the configured governing-equation with FEniCSx finite-element backend, and stores the solutions as operator-ready tensors. The training and inference stages use a registry-based interface, allowing different neural operators to be trained and deployed without changing the surrounding pipeline. In the current implementation, we instantiate this interface with a multi-branch Bayesian DeepONet. Experiments on benchmark ODE and PDE tasks show that PDEFlow can construct valid specifications, generate solver-backed datasets, train neural operators across steady and transient problem classes, and provide solver-free predictions from saved checkpoints. The framework is designed for repeatable scientific and engineering workflows where many related physics configurations must be specified, simulated, learned, and queried with minimal manual intervention.

</details>


### [335] [Physiological Noise Augmentation Improves Non-Invasive Brain-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05165)
*Benjamin Ballyk,Teyun Kwon,Miran Özdogan,Oiwi Parker Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出生理噪声增强(PNA)方法，通过将脑信号分解为干净数据和噪声伪迹(如眼电、心电)，再缩放混合生成生物物理真实的训练样本，使解码器对任务无关伪迹具有不变性。在MegNIST数据集上，PNA结合10次试次平均使EEGNet解码准确率绝对提升4.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MEG和EEG的非侵入式脑-语音解码方法由于信噪比低，词错误率较高，亟需提高解码鲁棒性的方法。

Method: 受自动语音识别中环境噪声增强启发，利用独立成分分析(ICA)将脑记录分解为干净数据和噪声伪迹，通过缩放和重新混合生成保持标签的生物物理真实训练样本，近似各向异性正则化，惩罚解码器沿伪迹主导方向的敏感性。

Result: 在MegNIST数据集的12k试次想象数字MEG数据上，PNA结合10次试次平均使EEGNet解码准确率绝对提升4.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 伪迹感知增强和试次平均是改善非侵入式语音脑机接口鲁棒性的互补工具。

Abstract: Non-invasive brain-to-speech decoding aims to restore communication to patients suffering from neurodegenerative disease, without the risks of neurosurgery. Existing MEG- and EEG-based methods, while scalable, continue to suffer from high word error rates driven by relatively low signal-to-noise ratios compared to invasive recordings. We propose physiological noise augmentation (PNA), a data augmentation method that explicitly trains decoders to become invariant to task-agnostic artifacts (e.g. ocular and cardiac activity). PNA draws inspiration from automatic speech recognition systems, where environmental noise (e.g. dogs barking, city traffic) is added to clean speech to improve robustness. Analogously, we decompose brain recordings into clean data and noise artifacts using independent component analysis (ICA), before scaling and remixing to generate biophysically realistic, label-preserving training examples. We show that PNA approximates anisotropic regularization, penalizing decoder sensitivity along artifact-dominated directions. On MegNIST, a 12k-trial imagined-digit MEG dataset, PNA with 10-trial averaging improves EEGNet decoding accuracy by 4.7 percentage points (absolute) over training on real data alone. Our results suggest that artifact-aware augmentation and trial averaging are complementary tools for improving robustness in non-invasive speech BCIs.

</details>


### [336] [MeGA-MP: Metric Graph Advection Message Passing -- A Physics-Informed Message Passing Operator for Advection-Dominated Metric Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05167)
*Janine Strotherm,Luca Hermes,André Artelt,Barbara Hammer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于物理信息的消息传递算子，用于度量图上的平流动力学建模，在纯平流场景下无需训练即可精确恢复动力学，在平流-反应动力学中优于基线方法并实现零样本泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多真实系统（供水网、电网、交通流等）表现为度量图上的时空动力学，传统PINNs和算子学习方法受限于全局欧氏空间假设，且平流等传输动力学需要处理图上的反对称和长程依赖关系，现有方法难以直接适用。

Method: 提出一种物理信息消息传递算子，将度量图上的线性平流编码为归纳偏置；在纯平流设置中，算子理论上可证明以可推导的离散化误差恢复精确动力学，无需训练；结合可训练MLP组件，可扩展至平流-反应动力学。

Result: 在纯平流场景下，方法理论上可达精确解；在供水系统的平流-反应动力学中，性能优于基线方法，并实现了跨不同图拓扑的零样本泛化。

Conclusion: 该工作为度量图上的传输动力学提供了一种兼具理论保证和实用泛化能力的物理信息学习方法，填补了PINNs和算子学习在非欧氏图结构上的空白。

Abstract: Many real-world systems are organized as networks where spatio-temporal dynamics unfold along connections and not discretely between nodes. Examples include utility networks such as water distribution systems or gas networks, electrical grids, and traffic flow networks. Such systems are naturally modeled as metric graphs, where edges correspond to one-dimensional Euclidean subspaces connected at vertices. Metric graphs are independent of an underlying global Euclidean space, limiting direct application of typical PINNs and operator-learning methods. Especially transport dynamics like advection require a methodology able to capture antisymmetric and long-range dependencies on graphs, which is itself a challenge. We propose a novel physics-informed message passing operator that encodes linear advection on metric graphs as an inductive bias. In the purely advective setting, the operator provably recovers the exact dynamics up to a theoretically derived discretization error without any training. Combined with trainable components like MLPs, our message passing operator extends to realistic advection-reaction dynamics in water distribution systems, where we achieve superior performance compared to baselines and zero-shot generalization across different graph topologies.

</details>


### [337] [Platonic Projection Structures: Operator-Induced Observability in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05175)
*Kazuo Ishii,Bishnu Prasad Gautam,Jieling Wu,Javaid Saher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Platonic Projection Structures (PPS)框架，通过自伴半正定观测算子和商几何刻画表示学习中的可观测性，统一了量子测量、表示推理、知识蒸馏等场景下的表示可达性分析，并揭示了基于输出的可解释性的结构限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示学习缺乏对可观测性的形式化刻画，观测输出常被直接视为潜在表示的反映，而未考虑观测过程的结构性限制。

Method: 构建三元组(H, Π, O)系统模型，其中Π为自伴半正定观测算子，通过核空间上的商几何H/ker(Π)对不可区分的潜在状态等价类进行形式化，并展示了观测算子在不同场景下的代数差异。

Result: 实证验证了核不变可观测性、投影诱导的归因缺口、以及秩控制下的可观测几何；揭示了核空间中的潜在分量从诱导可观测中不可达，构成解释方法的内在约束。

Conclusion: PPS提供了通过算子诱导商几何刻画可观测性的显式框架，为表示可达性、可解释性和投影介导推理提供了统一视角。

Abstract: We characterize observability in representation learning through Platonic Projection Structures (PPS), an operator-theoretic framework for analyzing representation accessibility under partial observation. Rather than treating observable outputs as direct reflections of latent representations, PPS models observation through a self-adjoint positive semidefinite operator acting on a latent representation space. A system is represented as a triple $(H, Π, O)$, where $H$ is a latent representation space, $Π\succeq 0$ is an observation operator, and $O(v)=\langle v,Πv\rangle$ defines an induced scalar observable. Observability is characterized by the quotient geometry $H/\ker(Π)$, representing equivalence classes of latent states indistinguishable under observation. We show that quantum measurement and representation inference under linear observation models share this operator-theoretic structure while differing in the algebraic properties of their observation operators; the correspondence is structural rather than physical. Representation transfer and knowledge distillation can likewise be interpreted as approximate preservation of observable geometry through $ΦΠ_T \approx Π_S Φ$. PPS also reveals a structural limitation of output-based interpretability: latent components in $\ker(Π)$ are inaccessible from induced observables, imposing intrinsic constraints on attribution and explanation methods. Controlled empirical validations demonstrate kernel-invariant observability, projection-induced attribution gaps, and rank-controlled observable geometry in latent representation spaces. PPS thus provides an explicit characterization of observability through operator-induced quotient geometry and a unified perspective on representation accessibility, interpretability, and projection-mediated inference.

</details>


### [338] [Relational Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Dynamic Pricing in High-Speed Railway Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05179)
*Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,David Muñoz-Valero,Luis Rodriguez-Benitez,Giovanni Montana,Luis Jimenez-Linares*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对铁路系统中部分可观测环境下的动态定价问题，提出基于实体图建模的表示学习方法，将环境建模为运营单元图而非智能体或静态设施，通过图卷积网络和注意力机制扩展多智能体TD3算法，在铁路定价强化学习环境中取得了更高收益和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放松管制的铁路系统中，运营商需在部分可观测环境下动态定价，但竞争对手间存在私有信息和监管限制，无法直接通信或交换信息，只能从市场数据中推断策略互动。标准多智能体强化学习方法将观测视为非结构化向量，忽略了市场拓扑结构对策略互动的影响。

Method: 提出实体图建模方法，将环境表示为运营单元图，编码竞争、协作和连通性关系；在此基础上扩展多智能体TD3算法，通过多层关系图卷积网络处理实体特征，并利用学习的注意力机制进行聚合。

Result: 在铁路定价强化学习环境中，该方法在两种不同市场复杂度的设置下，相比多种关系型和非关系型基线方法，实现了更高的收益和稳定性。

Conclusion: 图结构表示学习能够有效捕捉市场拓扑结构中的策略互动关系，提升部分可观测环境下多智能体定价决策的性能。

Abstract: In liberalised railway systems, operators must set prices dynamically in an environment with partial observability, as they retain private information about their objectives and performance, where regulatory constraints prohibit communication or direct information exchange between competitors to prevent explicit collusion. Consequently, agents must learn to infer strategic interactions only from observable market data which presents a significant challenge for multi-agent reinforcement learning, where standard approaches typically treat observations as unstructured vectors, ignoring the underlying market topology that governs strategic interactions. To address this, an entity graph modelling approach is proposed, which represents the environment as a graph of operational units, rather than decision-making agents or static infrastructure, encoding competition, coordination, and connectivity relations between entities. Then, an extension of the multi-agent twin delayed deep deterministic policy gradient algorithm with graph-based representation learning processes the features of the entities through a multi-layer relational graph convolutional network and aggregates them via a learnt attention mechanism. Experimental results in a rail pricing reinforcement learning environment show that this novel framework achieves higher revenue and stability in two different settings of increasing market complexity compared to a representative selection of relational and non-relational baselines. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Kinrre/RelationalRailPricing-RL

</details>


### [339] [Latent Programming Horizons in Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05188)
*André Silva,Han Tu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了编码智能体中语言模型残差流对演化程序的表征能力，发现其线性编码了程序属性（如语法正确性、测试通过率等），且能提前预测未来编辑结果，揭示出智能体存在“潜在编程视界”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在软件工程任务中需要多步推理、编辑和测试，但语言模型内部对当前正在处理的程序究竟表征了什么信息，目前尚不清楚。本文旨在探究语言模型残差流中是否编码了演化程序的关键属性。

Method: 使用逻辑回归探针（logistic-regression probe）对语言模型隐藏状态进行解码，预测程序是否解析成功、是否通过测试、是否减少失败测试数、是否引入回归等属性。同时训练探针预测未来编辑结果，并跨基准测试迁移验证。

Result: 探针能解码当前代码属性，AUC最高达0.83；更重要的是，探针能提前约25步预测未来编辑结果，且跨基准测试迁移有效。

Conclusion: 语言模型在编码智能体中不仅编码当前程序状态，还能提前表征未来程序状态，存在“潜在编程视界”。这一发现为编码智能体的机械可解释性研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: A coding agent solving a software-engineering task spends dozens of steps reasoning, editing code, and running tests, yet little is known about what the underlying language model internally represents about the program it is working on. We show that the residual streams of language models under coding agents linearly encode properties of the evolving program: a logistic-regression probe on hidden states is able to decode whether the current code parses, passes its test suite, reduces the number of failing tests, and introduces regressions, reaching AUC up to 0.83 for correctness across two models and two benchmarks. Our second finding is more surprising: these representations run ahead of the agent's own edits. Probes trained to predict the outcome of future edits (before they are materialized and written on disk) achieve performance above chance up to roughly 25 steps in advance. We call this the agent's latent programming horizon. As a proof of external validity, we show that the probes transfer across benchmarks without retraining. Our positive results open calls for more research in mechanistic interpretability of coding agents.

</details>


### [340] [FlatManifold: Robust Continual Learning under Severe Label Noise and Domain Shifts via Intrinsic Manifold Flattening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05201)
*Rai Hisada,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatManifold提出一种基于Nyström流形平坦化映射与正交化RKHS投影的鲁棒持续学习框架，在40%对称标签噪声和跨会话领域漂移条件下，通过结构线性化本身抵抗梯度损坏，实现高泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非平稳流式环境中同时适应非线性领域漂移（持续学习）与缓解严重、未校准标签噪声的灾难性影响是一个基础性数学挑战。传统基于样本过滤的方法复杂且易出错。

Method: 提出FlatManifold框架，利用核技巧的Nyström流形平坦化映射和正交化RKHS投影；通过将特征分布映射到固定正交目标拓扑并加入岭正则化器，平滑极端标签噪声影响；利用过去经验的协方差矩阵设计持续拓扑制动项来防止灾难性遗忘。

Result: 在多会话真实机器人数据集上，即使在40%对称标签噪声和跨会话极端领域漂移（不同季节和光照条件）下，FlatManifold成功缓解梯度损坏，显著优于标准顺序优化基线。

Conclusion: 结构线性化本身可作为抵御分布式标签损坏的强大数学屏障，无需复杂样本过滤即可实现鲁棒的持续学习。

Abstract: In non-stationary streaming environments, simultaneously adapting to complex, non-linear domain shifts via continual learning while mitigating the catastrophic effects of severe, uncalibrated label noise poses a fundamental mathematical challenge. In this paper, we propose \FlatManifold{}, a novel, streamlined robust continual learning framework that utilizes a Nyström manifold flattening map based on the kernel trick and projection onto an orthogonalized Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Space (RKHS).
  Unlike traditional methods that rely on complex, error-prone sample-filtering pipelines, the proposed approach exploits the intrinsic mathematical robustness of the flattened space itself. By mapping feature distributions onto a fixed orthogonal target topology with a ridge regularizer, the framework naturally smoothes and counteracts the influence of extreme label noise during the optimization process. Concurrently, catastrophic forgetting is prevented via a continual topology brake term that leverages the covariance matrix of past experiences.
  Extensive evaluation on real-world multi-session robotics datasets demonstrates that even under severe conditions featuring 40\% symmetric label noise, \FlatManifold{} successfully mitigates gradient corruption. Under extreme cross-session domain shifts spanning various seasons and lighting conditions, the proposed framework establishes high generalization capabilities, significantly outperforming standard sequential optimization baselines and proving that structural linearization itself serves as a powerful mathematical barrier against distributed label corruption.

</details>


### [341] [Video-based detection of cessation of breathing in pre-term infants using machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05230)
*Dineo Serame,Lionel Tarassenko,Mauricio Villarroel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于摄像头非接触式视频监测技术在早产儿呼吸暂停检测中的应用。通过30名早产儿的视频和临床数据，使用残差网络提取躯干呼吸运动信号，单独视频模型平衡准确率达76.9%，结合阻抗呼吸描记法后提升至90.6%，证明视频信号可提供超越传统生理信号的呼吸信息，有助于提高新生儿呼吸监测的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早产儿因呼吸控制不成熟易发生呼吸暂停，但现有基于接触式传感器的监测方法易受运动伪影、传感器移位和皮肤脆弱性影响，可靠性不足。非接触式视频监测作为一种无需粘附传感器的补充手段，有望解决上述问题。

Method: 对30名早产儿同时采集视频和临床生理信号（阻抗呼吸描记法IP、心电导出呼吸EDR、光电容积脉搏波呼吸包络）。从视频中动态跟踪躯干区域提取呼吸运动时间序列，分别构建仅基于视频的ResNet模型和视频结合IP/EDR/PPG的混合模型进行呼吸暂停检测。

Result: 仅用视频信号的模型平衡准确率为76.9%；视频与IP结合的混合模型平衡准确率达90.6%，优于任一模态单独使用。结果表明视频信号提供了标准生理信号之外的额外呼吸信息。

Conclusion: 视频导出的呼吸信号包含临床相关的呼吸特征，与传统生理信号结合可显著提升呼吸暂停检测性能。非接触式视频监测可作为新生儿呼吸监测的补充模态，提高监测系统的整体鲁棒性。

Abstract: Pre-term infants are susceptible to potentially harmful apnoea-related cessations of breathing due to immature respiratory control. However, reliable respiratory monitoring in the neonatal intensive care unit (NICU) remains challenging because motion artefacts, sensor displacement, and skin fragility can compromise contact-based measurements. Non-contact video monitoring offers a complementary approach that does not depend on adhesive sensors while providing additional respiratory information.
  We investigated whether camera-based signals can detect apnoea-related cessation of breathing (COBE) and provide complementary information to routinely acquired physiological signals. Using video and clinical recordings from 30 pre-term infants, respiratory motion was extracted from dynamically tracked torso regions to generate camera-derived time-series signals. Camera-only models were trained using residual network (ResNet) architectures, while hybrid models combined video-derived signals with impedance pneumography (IP), ECG-derived respiration (EDR), and the PPG-derived respiratory envelope.
  Camera-only models achieved a balanced accuracy of 76.9%, demonstrating the feasibility of non-contact COBE detection. Combining video-derived features with IP improved balanced accuracy to 90.6%, outperforming either modality alone and indicating that video provides respiratory information beyond standard physiological signals.
  These findings show that video-derived signals contain clinically relevant respiratory features and enhance COBE detection when combined with conventional physiological signals. This supports non-contact video as a complementary modality for automated COBE detection and highlights its potential to improve the robustness of neonatal respiratory monitoring.

</details>


### [342] [CanniUplift: A Holistic Framework for Mitigating Seller and Incentive Cannibalization in E-commerce Uplift Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05242)
*Zuwang He,Shihao Shu,Yuli Qu,Hanyu Gao,Ziliang Zhang,Diwei Chen,Xiangda Yan,Buyu Gao,Tanchao Zhu,Yumeng Li,Junxiong Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CanniUplift框架，通过平台级全局对齐(PGA)和基于赎回的解耦去噪(RDD)机制，解决多卖家环境下激励分配的两种蚕食效应（卖家级和激励级），在合成和大规模工业数据集上显著优于基线，并在线上部署中实现平台增量GMV提升4.08%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统提升模型在多卖家复杂电商环境中因违反SUTVA假设而失效，主要面临两类蚕食效应：卖家级蚕食（激励导致支出在店铺间转移而非平台增长）和激励级蚕食（自然转化或替代奖励引入噪声），亟需统一框架同时缓解这两类问题。

Method: 提出CanniUplift统一框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 平台级全局对齐(PGA)，通过全局GMV一致性约束捕捉跨店替代效应；(2) 基于赎回的解耦去噪(RDD)，利用赎回行为在全体空间框架内分解处理结果并降低归因噪声；(3) Treat-Attention机制，建模用户历史行为与当前处理选项的复杂交互。

Result: 在合成和工业数据集上显著优于SOTA基线；消融实验验证PGA和RDD一致提升wAUUC和wQINI；线上A/B测试实现平台增量GMV相对提升4.08%，并改善ROI。

Conclusion: CanniUplift通过联合建模两类蚕食效应，有效提升了个体处理效应估计的准确性，并在实际电商平台中证明了驱动全局增长的商业价值。

Abstract: Personalized incentive allocation is vital for e-commerce, where uplift modeling is the standard for estimating Individual Treatment Effects (ITE). However, traditional models often fail in complex multi-seller environments with violations of the Stable Unit Treatment Value Assumption (SUTVA). We identify two critical challenges: Seller-level Cannibalization, where incentives shift expenditure between shops without growing the platform, and Incentive-level Cannibalization, where organic conversions or alternative rewards introduce significant noise into incrementality estimation. In this paper, we propose CanniUplift, a unified framework to mitigate these dual-source cannibalization effects. Specifically, we design Platform-level Global Alignment (PGA) to capture cross-shop substitution through global GMV consistency constraints. To tackle incentive-driven noise, we introduce Redemption-based Decomposition Denoising (RDD), which uses redemption behavior to decompose treated outcomes and reduce attribution noise within an entire-space framework. Furthermore, a Treat-Attention mechanism is designed to model intricate interactions between users' historical behaviors and current treatment options. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and large-scale industrial datasets demonstrate that CanniUplift significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Ablation studies confirm that the integration of PGA and RDD consistently improves wAUUC and wQINI. Successfully deployed online, our framework achieved a 4.08% relative increase in platform-wide incremental GMV (Delta GMV) over the production baseline and improved ROI in online A/B tests, proving effective in driving global platform growth.

</details>


### [343] [FUSE: FK-Steered Multi-Modal Flow Matching for Efficient Simulation-Based Posterior Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05252)
*Weichen Qin,Yufan Xie,Peihao Wang,Chia-Jui Chou,Minghui Du,Peng Xu,Ziren Luo,Yi Yang,Jingyi Yu,Bo Liang,Jiakai Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FUSE方法，采用双轨架构和FK引导采样策略，实现高效的多模态仿真推演，在标准SBI基准和真实系外行星轨道估计任务中均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBI方法在建模多模态数据时，依赖粗暴的融合策略，忽略了参数与观测之间的结构差异，限制了估计精度。

Method: 提出双轨架构保留多模态输入的各自特征同时促进动态交互，并设计FK-steered采样策略，利用中间观测似然引导生成轨迹以提升推理样本质量。

Result: 在标准SBI基准上优于SOTA基线，生成的后验分布与真实MCMC高度匹配；在真实系外行星轨道估计任务中成功解决参数简并问题。

Conclusion: FUSE方法显著提升了多模态SBI的推理质量，有望加速天体物理学及其他领域的科学发现。

Abstract: Simulation-Based Inference (SBI) is critical for scientific discovery, with generative models offering a promising path toward efficient inference. However, existing methods struggle with effective multimodal modeling. They often rely on brute-force fusion strategies that ignore the structural disparities between parameters and observations, thus limiting estimation fidelity. In this work, we introduce FUSE (Feynman-Kac steered mUlti-modal flow matching for efficient Simulation-based posterior Estimation). Unlike prior work, FUSE employs a dual-track architecture that preserves the distinct features of multimodal inputs while facilitating dynamic interaction. Additionally, we propose an FK-steered sampling strategy that leverages intermediate observation likelihoods to guide the generative trajectories, effectively improving the sample quality during inference. Our approach outperforms state-of-the-art baselines on standard SBI benchmarks, producing posteriors that closely match ground-truth MCMC. Furthermore, in a real-world exoplanet orbital estimation task, FUSE successfully resolves complex parameter degeneracies that challenge existing methods, highlighting its potential to accelerate complex scientific discoveries in astrophysics and beyond.

</details>


### [344] [GeoFlow: Geo-Aware Modeling of Inter-Area Relationships in Origin-Destination Flow Prediction and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05257)
*Zherui Huang,Guanjie Zheng,Hao Xue,Linghe Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GeoFlow是一个融合地理空间属性的OD流建模框架，通过几何-内在融合编码器和轴向-全局注意力解码器，结合流匹配模型，在预测准确性、生成保真度和多样性上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图的OD流建模方法忽略了重要的地理空间属性，导致无法有效建模长距离和多区域依赖关系。

Method: (i) 使用相对位置、k跳距离和测地距离增强区域表示；(ii) 设计几何-内在融合编码器，结合图注意力（内在区域信号）和坐标感知编码器（全局结构）；(iii) 采用轴向-全局注意力解码器捕获OD特定的竞争依赖关系；(iv) 与流匹配模型结合生成OD流。

Result: 在预测准确性上达到优越性能，同时在生成保真度和多样性上显著提升。

Conclusion: 消融和分析研究确认了每个组件的贡献，验证了GeoFlow在OD流建模中的有效性。

Abstract: Origin-destination (OD) flow modeling underpins urban planning and mobility analysis, but prevailing graph-based methods often neglect salient geographic attributes, limiting their ability to model long-range and multi-area dependencies. In this paper, we introduce GeoFlow, a novel framework that (i) augments area representations with geospatial attributes, including relative positions, k-hop and geodesic distances, (ii) employs a specialized geometric-intrinsic fusion encoder design that combines graph attention for intrinsic area signals with coordinate-aware encoders for global structure, and (iii) adopts an axial-global attention decoder to capture OD-specific competitive dependencies. For OD flow generation, GeoFlow is paired with flow matching models to produce more authentic and diverse mobility samples. Empirically, GeoFlow achieves superior performance in predictive accuracy, while substantially improving generative fidelity and diversity. Ablation and analytical studies confirm the contribution of each component. Code is available at https://github.com/ZheruiHuang/GeoFlow.

</details>


### [345] [Target-Guided Selective Reweighting for Physics-Informed Neural Network Inverse Problems: A Transfer Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05271)
*Qian Hu,Bin Fan,Yao Xiao,Zhicheng Lin,Meixin Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出目标引导选择性重加权PINN(TGSR-PINN)，通过神经元目标评分、高斯混合模型弱适应信号估计和选择性软衰减，解决PINN逆问题迁移学习中因物理机制差异导致的负迁移问题，在多个任务中改善目标参数恢复的同时保持场精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN在偏微分方程逆问题中面临不适定优化、损失竞争和参数补偿问题；迁移学习中直接微调在源域与目标域物理机制、控制参数或观测噪声不同时，会导致负迁移（低场误差但错误的目标物理参数），亟需一种表示修正方法。

Method: TGSR-PINN：仅迁移源PINN的权重和偏置，独立初始化目标物理参数；经短目标适应后，利用一阶泰勒敏感度和预激活方差计算神经元目标评分，通过高斯混合模型将低评分神经元关联的证据转化为连续弱适应信号，对低评分神经元的输入权重行和偏置施加选择性软衰减（而非硬剪枝或随机重置）。

Result: 在高Péclet数二维对流扩散任务、Allen-Cahn到Burgers跨PDE族迁移任务中，TGSR-PINN改善了目标参数恢复并保持可比场精度；5%噪声反应扩散任务提供了源-目标弱不匹配下的补充证据。

Conclusion: 消融研究表明神经元目标评分、弱适应信号估计、层保护和选择性软衰减共同贡献于性能提升。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) encounter ill-posed optimization, loss competition, and parameter compensation in partial differential equation (PDE) inverse problems. Transfer learning can reuse representations from source tasks, but direct fine-tuning may introduce negative transfer when dominant physical mechanisms, governing parameters, or observation noise differ between source and target domains: the model achieves low field error yet recovers incorrect target physical parameters. To mitigate, we propose Target-Guided Selective Reweighting PINN (TGSR-PINN), a target-evidence-driven representation correction method for PINN inverse transfer learning. TGSR-PINN transfers only the weights and biases from the source PINN, while target physical parameters are independently initialized; after a short target-adaptation phase, the method computes neuron target scores using first-order Taylor sensitivity and pre-activation variance on fixed scoring batches, and converts evidence associated with low-scoring neurons into continuous weak-adaptation signals via a Gaussian mixture model (GMM) with rank fallback. TGSR-PINN then applies selective soft decay to input weight rows and biases of low-scoring neurons instead of hard pruning or random resetting. In experiments, TGSR-PINN improves target parameter recovery while maintaining comparable field accuracy in the high-Péclet 2D advection-diffusion task and in the Allen--Cahn to Burgers cross-PDE-family transfer task; a 5%-noise reaction--diffusion case provides supplementary evidence under milder source-target mismatch. Ablation studies suggest that neuron target scoring, weak-adaptation signal estimation, layer protection, and selective soft decay jointly contribute to the benefits.

</details>


### [346] [Advances in Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05280)
*Benjamin Walker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对神经控制微分方程（NCDE）计算昂贵且本质顺序性的问题，提出了三种改进方法：Log-NCDE（应用Log-ODE方法高效近似求解）、线性NCDE（用线性向量场替代非线性以实现闭式解和并行计算）以及结构化线性NCDE（使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率），在不牺牲理论表达能力的前提下，将每步训练时间降低多达三个数量级，并在多个时间序列基准上达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCDE虽具有最大表达能力和连续时间灵活性，但其前向传播计算昂贵且本质上是顺序的，严重限制了可扩展性和实际应用。因此需要提升NCDE的训练效率和可扩展性。

Method: 本文提出三种方法：1) Log-NCDE：基于神经粗糙微分方程，采用Log-ODE方法在训练中高效近似NCDE的解；2) 线性NCDE：使用线性向量场替代非线性向量场，实现闭式解和时序并行计算；3) 结构化线性NCDE：使用结构化线性向量场进一步提升效率。

Result: 所提方法将NCDE每步训练时间降低多达三个数量级，同时在多个时间序列基准上达到最先进的性能。

Conclusion: 通过Log-ODE方法近似、线性化向量场以及结构化线性向量场三种互补策略，可以在大幅提升NCDE训练效率的同时保持理论表达能力和实际性能，从而推进NCDE的可扩展性和实际应用。

Abstract: Many real-world systems evolve continuously, yet most machine learning models interpret time series as discrete sequences. Continuous-time approaches instead treat time series as samples from an underlying input path, a formulation that naturally accommodates irregularly sampled or oversampled data. Among these, Neural Controlled Differential Equations (NCDEs) are a maximally expressive class of models that parametrise a vector field using a neural network and evolve their hidden state by solving a dynamical system driven by the input path. NCDEs typically use a non-linear vector field, so their expressive power and continuous-time flexibility come at the cost of a forward pass that is both computationally expensive and inherently sequential, limiting their scalability and practical applicability.
  This thesis advances the training and scalability of NCDEs through three complementary contributions. First, building on neural rough differential equations, Log-NCDEs apply the Log-ODE method to efficiently approximate an NCDE's solution during training, improving both computational speed and empirical performance. Second, Linear NCDEs replace the non-linear vector field with a linear one, enabling closed-form solutions and parallel-in-time computation without sacrificing theoretical expressivity. Third, Structured Linear NCDEs use structured linear vector fields to further enhance efficiency while maintaining theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance.
  Collectively, these methods reduce the time per training step for an NCDE by up to three orders of magnitude while achieving state-of-the-art performance across diverse time series benchmarks.

</details>


### [347] [Air Quality Downscaling with Station-Guided Pseudo-Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05292)
*Guorun Wang,Simone Foti,Andreas D. Demou,Leonidas Kotoulas,Theodoros Christoudias,Alexandros Koliousis,Mihalis Nicolaou,Stefanos Zafeiriou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种站引导的欧洲PM2.5降尺度框架，利用多尺度Transformer网络和时空传播策略，将粗分辨率CAMS大气成分场（×40倍，约1公里）与异构侧信息结合，在不依赖时序建模的情况下实现精细空间结构和偏差校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 粗分辨率大气场与局部PM2.5观测之间存在空间支撑不匹配问题：像素代表区域平均值，而地面真值观测是连续空间信号的离散、未对齐样本。现有方法难以弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出站引导的降尺度框架，融合CAMS粗分辨率场与异构侧信息（人类活动、土地覆盖、高程、卫星气溶胶观测、风场）；采用多尺度Transformer网络实现×40倍超分辨率；引入时间无关传播策略，利用OpenAQ观测的空间高斯混合插值来应对稀疏原位数据的密集监督挑战。

Result: 在欧洲范围的定性和站点级评估表明，模型能够恢复细粒度空间结构，并有效缓解局部的CAMS偏差。

Conclusion: 该框架成功实现了不依赖时序建模的PM2.5高分辨率降尺度与偏差校正，为粗分辨率大气产品到局部精细化估计提供了有效桥梁。

Abstract: Super-resolving coarse atmospheric fields to local PM$_{2.5}$ variations is uniquely challenged by a mismatch in spatial support: while pixels represent regional averages, ground-truth observations are discrete, unaligned samples of a continuous spatial signal. To bridge this gap, we present a station-guided framework for high-resolution PM$_{2.5}$ downscaling over Europe. Taking coarse CAMS atmospheric composition fields alongside heterogeneous side information (i.e., human activity, land cover, elevation, satellite aerosol observations, and wind fields) our framework jointly super-resolves ($\times 40$, $\approx$ 1 km) and bias-corrects CAMS rasters, without relying on temporal sequence modelling. To address the challenge of densely supervising our multi-scale transformer network with sparse in-situ data, we introduce a time-agnostic propagation strategy that utilises spatial Gaussian blending of interpolated OpenAQ observations. Extensive qualitative and station-level evaluations across Europe demonstrate that our model recovers fine-grained spatial structures and effectively mitigates localised CAMS biases.

</details>


### [348] [Learning Only What Valid Adapters Can Express: Subspace-Constrained Adaptation Against Fine-Tuning Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05300)
*Fabien Polly*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可信适配器池子空间约束的参数高效微调方法，该方法将更新限制在现有任务适配器估计的低维子空间中。实验表明，该方法在保留任务性能的同时，能有效抵御数据投毒和后门攻击，但牺牲了对池外任务的峰值适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数高效微调仍存在大量可被利用的行为改变更新空间，恶意目标函数可被表示和优化。现有方法缺乏对微调安全性的保障，因此需要研究一种既能保持适应性又能抵御攻击的约束机制。

Method: 将适配器更新约束在从可信任务适配器池估计的子空间中。使用Flan-T5-Large和196个公开LoRA适配器进行实验，通过子空间投影限制梯度更新的坐标维度（128维），并与完整LoRA微调对比。

Result: (1) 适配器功能相关内容位于低维共享子空间，30-38%权重范数冗余；(2) 子空间约束的梯度更新在干净分类数据上匹配完整LoRA，在标签反转攻击下LoRA完全崩溃（3-26%精确匹配），约束学习器保持62-96%；(3) 约束学习器无法拟合损坏数据，损失差异达120倍，天然提供OOD检测信号；(4) 对自适应后门攻击，当目标行为与池不匹配时攻击成功率0%（LoRA为100%），匹配时降至85%。

Conclusion: 基于可信适配器池的子空间约束能有效提升微调安全性，在攻击场景下显著优于无约束LoRA。但这种保护以牺牲对池外任务的峰值适应性为代价，且假设池本身可信。该方法在池覆盖不良的任务上不如无约束微调。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning still leaves a broad space of behavior-changing updates reachable, so a poisoned objective can be represented and optimized. We study an alternative: adaptation constrained to the subspace estimated from a trusted pool of existing task adapters. On flan-t5-large with 196 public LoRA adapters, we show that (1) the functionally relevant content of an adapter lies in a low-dimensional shared subspace, 30 to 38 percent of its weight norm being redundant under the evaluated task distributions; (2) gradient adaptation restricted to 128 coordinates on this subspace matches full LoRA fine-tuning on clean classification data, while under targeted label inversion LoRA collapses to 3-26 percent exact match and the constrained learner keeps 62-96 percent on the tasks the pool covers; (3) the constrained learner cannot fit corrupted data, its adaptation loss separating clean from garbage by two orders of magnitude (120x), an out-of-distribution signal without an extra detector; and (4) against an adaptive backdoor attacker who optimizes within the subspace, the attack is blocked (8 percent success versus 100 for LoRA) on the task where its target behavior is unlike anything in the pool, and only partially blocked (85 percent) when the target coincides with a common pool behavior. On these two tasks the outcome is consistent with how close the target is to the pool's directions, which suggests but does not establish a pool-relative boundary. The mechanism trades peak plasticity for these properties: on tasks the pool covers poorly, unconstrained fine-tuning wins, and the protection assumes the pool itself is trusted. Code and data are public.

</details>


### [349] [Biologically Informed Deep Neural Networks for Multi-Omic Integration, Pathway Activity Inference and Risk Stratification in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05306)
*Pedro Henrique da Costa Avelar,Le Ou-Yang,Min Wu,Sophia Tsoka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于多组学数据整合的通路活性自编码器框架，通过通路先验知识约束架构来实现可解释性，同时保持表征能力。在乳腺癌生存预测和亚型分类任务中验证了多组学整合的积极效果，发现基因、蛋白质和miRNA表达层贡献最大，并通过可视化展示了框架的临床相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多组学数据整合面临重大挑战，现有方法常在模型可解释性和表征能力之间权衡——要么依赖事后解释，要么使用线性模型而忽略复杂交互作用。

Method: 提出通路活性自编码器（Pathway Activity Autoencoders），通过通路先验知识约束网络架构，在保持表征能力的同时实现内在可解释性。

Result: 在乳腺癌中验证了多组学整合对生存预测和亚型分类的积极作用；基因、蛋白质和miRNA表达层贡献最大；dropout可提升鲁棒性但过度正则化会降低预测性能；可视化展示了框架的透明性和临床相关性。

Conclusion: 该框架能够生成具有生物学意义且可直接转化为临床相关见解的优越潜表征，为多组学整合提供了实用指南。

Abstract: Integrating complex, multi-omics data presents significant challenges. Existing approaches often face a trade-off between model interpretability and representational capacity, with most either relying on post-hoc interpretation or use linear models that may overlook complex interactions. We report Pathway Activity Autoencoders for the multi-omics setting, which embed prior knowledge via pathway-informed architectural constraints, fostering interpretability, while preserving representational power. Our multi-omic framework is applied in the context of breast cancer and is evaluated in survival prediction and subtype classification with results indicating a positive effect of integration. We conduct analysis of individual omics layer impact on end-task performance, revealing that gene, protein, and microRNA expression layers provide the strongest contribution. Repeatability studies indicate that, while dropout improves model robustness and consistency, excessive regularisation can reduce predictive performance. Finally, visualizations of the learned feature space illustrate the framework's intrinsic transparency and clinical relevance. The results underscore the value of multi-omic integration and delineate the impact of individual omics layers, establishing practical guidelines for integration within our framework. Overall, our pathway activity autoencoder frameworks yield superior latent representations that are biologically meaningful and are directly translatable into clinically relevant insights.

</details>


### [350] [How Far is Too Far? Defining the Distance Threshold for Verification Siamese Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05329)
*Heloísa Dias Viotto,Cauê Samonek,Lucas Garcia Pedroso,Marcos Sunye,André Abed Grégio,Paulo Lisboa de Almeida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无监督的Siamese验证网络阈值确定方法，通过假设距离分布为双峰函数并取两峰之间的最小值点作为阈值，无需标注数据即可达到与有监督方法相当的94%平均验证准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Siamese验证网络通常需要标注数据来设定阈值，但标注成本高且难以在部署环境中动态更新阈值，因此需要一种无需人工标注的自动阈值方法。

Method: 假设Siamese验证网络输出的距离分布服从双峰分布，通过识别两个模式之间的最小值点来自动确定验证阈值，完全无需标注样本。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、LFW和PKLot四个数据集上，该方法平均验证准确率达到94%，与等错误率（EER）方法相当。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在无需标注数据的情况下实现与有监督方法相近的性能，支持在部署环境中直接更新阈值，降低了人工标注成本。

Abstract: Siamese verification networks are widely used to compare items such as faces, cars, or signatures. In these scenarios, the network is trained to learn an embedding space in which similar objects are mapped closer together, while dissimilar objects are mapped further apart. Two objects are considered to belong to the same class (e.g., the same person in two different images) when the distance between their embeddings falls below a predefined threshold. Defining this threshold, however, is a non-trivial task and typically requires labeled data. In this work, we assume that the distribution of distances produced by a siamese verification network can be approximated by a bimodal function. Based on this assumption, we propose an unsupervised method to determine the verification threshold by identifying the minimum point between the two modes. The proposed approach does not require annotated samples, enabling the verification threshold to be updated directly in the deployment environment without the cost of manual labeling. We evaluate our method on four datasets: MNIST, CIFAR-10, LFW, and PKLot. The results indicate that the proposed approach achieves an average verification accuracy of 94%, comparable to the Equal Error Rate method, while eliminating the need for labeled data.

</details>


### [351] [TREK: Distill to Explore, Reinforce to Refine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05339)
*Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Kayhan Behdin,Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Hejian Sang,Xiaomin Li,Wenhui Zhu,Xinchen Du,Aida Rahmattalabi,Ran He,Sen Na,Zhipeng Wang,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TREK是一种两阶段训练方法，先用前向KL散度从教师模型（外部或自身）蒸馏高质轨迹以扩展学生策略支持域，再回到标准GRPO在线强化学习，解决GRPO在困难提示上因学生策略无法采样到正确解而停滞的问题。在数学推理和具身智能任务上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在困难提示上会停滞，因为正确解模式位于学生当前策略的采样支持域之外（off-policy），单纯依赖on-policy采样无法探索到这些高质轨迹。

Method: TREK分三阶段：（1）识别学生pass rate低的困难提示；（2）从教师（外部黑盒/白盒/自身+额外上下文）获取验证过的候选解，按学生似然保留top-r；（3）用短前向KL阶段将这些解拉入学生支持域，再回到标准on-policy GRPO。

Result: 数学推理：Qwen3-8B在AIME 2025从36.9→40.3，AIME 2024从47.9→51.1（avg@16）；自上下文变体无需外部教师达38.5/49.6。具身任务：ALFWorld 75.8→82.8，ScienceWorld 12.5→26.7，且最难任务类型上训练早期即达高成功率。

Conclusion: TREK通过蒸馏扩展支持域而非模仿，能泛化到多种教师设置，有效弥补GRPO在困难样本上的探索短板，且训练效率更高。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is effective when the current policy already samples useful reasoning trajectories, but it stalls on hard prompts whose correct solution modes lie outside the student's on-policy support. We propose TREK (Teacher-Routed Exploration via Forward KL), a simple staged procedure that uses distillation not for imitation but for exploration support expansion. A key advantage of TREK is its generality: because it only consumes verified output trajectories, it can use an external black-box teacher, a white-box teacher, or the same model given additional inference-time context, and it can efficiently identify which hard-prompt samples are most worth consolidating even when teacher internals are unavailable. TREK first identifies prompts where the unaided student has very low pass rate, queries a proposal source to produce verified candidate solutions, keeps the top-$r$ proposals ranked by current student likelihood, applies a short forward-KL phase to pull those verified modes into the student's support, and then returns to standard on-policy GRPO refinement. On mathematical reasoning, TREK with DeepSeek-V4 proposals improves Qwen3 models across all tested scales on AIME 2024 and AIME 2025; for Qwen3-8B, it improves AIME 2025 from 36.9 to 40.3 and AIME 2024 from 47.9 to 51.1 (avg@16), while the self-context variant reaches 38.5 and 49.6 without an external teacher. On agentic tasks, TREK raises ALFWorld success rate from 75.8 to 82.8 and ScienceWorld success rate from 12.5 to 26.7; notably, on the hardest task types, TREK achieves high success rates early in training while unaided GRPO requires substantially more optimization steps to reach comparable levels.

</details>


### [352] [CompactionRL: Reinforcement Learning with Context Compaction for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05378)
*Yujiang Li,Zhenyu Hou,Yi Jing,Jie Tang,Yuxiao Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CompactionRL，一种结合上下文压缩的强化学习策略，用于训练长程智能体LLM。该方法联合优化任务执行和摘要生成，采用token级损失归一化和跨轨迹广义优势估计，使LLM能从压缩的长程轨迹中学习。在多个开源模型上取得一致性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程智能体LLM受限于有限上下文窗口，扩展交互轨迹可能超出最大上下文长度导致任务未完成。上下文压缩虽能解决此问题，但将其融入强化学习训练尚未被充分探索。

Method: CompactionRL：1）在强化学习框架中联合优化任务执行与摘要生成；2）采用token-level loss normalization平衡任务与摘要的损失尺度；3）提出cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation (GAE)，在压缩后的长程轨迹上进行优势估计。

Result: 基于GLM-4.5-Air（106B-A30B）在SWE-bench Verified上Pass@1达66.8%（+7.0），Terminal-Bench 2.0达24.5%（+3.1）；基于GLM-4.7-Flash（30B-A3B）分别提升5.5和6.8点至56.0%和20.2%。已部署于GLM-5.2（750B-A40B）的RL训练流程。

Conclusion: CompactionRL有效结合上下文压缩与强化学习，在多种规模的开源模型上均能显著提升长程智能体任务性能，证明了该方法在训练长程agentic LLM中的实用价值。

Abstract: Long-horizon agentic LLMs are increasingly limited by finite context windows, as extended interaction trajectories can exceed the maximum context length before a task is completed. Context compaction offers a natural solution by summarizing previous interaction states and continuing the rollout under a compressed context, but incorporating compaction into reinforcement learning remains underexplored. We propose CompactionRL, a reinforcement learning strategy to train long-horizon agentic LLMs with context compaction. Our approach jointly optimizes task execution and summary generation with token-level loss normalization and cross-trajectory generalized advantage estimation. This design enables the LLM agents to learn from compacted long-horizon trajectories. We train CompactionRL on top of open models and observe consistent performance gains on agentic coding tasks. CompactionRL enables the open GLM-4.5-Air model (106B-A30B) to achieve Pass@1 scores of 66.8% on SWE-bench Verified and 24.5% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, with absolute gains of 7.0 and 3.1 points, respectively. Built upon GLM-4.7-Flash (30B-A3B), CompactionRL improves Pass@1 by 5.5 and 6.8 points, reaching 56.0% on SWE-bench Verified and 20.2% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, respectively. CompactionRL is thus deployed in the RL pipeline for training the open GLM-5.2 model (750B-A40B).

</details>


### [353] [TabPack: Efficient Hyperparameter Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05380)
*Yury Gorishniy,Akim Kotelnikov,Ivan Rubachev,Artem Babenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPack 是一种高效 MLP 集成方法，通过并行采样和训练不同超参数的 MLP 并在训练过程中动态选择成员，实现了强大的开箱即用性能，显著减少了对传统超参数调优的依赖和计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效 MLP 集成方法要求所有底层 MLP 使用相同超参数，导致仍需超参数调优才能达到最佳性能；研究者希望通过减少调优依赖来降低计算资源和人工成本。

Method: TabPack 在单次运行中并行采样并训练多个具有不同超参数的 MLP，并在训练过程中动态选择集成成员；用户只需指定超参数采样范围而非精确值。

Result: 在中大规模公开数据集上，使用默认配置的 TabPack 性能与经过大量调优的先前方法相当；在 MacBook 上运行默认配置的时间少于在工业级 GPU 上调优某些基线方法的时间。

Conclusion: TabPack 显著减少了在表格数据任务上获得竞争性结果所需的调优工作和计算资源，实现了强大的开箱即用性能。

Abstract: In deep learning for tabular data, efficient ensembles of multilayer perceptrons (MLPs) have recently emerged as effective and practical architectures. Existing methods of this kind use the same hyperparameters for all underlying MLPs, which requires hyperparameter tuning for achieving the best performance. In this work, we introduce TabPack, an efficient MLP ensemble with strong out-of-the-box performance and reduced reliance on traditional tuning. In a single run, TabPack samples and trains many MLPs with different hyperparameters efficiently in parallel and selects ensemble members on the fly during training. Thus, TabPack only requires specifying ranges from which to sample MLP hyperparameter rather than exact hyperparameter values, which naturally demands less precision for good performance. In experiments on medium-to-large public datasets, TabPack with default settings performs on par with extensively tuned prior methods, thus substantially reducing effort and compute resources needed to achieve competitive results on tabular tasks. Notably, running the default TabPack configuration on a modern MacBook took less time than tuning some baselines on an industry-grade GPU.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [354] [Characterizing and Bridging the Diagnostic Gap in eBPF Verifier Rejections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02748)
*Yusheng Zheng,Zhengjie Ji,Weichen Tao,Xiangyu Gao,Jianchang Su,Wei Zhang,Andi Quinn,Dan Williams*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: eBPF验证器错误信息模糊，47%的拒绝仅返回EINVAL，一个错误字符串对应多达9种根因。本文提出bpfix工具，从验证器日志重建证明位置并输出Rust风格诊断。评估显示，bpfix定位可将LLM修复成功率提升11-21个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eBPF验证器拒绝程序时返回的错误信息不清晰，开发者难以定位程序在何处丢失了验证器所需的证明。现有工具仅帮助阅读错误，缺乏定位根因的能力。

Method: 对235个复现的拒绝案例进行实证研究，分析错误与根因的映射关系；提出bpfix工具，从验证器日志重建证明建立和丢失的位置，输出Rust风格诊断；构建75个LLM修复任务的基准评估集。

Result: 47%的拒绝仅返回EINVAL，一个错误字符串最多映射到9种根因，12种根因中有10种是eBPF特有的。原始日志下LLM一次修复成功率为0-37%，使用bpfix定位后修复率提升11-21个百分点。

Conclusion: 定位证明丢失的位置是指导修复的关键，bpfix能有效提升eBPF程序修复效率。代码已开源。

Abstract: eBPF lets developers run custom programs inside the Linux kernel, where a verifier proves each program safe. However, when the verifier rejects a program, the unclear error makes repair challenging: the error reports where verification stopped, not where the program lost the proof the verifier required. To quantify this gap, we conduct an empirical study of 235 reproduced rejections, showing that 47% of rejections return only EINVAL, one error string maps to as many as nine distinct root causes, and 10 of the 12 root causes are eBPF-specific. Repair thus requires both domain knowledge and locating where the proof was lost, yet existing tools only help developers read the error. We present bpfix, which reconstructs where the required proof was established and where it was lost from the verifier log, and prints a Rust-like diagnostic. To evaluate bpfix and the ability of LLMs to help repair, we construct a benchmark of 75 LLM repair tasks. Current models achieve 0-37% one-shot success with the raw log, and replacing the log with the bpfix localization improves repair by 11-21pp, suggesting that locating where the proof was lost is key to guiding repair. bpfix is available at https://github.com/eunomia-bpf/bpfix

</details>


### [355] [Elastic Gang: Per-Token Membership Change for a Hard-Barriered LLM Inference Gang Co-Scheduled with OS Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04668)
*Daeyeon Son*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Anima OS提出弹性群体（elastic gang）机制，将设备端LLM推理的屏障同步计算作为一等调度实体，通过ACK锁存epoch协议实现核心成员在token间动态变化，在保证推理比特精确性的同时提升通用任务吞吐量，并在AMD Zen 5机器上验证了其Pareto优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端LLM解码是屏障同步的CPU-SIMD计算，每个token需要占用所有核心数毫秒，而操作系统希望连续使用这些核心。直接将屏障群体放入抢占调度器会导致死锁或logits静默损坏，现有方案无法在保证推理正确性的同时灵活复用核心。

Method: 提出弹性群体，作为Anima OS中的一等可调度实体，核心成员可在token间变化。核心机制是ACK锁存epoch协议：采用seqlock风格代际标记锁存与RCU/epoch风格成员身份同意组合，每个token的参与者集合是群体请求的核心与确认当前epoch的核心的交集。未确认的核心不参与当前token，最多延迟一个token加入。被移出的通用进程迁移并继续运行，代际结束时核心立即归还。

Result: 在AMD Zen 5 (8C/16T) 机器上，135M和7B模型的推理输出在验证的逐token成员变化下比特精确。相比静态核心分区，弹性群体在中间推理占空比下通用吞吐量提升1.75倍(25%)、1.52倍(50%)、1.28倍(75%)，推理空闲时回收全部8个核心，饱和时收敛到静态分区。归还核心开销0.22μs(p50)，获取繁忙核心开销约16ms（一个调度量子）。解码吞吐在群体宽度8时饱和，让出超出核心几乎无成本。

Conclusion: 弹性群体机制通过epoch协议安全地实现了LLM推理核心的动态弹性分配，在不牺牲推理正确性和吞吐的前提下显著提升通用计算吞吐量，并能在线自动调整群体大小，为设备端LLM推理与通用计算的高效共存提供了可行的系统级解决方案。

Abstract: On-device LLM decoding is a hard-barriered CPU-SIMD computation that wants every core for milliseconds per token, while the rest of the OS wants those same cores continuously. A barriered gang cannot simply be dropped into a preemptive scheduler: an unannounced departure deadlocks a barrier, and an unannounced arrival silently corrupts logits. I present the elastic gang of Anima OS, a bare-metal x86-64 Rust kernel in which the inference gang is a first-class schedulable entity whose core membership may change between any two tokens. The core mechanism is an ACK-latched epoch protocol that never waits on a named core: a seqlock-style generation-tagged latch composed with RCU/epoch-style membership consent, so each token's participant set is the intersection of the cores the gang requested and the cores that acked the current epoch. An un-acked core is outside this token and joins at most one token later. Displaced general processes migrate and keep running; cores return to them the moment a generation ends.
  On a real AMD Zen 5 machine (8C/16T), inference output is bit-exact under verified per-token membership change on both a 135M and a 7B model, the property that makes elasticity safe in a kernel whose safety gate reads logits. Against fair static core partitions, elastic membership Pareto-dominates: at intermediate inference duty cycles it delivers 1.75x (25%), 1.52x (50%), and 1.28x (75%) the general throughput of a static 8-core split at equal or better inference throughput, recovers all eight stranded cores when inference is idle, and converges to the split at saturation. Returning a lent core costs 0.22 us (p50); acquiring a busy, tenant-occupied core costs one scheduling quantum (~16 ms): a running tenant is never preempted mid-slice. Decode throughput saturates at gang width 8, so ceding cores past the knee is nearly free: elasticity auto-sizes the gang online.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [356] [When Should Service Agents Reconsider? Difficulty-Routed Control in Customer-Service Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01426)
*Qian Chen,Chengyuan Liu,Xin Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种难度路由的服务控制架构，通过轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由到升级工作流。升级路径采用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中决策和安全措施，而非对所有会话统一施加额外控制。在零售和航空任务上验证了该方法能持续提升存在操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主客服智能体正从对话交互转向操作执行角色（如退款、取消、改单等），这带来了服务控制问题：企业需保持常规服务快速低摩擦，同时防止因客户指令、策略约束、企业记录和后端写入之间交互产生的操作错误。现有方法对所有会话统一施加控制，效率低下。

Method: 提出难度路由的服务控制架构：(1) 轻量级路由器将常规会话保持在低成本基线路径，将操作耦合的会话路由至升级工作流；(2) 升级路径使用冲突感知通信和写入触发重新考虑机制，在关键后端写入前集中决策和安全措施。

Result: 在零售任务中，该方法持续提升了存在操作冲突的服务请求的可靠性。路由证据表明更强的控制被定向到冲突请求而非常规请求。对话和工具使用分析显示改进并非来自无差别交互扩展或更广工具链，而是增加的回合和工具调用用于证据收集、写入分离和写入前重新考虑。案例证据显示升级工作流保留了备用计划、将检索记录绑定到正确操作、排序写入并分解多实体请求。航空任务验证了相同逻辑的有效性。

Conclusion: 难度路由的服务控制架构能在保证常规服务高效的同时，对存在操作冲突的请求施加更有针对性的安全控制，有效提升自主客服智能体在操作执行场景中的可靠性。

Abstract: Autonomous customer-service agents are shifting from conversational interfaces toward operational execution roles: they retrieve firm records, apply service policies, and execute backend writes such as refunds, cancellations, exchanges, order modifications, and reservation changes. This shift creates a service-control problem: firms must keep routine service fast and low-friction while preventing operational errors on requests where customer instructions, policy constraints, firm records, and backend writes interact. We propose a difficulty-routed service-control architecture that asks when service agents should reconsider before acting. A lightweight router keeps routine sessions on a low-cost baseline path and routes operationally coupled sessions to an escalated workflow.
