The escalated path uses conflict-aware communication and write-triggered reconsideration to concentrate deliberation and safeguards before consequential backend writes, rather than applying additional control uniformly across all service sessions. We evaluate the architecture on human-verified retail and airline tasks from $τ^{2}$-bench. In retail, the method improves reliability consistently on service requests with operational conflict. Routing evidence shows that stronger control is directed toward conflicted requests rather than broadly applied to routine ones. Dialogue and tool-use profiles suggest that gains do not come from indiscriminate interaction expansion or broader tool chains; instead, added turns and tool calls support evidence gathering, write separation, and pre-write reconsideration. Case-level evidence shows that the escalated workflow preserves fallback plans, binds retrieved records to the correct action, sequences writes, and decomposes multi-entity requests. Airline results extend the same service-control logic to reservation operations.

</details>


### [357] [Evaluating Generative Agents with Actions Grounded in Socially Distributed Task Environments using Incognita](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02975)
*Dan C. Hsu,Luke Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Incognita 框架，将社会交互与可执行操作分离，构建了角色隔离的知识分布环境（Socially Distributed Task Environments），用于评估智能体在需要与不同角色实体通信以获取知识并执行操作的任务中的表现。在 Incognita-Retail 环境上对三个生成式智能体模型的实验表明，尽管更强大的模型能更好地获取知识、联系更多实体并尝试更多操作，但整体成功率仍很低（最高17.2%），且普遍存在过早结束任务的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准环境要么提供可执行操作和可验证结果（如 tau-bench），要么提供丰富的社交交互（如社交模拟环境），但缺乏将二者结合的场景。然而实际社会环境中，任务相关知识分散在不同角色参与者之间，只有通过与这些参与者交互才能获取知识并执行关键操作。因此需要一种能够同时评估智能体社交探索（知识获取）与可执行操作（环境利用）能力的评价设置。

Method: 1. 定义 Socially Distributed Task Environments（SDTE），其中任务知识按角色隔离分布，关键操作只能通过角色参与者访问。2. 提出 Incognita 框架，基于 Concordia 构建，将社交交互与可执行操作分离：智能体向用户或专业实体路由消息；专业实体调解可执行操作；确定性子环境执行被接受的操作并维护规范状态；离线评估器使用继承奖励评分。3. 构建 Incognita-Retail，将 tau-bench retail 转换为多实体环境，保留最终状态奖励语义。4. 在 18 个按社交广度分层的任务上评估三个生成式智能体模型（共 540 次试验）。

Result: 成功率从 0% 提升至 8.9% 和 17.2%；过早结束任务的比例从 100% 下降至 87% 和 58%；更强的模型能获取更多隐藏知识、联系更多实体、尝试更多对环境的写操作；但整体可靠性仍然很低。

Conclusion: 社交分布任务环境能够在成功之前暴露智能体的行为模式，包括知识获取、信息来源选择、环境操作尝试以及过早完成任务的信令。这些发现表明当前生成式智能体在需要社交交互与可执行操作结合的真实任务中仍存在显著不足。

Abstract: Effective agency in social environments depends on when an agent seeks knowledge, when it acts, and whether its actions are justified by acquired information. Existing grounded benchmarks provide executable actions, persistent state, and verifiable outcomes, while social simulation environments provide rich interaction among language agents. We study an evaluation setting that combines these requirements. We define socially distributed task environments as interactive environments where task-relevant knowledge is partitioned across role-isolated participants and consequential actions are accessible only through them. Communication serves as exploration over role-partitioned knowledge, while grounded action serves as exploitation over environment state. We introduce Incognita, a Concordia-based framework that separates social interaction from grounded execution. The evaluated agent routes messages to a user or specialist entities; specialists mediate admissible operations; a deterministic sub-environment executes accepted operations over a canonical state; and an offline evaluator scores outcomes with inherited rewards. Incognita-Retail transforms tau-bench retail into a multi-entity environment while preserving final-state reward semantics. We evaluate three generative agent models on 18 tasks stratified by social breadth, with 540 trials. Progress appears in reward and behavior: success rises from 0 percent to 8.9 percent and 17.2 percent, while premature finalization falls from 100 percent to 87 percent and 58 percent. Stronger models elicit more hidden knowledge, contact more entities, and attempt more grounded writes, yet reliability remains low. These findings show that socially distributed task environments expose behavior before reliable success, including knowledge elicitation, source selection, grounded action attempts, and premature completion belief.

</details>


### [358] [Silicon Sampling via Cross-Survey Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03091)
*Chan-Tung Ku,Chan Hsu,Pei-Cing Huang,Frank Cheng-shan Liu,I-Ling Cheng,Yihuang Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出跨调查迁移框架评估LLM作为硅样本的有效性，发现零样本LLM在个体层面预测准确率达52%，存在稳定的构念可预测性层级（从党派态度的67%到主权的23%），并揭示了方差塌缩和安全对齐效应的复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估大多基于分布比较而非个体层面预测，这可能将模式匹配与一致的个体层面预测混为一谈，缺乏更严格的评估框架来检验LLM作为调查受访者的有效性。

Method: 提出跨调查迁移评估框架：给定受访者对一组问题的回答，预测其对同一调查中完全不同问题的回答。使用TEDS 2024数据、三个开源LLM（27B-120B参数）和监督机器学习基线进行实验。

Result: (1) 零样本LLM在未见项目上达到52%准确率，距随机森林仅差6个百分点；(2) 存在稳定构念可预测性层级：党派态度67%、主权23%；(3) 方差塌缩和安全对齐效应比先前报告更为复杂。

Conclusion: 澄清了硅样本的前景与边界，揭示了LLM作为受访者的实际能力与局限性。

Abstract: Silicon sampling-using large language models (LLMs) to simulate human survey respondents-has emerged as a promising approach for augmenting traditional survey research. However, most evaluations rely on distributional comparisons rather than individual-level prediction, which risks conflating pattern matching with coherent respondent-level prediction. We propose cross-survey transfer, a more rigorous evaluation framework in which an LLM is given a respondent's answers to one set of questions and must predict their answers to entirely different questions from the same survey. Using data from the Taiwan Election and Democratization Study (TEDS) 2024, three open-weight LLMs (27B-120B parameters), and supervised machine learning baselines, we find that: (1) zero-shot LLMs achieve 52% accuracy on genuinely unseen items, closing to within 6 percentage points (pp) of a supervised random forest trained on same-population data; (2) a stable construct predictability hierarchy emerges, from 67% for partisan attitudes to 23% for sovereignty; and (3) variance collapse and safety alignment effects-two commonly cited LLM limitations-turn out to be more nuanced than previously reported, with variance collapse affecting supervised models as well and alignment effects varying dramatically across model families. These findings clarify both the promise and boundaries of silicon sampling.

</details>


### [359] [Evaluating LLM Uncertainty in Long-Form Generation Using Deterministic Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03870)
*Ido Amit,Ido Galil,Ran El-Yaniv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SALT基准，包含六个程序化生成的长文本任务，具有确定性真实答案，用于无偏评估LLM在原子级（token级）到整体生成级的不确定性估计。通过对50+ LLM的分析，揭示了不同置信度函数的性能差异、原子级排序失效、未来错误的两种可分离驱动因素（前缀传播和上下文长度退化），以及推理（CoT或内化）带来的准确率与置信度排序之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长文本生成的不确定性评估方法受限于标签不完美，缺乏零噪声基准来精确评估不同粒度（从token到整个生成）的正确性、校准和排序性能。

Method: 构造SALT基准，包含六个程序化任务，每个任务有单一确定性长文本真实答案；在SALT上对50+ LLM进行系统性分析，评估不同置信度函数在不同粒度下的表现，并通过受控原子级干预实验研究错误的驱动因素。

Result: 不同置信度函数在不确定性各维度上表现各异；置信度排序在原子级（token级）基本失效，但在行级粒度上可分离性增强；错误由两种可分离因素驱动——前缀传播（受全局上下文正确性主导）和答案-上下文长度增长带来的有界退化；推理（CoT或内化）在提升准确率的同时损害了置信度排序能力。

Conclusion: SALT为长文本不确定性估计提供了可靠的零噪声评估平台，揭示了原子级不确定性估计的局限性以及推理带来的准确率-校准权衡，对高风险应用中可靠错误识别与缓解具有直接影响。

Abstract: As LLMs generate increasingly long outputs, effective uncertainty estimation must identify errors at fine-grained levels rather than discard entire responses. While such methods exist, evaluating uncertainty at any resolution (token to an entire generation) is challenging and highly sensitive to label imperfections, making zero-noise benchmarks essential; yet, long-form generation benchmarks tend to rely on fallible labels rather than deterministic ground truth. We introduce Single-answer Atomic Long-form Target (SALT), a benchmark of six procedurally generated tasks with single deterministic long textual ground truths, enabling unit-level evaluation of correctness, calibration, and ranking without external judges. Equipped with SALT, our analysis of 50+ LLMs reveals key insights: We identify which confidence functions dominate each uncertainty aspect and show that confidence ranking largely breaks at atomic resolution, even when clearer separability emerges at coarser line-level units. SALT further enables controlled atom-level interventions throughout generation, revealing two separable drivers of future errors: propagation from corrupted prefixes, dominated by global context correctness, and bounded degradation from increasing answer-context length. Finally, we demonstrate that reasoning, via Chain-of-Thought prompting or internalized through training, introduces a trade-off, improving accuracy while degrading confidence ranking. These findings directly impact risk-critical applications requiring reliable error identification and mitigation.

</details>


### [360] [Explainable AI for Screening Abuse-Related Trauma in Bangladeshi Children: A Training-Free Multimodal Framework Evaluated on Noise-Aware Synthetic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04010)
*Salma Hoque Talukdar Koli,Fahima Haque Talukder Jely*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ShishuRaksha AI，一个面向孟加拉国儿童虐待相关心理创伤的早期筛查决策支持框架。该框架融合四种筛查模态（问卷、孟加拉语叙事文本、HTP绘画特征、面部情感），采用无训练、临床加权的跨模态注意力机制，并引入含噪声的合成基准数据集进行验证。融合模型AUC达0.874，显著优于仅用SDQ问卷的基线（0.756）。本研究为低资源环境下可伦理部署的儿童保护筛查提供了可行性验证与设计贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国儿童心理健康资源极度匮乏（每10万人口仅1.17名心理健康专业人员，全国仅6名儿童精神科医生），且缺乏孟加拉语、文化适应的儿童虐待相关心理创伤早期筛查工具。现有筛查方法单一，无法充分利用多模态信息，且在实际临床中难以伦理地收集受虐儿童数据集。

Method: 提出 ShishuRaksha AI 框架，融合四种筛查模态：验证问卷（SDQ、CPSS）、孟加拉语叙事文本、House-Tree-Person（HTP）绘画特征、面部情感。融合采用无训练、临床加权的方式，使用跨模态注意力机制，并包含单模态覆盖规则。每个风险分数通过临床加权的、基于扰动的加法归因进行解释，生成双语报告并路由至国家儿童保护服务机构。由于无法伦理收集真实临床数据，构建了含噪声的合成基准数据集（500例，阳性率23.2%，四层故意噪声，基于文献的HTP先验），并用树集成替代融合设计（排除面部通道）进行5折分层交叉验证评估。

Result: 融合模型AUC达0.874 [0.834-0.908]，显著优于仅用SDQ问卷的基线0.756 [0.705-0.803]。进行了消融分析、操作点分析、亚组分析和校准分析。所有局限性被公开说明，包括仅使用合成数据、无独立测试集、文本特征循环问题以及城乡亚组差距。

Conclusion: 本研究是面向低资源环境下可伦理部署的儿童保护筛查的可行性研究与设计贡献。尽管存在合成数据等局限性，但多模态融合方法显示出优于单模态基线的性能，并为未来在真实临床环境中的部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Bangladesh has an estimated 1.17 mental-health professionals per 100,000 population and only six child psychiatrists nationwide. No Bengali-language, culturally adapted tool exists for early screening of abuse-related psychological trauma in children. We present ShishuRaksha AI, a decision-support (not diagnostic) framework that fuses four screening modalities: validated questionnaires (SDQ, CPSS), Bengali narrative text, House-Tree-Person (HTP) drawing features, and facial affect. The fusion is training-free and clinically weighted, uses cross-modal attention, and includes a single-modality override rule. Every risk score is explained through clinically weighted, perturbation-based additive attribution and rendered as a bilingual (Bangla/English) report with referral routing to national child-protection services (OCC, DSS, NMHH) under the Children Act 2013. No clinical dataset of abused children can be collected ethically at this stage, so we introduce a noise-aware synthetic benchmark (500 cases, 116 positive [23.2%], four deliberate noise layers, literature-grounded HTP priors) and evaluate tree-ensemble surrogates of the fusion design (facial channel excluded) under 5-fold stratified cross-validation. The fused model reaches an AUC of 0.874 [0.834-0.908], against 0.756 [0.705-0.803] for an SDQ-only baseline, with ablation, operating-point, subgroup, and calibration analyses. We state all limitations openly, including synthetic-only data, no held-out set, text-feature circularity, and an urban-rural subgroup gap. This work is a feasibility study and a design contribution toward ethically deployable child-protection screening in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [361] [DSpark: Confidence-Scheduled Speculative Decoding with Semi-Autoregressive Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05147)
*Xin Cheng,Xingkai Yu,Chenze Shao,Jiashi Li,Yunfan Xiong,Yi Qian,Jiaqi Zhu,Shirong Ma,Xiaokang Zhang,Jiasheng Ye,Qinyu Chen,Chengqi Deng,Jiping Yu,Damai Dai,Zhengyan Zhang,Yixuan Wei,Yixuan Tan,Wenkai Yang,Runxin Xu,Yu Wu,Zhean Xu,Xuanyu Wang,Muyang Chen,Rui Tian,Xiao Bi,Zhewen Hao,Shaoyuan Chen,Huanqi Cao,Wentao Zhang,Anyi Xu,Huishuai Zhang,Dongyan Zhao,Wenfeng Liang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DSpark是一种统一并行推测解码与自适应负载感知验证的框架，通过半自回归架构引入块内依赖建模以维持草稿质量，并采用置信度调度验证动态调整验证长度以优化系统效率，在DeepSeek-V4生产系统中实现了每用户生成速度60-85%的提升，并拓展了服务系统的帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行草稿生成器在单次前向传播中提出长序列时，因缺乏token间依赖导致接受率快速衰减；同时，不加区分地验证长块浪费了批处理容量在高拒绝风险token上，严重降低了高并发服务系统的吞吐量。

Method: DSpark包含两个核心设计：(1) 半自回归架构——将并行骨干网络与轻量级顺序模块耦合，引入块内依赖建模以缓解后缀衰减；(2) 置信度调度验证——基于估计的前缀存活概率和引擎吞吐量特征，动态调整每个请求的验证长度。

Result: 在跨领域离线基准测试中，DSpark相比最先进的自回归和并行草稿生成器显著提升了接受长度。在DeepSeek-V4生产系统真实用户流量下，相比MTP-1基线，在匹配吞吐量水平上实现了每用户生成速度60-85%的提升，并防止了严格交互约束下的严重吞吐量下降。

Conclusion: DSpark通过统一高质量并行生成与负载感知验证，不仅提升了推测解码的效率，还使得之前无法实现的性能层级成为可能，成功推动了服务系统帕累托前沿的迁移。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Model (LLM) inference by decoupling draft generation from target verification. While recent parallel drafters efficiently propose long token sequences in a single forward pass, they suffer from rapid acceptance decay due to a lack of inter-token dependencies. Furthermore, indiscriminately verifying these extended blocks wastes critical batch capacity on tokens with high rejection risks, severely degrading throughput in high-concurrency serving systems. We introduce DSpark, a speculative decoding framework that unifies high-throughput parallel generation with adaptive, load-aware verification. To maintain draft quality, DSpark utilizes a semi-autoregressive architecture, coupling a parallel backbone with a lightweight sequential module, to introduce intra-block dependency modeling and mitigate suffix decay. To optimize system efficiency, DSpark employs confidence-scheduled verification, dynamically tailoring the verification length for each request based on estimated prefix survival probabilities and engine-specific throughput profiles. On offline benchmarks across diverse domains, DSpark substantially improves the accepted length over state-of-the-art autoregressive and parallel drafters. When deployed within the DeepSeek-V4 serving system under live user traffic, DSpark successfully mitigates verification waste. Compared to the established production baseline (MTP-1), DSpark accelerates per-user generation speeds by 60 to 85 percent at matched throughput levels. More importantly, by preventing severe throughput degradation under strict interactivity constraints, it enables performance tiers that were previously unattainable, shifting the Pareto frontier of our serving system.

</details>


### [362] [LLM-as-a-Verifier: A General-Purpose Verification Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05391)
*Jacky Kwok,Shulu Li,Pranav Atreya,Yuejiang Liu,Yixing Jiang,Chelsea Finn,Marco Pavone,Ion Stoica,Azalia Mirhoseini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM-as-a-Verifier，一种无需额外训练的通用验证框架，通过连续概率评分实现验证在粒度、重复评估和标准分解三个维度的缩放，在多个基准上达到SOTA，并可用于RL反馈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有缩放范式（预训练、后训练、测试时计算）主要关注LLM能力提升，但验证（判断解决方案正确性）作为关键能力尚未被充分探索。现有LM评判器使用离散评分，缺乏细粒度反馈且难以扩展。

Method: 提出LLM-as-a-Verifier框架：对评分Token的logits分布计算期望得到连续分数，支持三个维度的缩放——评分粒度（细粒度分数）、重复评估（多次评估取平均降低方差）、标准分解（将复杂标准拆解为子标准降低复杂度）。并提出基于连续分数的成本高效排序算法。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%)、SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%)、RoboRewardBench (87.4%)、MedAgentBench (73.3%)上达到SOTA。细粒度信号可用于任务进度估计和RL训练，提升SAC和GRPO的样本效率。

Conclusion: 验证可以作为LLM能力缩放的新轴，连续概率评分框架能有效实现验证的多维度缩放，提升验证准确率并提供广泛的下游应用价值。

Abstract: Scaling pre-training, post-training, and test-time compute have become the central paradigms for improving the capabilities of LLMs. In this work, we identify verification, the ability to determine the correctness of a solution, as a new scaling axis. To unlock this and demonstrate its effectiveness, we introduce LLM-as-a-Verifier, a general-purpose verification framework that provides fine-grained feedback for agentic tasks without requiring additional training. Unlike standard LM judges that prompt LLMs to produce discrete scores for candidate solutions, LLM-as-a-Verifier computes the expectation over the distribution of scoring token logits to generate continuous scores. This probabilistic formulation enables verification to scale along multiple dimensions: (1) score granularity, (2) repeated evaluation, and (3) criteria decomposition. In particular, we show that scaling the scoring granularity leads to better separation between positive and negative solutions, resulting in more calibrated comparisons. Moreover, scaling repeated evaluation and criteria decomposition consistently lead to additional gains in verification accuracy through variance and complexity reduction. We further introduce a cost-efficient ranking algorithm for selecting the best solution among candidates using the verifier's continuous scores. LLM-as-a-Verifier achieves state-of-the-art performance on Terminal-Bench V2 (86.5%), SWE-Bench Verified (78.2%), RoboRewardBench (87.4%), and MedAgentBench (73.3%). Beyond verification, the fine-grained signals from LLM-as-a-Verifier can also serve as a proxy for estimating task progress. We build an extension for Claude Code, enabling developers to monitor and improve their own agentic systems. Finally, we show that LLM-as-a-Verifier can provide dense feedback for RL, improving the sample efficiency of SAC and GRPO on robotics and mathematical reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [363] [ASK in the Dark: Uncertainty-Gated LLM Assistance under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02686)
*Juarez Monteiro,Nathan Gavenski,Guilherme Lima,Francisco Galuppo,Odinaldo Rodrigues,Adriano Veloso*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ASK+方法，通过为小语言模型提供轨迹感知上下文（部分地图、访问位置、动作历史）和结构化思维链，解决其在部分可观测环境中无法有效指导强化学习智能体的问题。实验表明该方法显著提升成功率，且小模型性能可媲美大模型，证明提示设计和选择性门控比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境下，强化学习智能体信息不完整，理论上适合用小语言模型（SLM）的推理先验来引导，但现有不确定性门控方法中SLM几乎从不输出独立动作（覆盖率为零），导致指导失效。作者将这一失败归因为"裸露的自我中心提示"缺乏足够上下文，识别出这是上下文问题而非模型容量问题。

Method: 提出ASK+方法：1）为SLM提供轨迹感知上下文（部分地图、访问位置、动作历史）；2）引入结构化思维链推理，将SLM从被动冗余检查转变为提供信息性建议的顾问；3）利用预测熵作为选择性查询信号（证明该熵度量动作不确定性而非状态不确定性，在POMDP中仍有效）。

Result: DoorKey任务：ASK+达93%成功率（基线ASK与PPO均为89%）；FourRooms：从53%提升至70%；HigherLower：达73.7%准确率，匹配SLM-only上限。Qwen3.5-2B与Qwen3.5-4B性能相当，表明提示设计和选择性门控主导影响。

Conclusion: 通过高质量的上下文设计和选择性查询机制，小语言模型可以在部分可观测环境中有效引导强化学习智能体，且模型规模不是关键因素。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning agents operating under partial observability must act on incomplete information, making them natural candidates for guidance from small language models (SLMs) that carry broad reasoning priors. Yet integrating SLM guidance into this setting has proven difficult: across all test environments, vanilla uncertainty-gated approaches achieve an overwrite rate at or near zero, meaning the SLM almost never contributes an independent action. We trace this failure to the bare egocentric prompt, which provides insufficient context for genuine reasoning, and identify it as a context problem rather than a capacity problem. We propose ASK+, which supplies the SLM with trajectory-aware context (a partially revealed map, visited positions, and action history) and structured chain-of-thought reasoning, converting it from a passive redundancy check into a more informative consultant that occasionally corrects the policy. We further establish that the predictive entropy signal used for selective querying measures action uncertainty rather than state uncertainty and remains informative in POMDPs, making uncertainty-gated assistance viable beyond fully observable settings. The stateful prompt drives substantial gains: on DoorKey, where vanilla ASK matches PPO (both 89%), ASK+ reaches 93% success; on FourRooms, success climbs from 53% to 70%; on HigherLower, accuracy reaches 73.7%, matching the SLM-only upper bound. Across all environments, Qwen3.5-2B matches or exceeds Qwen3.5-4B, confirming that prompt design and selective gating dominate the impact of model scale, enabling guidance without large models.

</details>


### [364] [When Aggregate Alignment Misleads: Auditing Policy Repair Without Per-State Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03386)
*Peiying Zhu,Sidi Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在缺乏逐状态专家标签的情况下，如何通过区域级诊断反馈（而非直接行动标签、奖励或代码）评估智能体AI系统对决策策略的编辑修复效果。在酒店定价模拟器中，多重启LLM编辑器通过区域级反馈达到了接近基准策略的RevPAR（108.47 vs 108.75），且显著降低了剧集组成距离（从1.153降至0.609）。实验表明，该效果不能由随机搜索或提示格式解释，但树编辑器的更高对齐度反而导致收益下降，说明策略修复应通过诊断反馈能否成为可靠闭环结果来评估，而非单一行为距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在智能体AI系统编辑、优化决策策略的场景中，逐状态的专家行动标签往往不可获得，因此需要探索如何仅利用区域级诊断反馈（如价格分布与基准策略的差异摘要）来评估和指导策略编辑，而不依赖直接的动作标签、奖励值或基准代码。

Method: 在酒店定价模拟器中，智能体策略编辑器仅接收区域级诊断反馈（价格分布在时间、库存和市场区域上的差异摘要），无法观测基准动作、基准源代码、奖励值或保留结果。编辑器通过多重启LLM（大语言模型）对目标动作表提出受限编辑。实验设置包括多重启LLM编辑器、非语义搜索基线（最多2500次评估）、打乱诊断信息的对照实验以及树编辑器对比。

Result: 多重启LLM编辑器在5000个保留剧集上达到RevPAR 108.47（95% CI 107.61-109.34），接近基准策略的108.75（95% CI 107.81-109.68），LLM与基准的配对差为-0.276（95% CI [-0.692, 0.146]）。仅用廉价诊断投影即可恢复大部分收益（107.90），LLM编辑器的独特收益在于将剧集组成距离从1.153降至0.609。非语义搜索基线落后8.77-14.57 RevPAR点，打乱诊断的对照实验跌至94.30。树编辑器虽对齐度更高（0.214 vs 0.266）和参考状态D1更高（0.328 vs 1.197），但收益降至98.91。

Conclusion: 智能体策略修复的评估不应仅依赖单一的行为距离指标，而应考察诊断反馈能否成为可靠的闭环结果信号。LLM编辑器的修复效果是真实但部分的，更强的行为对齐并不必然带来更高的收益，这提示了策略修复评估需要更全面的方法论。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems are increasingly used to edit, refine, and repair decision policies, but evaluating these edits is difficult when per-state expert action labels are unavailable. We study this problem in a hotel-pricing simulator where an agentic policy editor receives only region-level diagnostic feedback: summaries of how its price distribution differs from a benchmark policy across time, inventory, and market regions. The editor cannot observe benchmark actions, benchmark source code, reward numbers, or held-out outcomes, and may only propose constrained edits to a target-action table. On 5,000 held-out episodes, a multi-restart LLM editor reaches RevPAR 108.47 (95% CI 107.61 - 109.34), close to the benchmark policy's 108.75 (107.81 - 109.68), with paired gap (LLM minus benchmark) -0.276 and 95% CI [-0.692, 0.146]. A cheap diagnostic projection already recovers much of the revenue (107.90), so the LLM editor's distinctive gain is not raw revenue lift alone: it also reduces episode composition distance from 1.153 to 0.609. This is the strongest non-benchmark repair result. This profile is not explained by restart search alone: non-semantic proposers with up to 2,500 evaluations fall 8.77 - 14.57 RevPAR points short. Nor is it explained by plausible prompt format: a shuffled-diagnostic control breaks region-error correspondence and falls to RevPAR 94.30. The match is genuine but partial. A tree editor achieves stronger pooled alignment, 0.214 versus 0.266, and stronger reference-state D1, 0.328 versus 1.197, yet revenue falls to 98.91. These results show that agentic policy repair should be evaluated by whether diagnostic feedback becomes reliable closed-loop outcome, not by a single behavioral distance.

</details>


### [365] [From Mobile Data to Business Insights: An End-to-End Analytics Framework for Large-Scale Urban Mobility Analysis and Decision Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03394)
*Thiago Andrade,Shazia Tabassum,Miguel E. P. Silva,Ricardo Dinis,Joao Gama*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于移动应用实时位置数据的综合数据平台，涵盖数据采集、匿名化处理、ETL管道、机器学习建模及可视化分析，用于解决城市规划（旅游、交通）和商业决策（位置服务、市场份额）中的多类移动性分析任务，最终在精细时空尺度上捕获用户移动动态并提供可操作洞察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动应用产生的实时位置数据虽能解决城市挑战（旅游规划、停车管理、公交优化）和商业问题（位置服务、市场份额分析、行为画像），但缺乏一个从数据平台构建到多场景应用的系统性框架来同时应对这些挑战。

Method: 构建端到端数据平台：包括用例定义、架构设计、模块实现；采用数据匿名化、ETL管道、Google BigQuery和Vertex AI进行数据处理和机器学习；开发基于可复用分析构建块的模块化架构生成数据产品；利用Power BI进行交互式可视化。

Result: 框架能够在精细时空分辨率下捕获用户移动动态，成功支持移动性画像、频繁轨迹挖掘、影响区域分析、交通异常检测和起讫点模式分析等任务。

Conclusion: 该平台为城市规划和商业战略决策提供了可操作的洞察，证明了其在多领域移动性分析中的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Real time location data derived from mobile applications is a powerful tool for addressing various urban challenges, including tourism planning, parking management, bus route optimization, and resource allocation. Besides, it offers invaluable insights for shaping strategic decisions in commercial domains such as location based services, market share analysis, and behavioral profiling. In this expansive study, we aim to address all of the aforementioned challenges by investigating the behaviors and patterns of smartphone users within urban environments, particularly in the domains of tourism, transportation, and retail. Our approach encompasses the development of a sophisticated data platform from inception to implementation, which includes the formulation of use cases, architectural design, and implementation of modules. We employ state of the art techniques and technologies, including data anonymization, ETL pipelines, and utilizing Google BigQuery and Vertex AI for data processing and machine learning model development. A modular architecture based on reusable analytical building blocks was developed to generate data products that support multiple stakeholder driven use cases. Additionally, we apply interactive data visualization techniques via Power BI to facilitate the effective interpretation of analytical findings by stakeholders. The developed models address a wide range of mobility analytics tasks, including mobility profiling, frequent trajectory mining, area of influence analysis, traffic anomaly detection, and origin destination pattern analysis. The results demonstrate the framework's ability to capture user mobility dynamics at fine spatial and temporal resolutions, providing actionable insights for urban planning and strategic business decision making.

</details>


### [366] [How to Avoid Debate: Scalable AI Safety via Doubly-Efficient Interactive Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03561)
*Liyan Chen,Yael Tauman Kalai,Zoe Xi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出无需辩论的单一证明者交互式证明方法，用于AI安全验证，特别是在计算涉及人类判断或外部数据库等预言机访问的情况下。作者针对两类预言机访问场景（鲁棒计算和低阶多项式预言机）给出了双高效的单证明者交互式证明和论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI安全验证方法依赖辩论（debate）机制，但该机制假设两个AI模型能力相等且其中一方诚实，这在现实中难以保证。因此需要探索无需辩论的单一证明者交互式证明方法。

Method: 提出双高效（doubly-efficient）的单证明者交互式证明和论证方法，针对预言机辅助计算（relativizing proofs）的两类场景：（1）计算具有鲁棒性，即输出在少量预言机查询答案错误时不变；（2）预言机为低阶多项式。

Result: 在鲁棒计算场景和低阶多项式预言机场景下，实现了无需辩论的单一证明者交互式验证，表明在结构化或抗噪预言机访问条件下，交互式验证可以不依赖辩论。

Conclusion: 这些结果说明，在预言机访问具有结构化或噪声容忍特性时，即使没有辩论机制，AI对齐验证也是可行的，为AI安全验证提供了新的理论路径。

Abstract: As AI models continue to develop powerful capabilities, it becomes critical that we are able to verify that their output is aligned with our intentions. A recent line of work focuses on verification via debate, a model of interactive proofs where two competing powerful provers, or AI models, debate each other to convince a weak verifier, or a human, of the correctness of their claim. However, debate assumes that the two AI models possess equal abilities and that one of them is truthful, which may not be realistic.
  In this work, we show \emph{how to avoid debate}: we initiate the study of \emph{single-prover} interactive proofs for AI safety. Prior results in single-prover interactive proofs do not immediately carry over to the AI safety setting: for example, they do not work when the computation has access to an oracle, such as to human judgment or an external database such as the web. We present doubly-efficient single-prover interactive proofs and arguments for oracle-aided computations (also known as relativizing proofs), in the settings where (1) the computation is robust, in the sense that the output does not change if at most a small fraction of the answers to oracle queries are incorrect, or (2) the oracle is a low-degree polynomial. These results suggest that interactive verification is possible even without debate, under structured or noise-tolerant oracle access.

</details>


### [367] [Explainable Reinforcement Learning for Adaptive Traffic Signal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03703)
*Dickens Kwesiga,Nishu Choudhary,Angshuman Guin,Michael Hunter*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于实体中心的可解释强化学习框架用于交通信号控制，通过解构交通状态为独立车道实体和相位配置，利用双阶段注意力网络提取关系依赖，并集成动作屏蔽接口保证安全合规，在微观仿真中实现了优于SOTA的延迟最小化，且注意力权重与交通工程原理高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习在交通信号控制中表现优异但缺乏可解释性，导致交通管理机构难以接受、无法合规监管、缺乏运维信任、难以故障排查和微调，亟需在高性能优化与人类可理解的解释性之间搭建桥梁。

Method: 1) 将交通状态解构为高维车道实体和相位时序配置，保留交叉口结构拓扑；2) 采用双阶段注意力网络（顺序多头交叉注意力和自注意力块）提取关系依赖和车道间冲突，生成实时亲和力矩阵量化信号相位对交通流的影响；3) 在PPO管道中集成确定性动作屏蔽接口，阻断无效相位转换以保证安全约束合规。

Result: 在微观仿真环境中，该方法在延迟最小化方面优于现有最先进基线；注意力权重与已建立的交通工程原理精确对齐，提供可审计、可信任的架构。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持高性能优化的同时提供了完全的视觉和分析可解释性，为下一代自适应交通控制系统提供了可部署的解决方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) has emerged as a powerful paradigm for adaptive traffic signal control. However, in safety-critical infrastructure like traffic control, the opaque, black-box nature of deep RL models poses challenges for transportation agency acceptance, regulatory compliance, operational trust, troubleshooting, and fine-tuning. To bridge this gap between high-performance optimization and human-comprehensible interpretability, this effort introduces a novel, explainable entity centric RL framework for safe and transparent traffic signal control. Rather than processing traffic states through monolithic, flat vectors, the proposed architecture disaggregates real-time intersection observations into distinct, high-dimensional lane entities and phase temporal configurations to inherently preserve the structural topology and geometric configurations of the intersection. Relational dependencies and inter-lane conflicts are dynamically extracted via a dual-stage attention network featuring sequential multi-head cross-attention and self-attention blocks. This design yields a real time affinity matrix that quantifies the direct influence of signal phases on specific approach volumes and queues, providing full visual and analytical interpretability. To ensure strict operational reliability, a deterministic action-masking interface is integrated directly into the Proximal Policy Optimization pipeline, explicitly blocking invalid phase transitions to guarantee absolute compliance with established signal timing and safety constraints. Evaluated in a microscopic simulation environment, outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in delay minimization. More importantly, the emergent attention weights align precisely with established traffic engineering principles, offering an auditable, trust-enabling, and deployable architecture for next-generation adaptive traffic control systems.

</details>


### [368] [Online Linear Programming for Multi-Objective Routing in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03948)
*Zixi Chen,Yinyu Ye,Zijie Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于在线线性规划的大语言模型服务路由框架，使用暖启动投影一阶方法跟踪影子价格，在满足毫秒级决策要求的同时，相比启发式方法在端到端延迟、首令牌时间、吞吐量和尾延迟等多项SLO指标上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务中的路由启发式方法与显式服务等级目标（SLO）脱钩，对延迟-吞吐量权衡的控制有限，缺乏可解释的优化目标。

Method: 将路由问题建模为在线线性规划，采用基于影子价格的bid-price控制策略，结合暖启动投影一阶更新方法进行在线影子价格追踪，满足毫秒级决策需求。

Result: 在Vidur模拟器上的实验表明，该方法在端到端延迟、首令牌时间、吞吐量和尾延迟等多个SLO指标上显著优于标准基线方法。

Conclusion: 基于科学的方法（可解释的优化框架）优于基于启发式的方法，为LLM服务路由提供了更系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: We study the online routing problem in large language model serving, where requests arrive sequentially and must be dispatched to parallel decode workers under tight batch-size and KV-cache constraints. Unlike widely used routing heuristics that are not tied to explicit service-level objectives (SLOs) and offer limited control over latency-throughput trade-offs, we introduce a multi-objective optimization framework that formulates routing as an online linear programming with interpretable decision rewards. We apply an efficient bid-price control policy based on the online linear programming that admits requests when their SLO-weighted benefit exceeds their shadow prices. To meet millisecond decision requirements, we develop a warm-started, projected first-order updates that track the evolving dual shadow prices online with predictable runtime. We integrate our router into the Vidur simulator and demonstrate substantial improvements over standard baselines across multiple SLO regimes, including end-to-end latency, time-to-first-token, throughput, and tail performance. A big picture from our result: a science-based approach outperforms others based on heuristics.

</details>


### [369] [A Clustering-Based Framework for Identifying Suspicious Trading Patterns in Capital Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04184)
*Asif Zaman,Romona Magdalene Sarkar,Sabiha Khair Ohi,Iftekharul Mobin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于K-Means++聚类的无监督欺诈检测框架，用于识别金融市场中的操纵行为。在对2012-2024年间约100万条交易记录的分析中，该方法标记出2.02%的可疑交易，其中51.10%为幌骗交易，0.10%为拉高出货，0.55%为内幕交易，1.43%为虚假突破，46.83%未分类，模型轮廓系数为0.561。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市场操纵行为严重损害交易平台的信任度，现有方法缺乏有效的无监督检测手段来识别多种类型的欺诈交易模式。

Method: 采用无监督欺诈检测流程，以K-Means++聚类为核心，结合市场实践启发式阈值对交易进行分类和标记。

Result: 在约100万条交易记录中识别出2.02%的可疑交易，其中幌骗交易占51.10%，拉高出货占0.10%，内幕交易占0.55%，虚假突破占1.43%，另有46.83%未分类；轮廓系数为0.561。

Conclusion: 该无监督聚类方法在缺乏真实标注的情况下仍能有效检测多种市场操纵行为，并通过轮廓系数验证了模型性能。

Abstract: Market manipulation is the dubious practice of manipulating stock prices in order to make a quick profit, which truly degrades confidence on trading platforms. We implemented an unsupervised fraud-detection toolkit that begins with K-Means++ clustering to address this issue. A dataset of roughly one million financial transactions from 2012 to 2024 is used. In order to identify fraudulent trades and categorize them using market practice heuristic thresholds, the study suggests a clustering-based pipeline. The method highlights 2.02% of trades as suspicious where 51.10% clearly indicate spoofing, 0.10% indicate pump and dump, 0.55% indicate insider trading, 1.43% indicate a fake breakout, and 46.83% are unclassified. Despite the lack of ground truth, the model's performance is confirmed by a Silhouette Score of 0.561.

</details>


### [370] [Server-side Anti-cheat in FPS games for Aimbot detection using Deep learning and Machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04336)
*Siddhesh A. Dhinge,Shubham G. Sukum,Harsh S. Ranjane,Ruturajsingh R. Rajput,Jyoti H. Jadhav*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出YAACS系统，利用堆叠LSTM深度学习模型对FPS游戏中的自瞄作弊行为进行服务器端检测，通过分析时间序列数据（瞄准速度、射击次数、目标距离等）和行为数据，在128帧序列上实现了88.6%的准确率和0.97%的误报率，优于决策树基线（96.2%准确率但2.68%误报率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着FPS游戏中自瞄、透视、加速等作弊手段日益泛滥，破坏了游戏的公平性和玩家体验，亟需一种高效、低误报的服务器端作弊检测方法来维护游戏环境。

Method: YAACS系统由解析器、深度学习模型和中间连接工具组成。采用堆叠LSTM（长短期记忆网络）结合全连接层，输入序列长度为128帧（负例56帧，正例24帧），提取的时间序列特征包括瞄准速度、射击次数、目标距离等，行为特征包括道具使用、玩家移动等游戏模式数据。

Result: 堆叠LSTM+Dense层模型在128帧序列上达到88.6%分类准确率和0.97%误报率（FPR），决策树基线准确率96.2%但FPR为2.68%，后者误报率是LSTM最优配置的2.76倍。

Conclusion: 通过序列建模引入时间上下文信息对于降低FPS作弊检测中的误报率至关重要，YAACS在准确率和极低误报率之间取得了更优的平衡，更适合实际部署。

Abstract: Modern video games are becoming more complex day by day. Most of these modern games are multiplayer first-person shooter (FPS) games. The rising popularity of FPS games emphasizes the need to combat cheating for fair and enjoyable gaming. As the number of players using cheating techniques like aimbots, wallhacks, and speed hacks is also increasing, we need a way to detect players who are using cheating tools to gain an unfair advantage over regular players. In this system, we focus exclusively on detecting aimbot cheats. Players who use aimbot cheats generally do not prioritize other aspects of the game. To distinguish between regular and cheating players, we identify specific features encompassing time-series data such as aim velocity, number of shots, distance to target, and more, along with behavioral data such as utility usage, player movement, and other gameplay patterns. Utilizing these features, we construct a server-side aimbot detection classifier named 'YAACS'. YAACS comprises a parser, a deep learning model, and intermediary connection utilities designed for integration with the game server. The proposed system achieves a classification accuracy of 88.6% with a false positive rate of 0.97% using a Stacked LSTM with Dense layers trained on sequences of 128 ticks (Tick Delta Negative=56, Tick Delta Positive=24), outperforming the Decision Tree baseline which achieves a higher accuracy of 96.2% but at a false positive rate of 2.68%, 2.76x worse than the best LSTM configuration. These results demonstrate that incorporating temporal context through sequence modelling is critical for minimising false accusations in FPS cheat detection.

</details>


### [371] [Detecting Answer-Driven Reasoning in LLM-Based Educational Tutors via Truncated Chain-of-Thought Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04572)
*Bonan Shen,Dingyan Shang,Youting Wang,Tao Ning*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE方法（截断推理AUC评估），用于检测LLM辅导解释是否依赖私有答案信息而非真正的推理过程。在GSM8K数学题上，答案键访问使TRACE AUC中位数从0.375升至0.900，表明解释过程的早期阶段即可获得正确答案，揭示了"答案驱动"的推理现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实辅导系统中，LLM可能同时访问学生问题与教师答案键等私有信息，导致生成的解释看似正确但实际上是"答案驱动"的——在推理过程尚未合理解释前，最终答案已在行为上可用。需要一种轻量级过程级诊断方法来检测这种虚假推理。

Method: 使用TRACE（截断推理AUC评估）：在模型生成的逐步解释的不同前缀位置，强制模型立即给出答案并通过验证器检查正确性。在三种对比设置下（仅问题、正确答案键、错误答案键）对1000个GSM8K问题使用Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct进行评估。

Result: 答案键访问使TRACE AUC中位数从0.375（仅问题）提高到0.900；在997/1000个案例中，前10%解释前缀即可获得正确答案；该效应在仅问题和答案键都能得到正确答案的746个样本上依然显著。

Conclusion: TRACE可作为轻量级过程级审计工具，用于检测数学辅导解释中的答案驱动推理现象，揭示了LLM在拥有答案信息时可能产生"假性推理"的风险。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) tutors often produce fluent step-by-step explanations, but a correct and pedagogically formatted response does not guarantee that the answer was derived from the student-facing problem. In realistic tutoring systems, the model may also have access to teacher notes, answer keys, rubrics, or retrieved solution artifacts. We study whether such private answer information can make tutor explanations answer-driven: the final answer is behaviorally available before the written explanation has justified it. Using Truncated Reasoning AUC Evaluation (TRACE), which probes how early a chain-of-thought prefix can pass a verifier, we evaluate 1000 GSM8K test problems under three paired tutoring contexts: question-only, correct answer-key, and wrong answer-key. At fixed fractions of each generated explanation, we force the model to answer immediately and verify the response against the gold numeric answer. With Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, answer-key access raises median TRACE AUC from 0.375 to 0.900 and makes the gold answer available at the first 10% prefix in 997 of 1000 cases. The effect remains strong on the 746 examples where both question-only and answer-key explanations end with the correct answer. These results support truncated CoT auditing as a lightweight process-level diagnostic for answer-driven reasoning in math tutoring explanations.

</details>


### [372] [Rethinking On-Policy Self-Distillation for Thinking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05184)
*Simran Kaur,Narutatsu Ri,Yinghui He,Liam Fowl,Sanjeev Arora*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 特权自蒸馏会降低思考模型在长推理链上的性能，最高导致avg@16准确率下降17%。这种现象源于特权教师上下文重塑了高熵分叉位置的学习，降低分叉率，抑制自我纠正和回溯行为，且与推理预算规模正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索自蒸馏（self-distillation）在思考模型（thinking models）上的有效性，特别是当教师模型拥有特权信息（如问题答案）时，是否能通过测试时推理来提升学生模型的表现。

Method: 在Qwen3和OLMo系列的5个思考模型上，在AIME24、AIME25、HMMT25基准上比较特权上下文蒸馏（privileged-context distillation）与标准蒸馏的效果；分析分叉率（fork rate）、自我纠正标记、回溯标记等token级信号变化；对比on-policy蒸馏（OPD）与特权OPD的效果差异。

Result: 特权自蒸馏导致avg@16准确率相对下降最高17%，退化程度与特权上下文保留量正相关，在长推理预算下最明显。特权OPD逆转了常规OPD带来的提升。特权上下文降低思考模型的分叉率但指令模型不受影响，并且减少验证、回溯和 hedging 标记的使用。

Conclusion: 特权自蒸馏对强思考模型有害，尤其是在长推理链上。改进自蒸馏需要关注token级信号，特别是围绕修正和推理步骤的信号设计，避免特权上下文压制模型的内在探索和纠正能力。

Abstract: Self-distillation is a promising recipe for self-improvement in language models. In this setting, a model can serve as its own teacher when given privileged information, such as a solution to a math problem. This seems especially appealing for thinking models, which can use test-time reasoning to absorb the privileged information. Surprisingly, we show that privileged self-distillation degrades thinking models on long reasoning traces: across five Qwen3 and OLMo thinking models evaluated on AIME24, AIME25, and HMMT25, privileged-context distillation causes a relative drop of up to 17% in avg@16 accuracy. The degradation scales with the amount of privileged context withheld from the student and is most pronounced at long rollout budgets, where thinking models otherwise obtain their largest gains. This failure mode is not specific to self-distillation: on-policy distillation (OPD) improves thinking models, but privileged OPD reverses these gains. Our diagnostics link this failure mode to how privileged teacher context reshapes learning at high-entropy forking positions, where multiple continuations remain plausible and may lead to different reasoning paths. Privileged context lowers fork rates in thinking-model rollouts but not in instruction-model rollouts. This leads to an interesting dichotomy, where privileged context can help instruction-tuned models but hurts stronger thinking models. The effect is visible when the student begins a self-correction branch, where privileged OPD penalizes sampled reconsideration tokens that vanilla OPD supports. Thinking models trained with a privileged teacher produce fewer verification, backtracking, and hedging markers, even after length normalization. These findings indicate that self-distillation for strong thinking models requires attention to token-level signal, especially around correction and reasoning steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [373] [Fine-Grained Computation Offload for Off-the-Shelf Servers in Tens of Lines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02630)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需重写服务器代码即可实现硬件加速器卸载与请求处理重叠执行的方法，通过利用服务器已有的并发机制（请求挂起/恢复）将卸载任务路由到执行器，仅需修改22-138行代码即可实现1.2-5.4倍性能提升，并分析了该方法的原子性破坏问题及保护机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件加速器（GPU、FPGA、HSM等）在微秒到毫秒级细粒度卸载场景中，传统应对停顿的方法（上下文切换成本高、忙等待浪费核心）均失效。现有解决方案需重写异步框架、更换运行时或数据平面操作系统，工作量大且侵入性强。

Method: 观察到服务器在处理并发请求时天然具备挂起和恢复机制，因此将重叠问题转化为路由问题：将硬件卸载提交给执行器，利用服务器已有的延迟响应原语挂起请求，卸载完成后恢复请求。在10个现成服务器上仅需22-138行代码修改（最多改1个文件）即可实现。极限情况下，通过LD_PRELOAD的纤程运行时可在未修改的线程-连接二进制中注入路由（17.3倍提升）。

Result: 在真实硬件上实现1.2-5.4倍加速；服务器的并发模型和卸载开销可提前预测加速比；性能上限受限于设备吞吐量和服务器自身的重叠容量。LD_PRELOAD方法在未修改二进制上实现17.3倍加速。

Conclusion: 利用服务器已有并发机制实现硬件卸载重叠是可行的，仅需少量修改而非重写；但该方法会破坏run-to-completion原子性，通过测量分类确定了危险仅限于未加锁的共享聚合数据，并设计了透明页面保护检测器来防护（已在Redis上验证）。

Abstract: Hardware accelerators now sit on the critical path of online serving. GPUs, FPGAs, and increasingly remote services such as hardware security modules, post-quantum KEMs, and inference servers. For fine-grained offloads (microseconds to a few milliseconds) the classic responses to the resulting stall both fail: a context switch costs as much as the offload, and a busy-wait burns the core. Overlapping the offload with other requests is the fix, and prior systems obtain it by adding concurrency: an async-framework rewrite, a new runtime or dataplane OS, or a hand-tuned point integration.
  We observe that the concurrency already exists: serving concurrent requests is suspending and resuming them, so every server ships the machinery overlap needs. Overlap is then a routing problem, not a rewrite problem: submit the offload to an executor, suspend the request with the server's own deferred-response primitive, resume it on completion. Across ten off-the-shelf servers spanning every production concurrency model, this recipe takes 22-138 lines added, at most one modified, and recovers 1.2-5.4x on real hardware; the server's concurrency model and the offload's weight predict both numbers in advance, and the win is bounded by device throughput and the server's own overlap capacity. At the limit, an LD_PRELOAD fiber runtime injects the reroute into an unmodified thread-per-connection binary (17.3x) within a characterized envelope. Rerouting suspends run-to-completion atomicity; a measured taxonomy confines the hazard to unlocked shared aggregates, and a transparent page-protection detector guards exactly those, validated on stock Redis.

</details>


### [374] [Cross-IP Request Coalescing: Relocating the Fan-out Point in Virtualized I/O](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02969)
*Kiseok Kim,Hyeontae Joo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文识别出虚拟化存储I/O路径中开销的主要来源是每设备提交结构本身，而非内核I/O路径，并提出跨IP请求合并机制，将扇出点从客户机移至SPDK vhost-user后端，用单次提交替代多次往返，在SPDK虚拟化环境中实现了最高1.78倍的延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据密集型AI工作负载规模增长，虚拟化层存储I/O路径性能成为关键瓶颈。单个用户请求需跨越多个IP块，每次虚拟化设备访问都导致独立的客户机与后端往返和上下文切换，累积开销随设备数量增长，亟需优化。

Method: 提出跨IP请求合并方法：将扇出点从客户机侧重定位到SPDK vhost-user后端。客户机将多设备I/O作为单一复合请求提交，后端加速bdev分解并分发至各目标设备，将多次往返缩减为单次提交。

Result: 在SPDK虚拟化环境中，相比每设备基线方案，延迟最高降低1.78倍，且收益随并发度提高而增大。

Conclusion: 跨IP请求合并通过消除多设备场景下的冗余往返开销，能有效提升虚拟化环境中AI工作负载的存储I/O性能。

Abstract: Cloud data centers rely on virtualization technologies to serve AI workloads in multi-tenant environments. With the growing scale of data-intensive AI workloads, the performance of storage I/O paths at the virtualization layer has become a critical factor. A single user request often crosses multiple IP blocks, where functional units such as storage, GPU, and accelerator devices under virtualization fan out into separate stack traversals between the guest and the backend. As a result, round-trip and context-switching overheads accumulate with the number of devices. In this letter, we identify that a dominant factor in this overhead lies not in the kernel-mediated I/O path alone, but in the per-device submission structure itself, which persists even in user-space, kernel-bypass storage frameworks such as SPDK. To address this, we propose cross-IP request coalescing, which relocates the fan-out point from the guest to the SPDK vhost-user backend. The guest submits multi-device I/O as a single compound request, and the accelerated bdev at the backend decomposes and dispatches it to each target device, replacing multiple per-device guest-backend round trips with a single submission. Evaluation in an SPDK-based virtualized environment shows that the proposed approach achieves up to 1.78x lower latency than the per-device baseline, with the benefit growing as concurrency increases.

</details>


### [375] [GLM-5 Serving Parameter Tuning for OpenClaw: Single-Deployment MaaS Inference Optimization for Long-Context Agent Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02518)
*Minjie Hua,Ning Wang,Peijun Yang,Kai Wang,Shiguo Lian*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对OpenClaw工作负载（长前缀、工具增强的请求，约28k-30k输入token/500输出token），在MaaS多模型推理优化架构的单节点优化模块中，对GLM-5的chunked prefill、TP、PP和请求并发度进行调参实验。最优配置为chunked-prefill-size=3072, tp=4, pp=4, max-running-requests=24，相比基线吞吐量提升11.6%，TTFT降低25.5%，P90延迟降低18.9%，对应服务成本降低约10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OpenClaw请求以长工具增强前缀（含系统提示、对话历史、工具输出）为主，服务质量受吞吐量、TTFT和尾延迟共同制约，需要针对该特定工作负载进行系统化的服务参数调优以降低部署成本。

Method: 在MaaS多模型推理优化架构的单节点优化模块中，对GLM-5的四个关键服务参数（chunked prefill size、tensor parallelism、pipeline parallelism、max running requests）进行网格搜索调参。实验在两节点十六GPU集群上进行，评估指标包括请求吞吐量、token吞吐量、TTFT和P90延迟。

Result: 最优配置(3072/4/4/24)相比基线(2048/4/4/16)：请求吞吐量从0.43提升至0.48 req/s(+11.6%)，token吞吐量从9029.64提升至9993.23 tok/s(+10.7%)，平均TTFT从8.98降至6.69s(-25.5%)，P90延迟从40.23降至32.64s(-18.9%)，估计服务成本降低约10%。

Conclusion: 最优参数具有工作负载特异性，更大的chunk size和更深的排队并非单调提升性能。推荐3072/tp4/pp4/max24作为OpenClaw默认部署配置。

Abstract: OpenClaw requests are dominated by long, tool-augmented prefixes, including system prompts, conversation history, and tool outputs fed back into the context window. For this workload, with about 28k-30k input tokens and 500 output tokens per request, serving quality is governed by throughput, TTFT, and tail latency rather than short-prompt throughput alone.
  This report studies GLM-5 serving-parameter tuning within a MaaS multi-model inference optimization architecture. The scope is the Single-Node Optimization block of the inference-optimization layer, where chunked prefill, tensor parallelism (TP), pipeline parallelism (PP), and request concurrency are tuned for one GLM-5 serving deployment; in this report, "Single-Node Optimization" denotes the architecture block, while experiments run on a two-node, sixteen-GPU cluster.
  Within the tested space, the best configuration is chunked-prefill-size=3072, tp=4, pp-size=4, and max-running-requests=24. Compared with the conservative 2048/4/4/16 baseline, it increases request throughput from 0.43 to 0.48 req/s and total token throughput from 9029.64 to 9993.23 tok/s, while reducing average TTFT from 8.98 to 6.69 s and latency P90 from 40.23 to 32.64 s. Under the same hardware footprint, this corresponds to an estimated 10.4% lower serving cost per request and 9.6% lower cost per token. The results show that the optimum is workload-specific: larger chunk sizes and deeper queueing do not monotonically improve performance. We therefore recommend 3072 / tp4 / pp4 / max24 as the default OpenClaw deployment profile.

</details>


### [376] [Static PTX Metrics Track Structural Kernel Regressions but Miss Semantic Ones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02541)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过对比五个GPU类上的静态PTX指标和运行时测量，发现静态PTX差异可有效区分结构性和语义性bug：结构性bug改变内核工作量，在PTX中可见；语义性bug（如常量替换）在PTX中不可见。测量到的运行时性能差异对硬件和噪声敏感，不能替代静态分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以区分GPU内核中的结构性bug（改变工作量）和语义性bug（仅改变语义），运行时测量受硬件和噪声影响大，需要一种可移植的预过滤方法。

Method: 将每个GPU内核的静态PTX指标（寄存器、溢出、指令数）与CUDA事件计时运行时测量配对，在RTX 3060、A10、L40S、A100 SXM4、H100 NVL五类GPU上实验。分析（正确，有bug）配对间的Delta-regs、Delta-instrs和Delta-perf%。

Result: Delta-regs和Delta-instrs在五个GPU上完全一致；Delta-perf%则不同。结构性bug（如gelu_triton_buggy去掉前导0.5因子）在静态信号中明显（移除8条指令和8个寄存器），而运行时Delta-perf%仅+3.2%，在噪声带内。语义性bug（如softmax_triton_buggy将-inf替换为0.0）编译为字节相同的PTX，静态信号不可见。

Conclusion: 对于该语料库和工具链，静态PTX差异门控是一种可移植的预过滤方法，可分离结构性变更和语义性变更；在此规模下，测量运行时差异对硬件和噪声敏感，不能替代静态分析。

Abstract: We pair each GPU kernel's static PTX metrics (registers, spills, instruction count) with CUDA-event-timed runtime on five GPU classes: RTX 3060, A10, L40S, A100 SXM4, and H100 NVL. In this corpus and toolchain the static and measured signals separate cleanly along one axis. Per-pair Delta-regs and Delta-instrs are identical across all five GPUs for any given (correct, buggy) pair. Measured Delta-perf% is not. Structural bugs that change the kernel's work are unambiguous in the static signal. The gelu_triton_buggy variant, which drops a leading 0.5 factor, removes 8 instructions and 8 registers. The corresponding measured Delta-perf% on RTX 3060 is +3.2%, within the run-to-run noise band at the sub-millisecond scale these corpus kernels occupy. Semantic bugs that swap one constant for another are invisible to the static signal. The softmax_triton_buggy variant, which substitutes other=0.0 for -inf on the masked load, compiles to byte-identical PTX. The paper's bounded claim is that, for this corpus and toolchain, a static-PTX delta gate is a portable pre-filter that separates structural from semantic changes; measured runtime deltas at this scale are hardware- and noise-sensitive and are not a substitute.

</details>


### [377] [Kotlin-MP: DSL and IR Transformer for Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02519)
*Ruikai Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Kotlin-MP是一个Kotlin编译器插件，通过领域特定语言（DSL）和中间表示（IR）转换器，在编译时将并行循环结构直接降级为优化的ForkJoinPool任务，实现低开销的硬件并行，避免了传统任务并行库的运行时开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kotlin提供了协程等异步并发抽象，但缺乏低开销、指令式硬件并行构造。开发者使用标准任务并行库进行数学循环并行化时，面临lambda分配、状态机生成等运行时开销，且难以保证可读性、可靠性和可维护性。

Method: 提出Kotlin-MP，包含一个原生Kotlin DSL（提供类似OpenMP的语法，用于标记并行区域、调度和临界区）和一个自定义IR转换器（作为Kotlin编译器插件）。IR转换器在编译时拦截DSL，进行代码结构重写，直接将并行化部分降级为优化的java.util.concurrent.ForkJoinPool任务，自动管理循环分块、线程调度，并注入硬件同步和互斥。

Result: 该方法在编译时完成并行转换，避免了运行时包装器的开销；自动管理循环分块、线程调度和同步互斥，提高了代码的可读性和可维护性。

Conclusion: Kotlin-MP通过编译时IR转换，为Kotlin提供了低开销的硬件并行能力，解决了传统任务并行库在计算密集型数学循环中的性能开销和可维护性问题。

Abstract: While Kotlin provides exceptional abstractions for asynchronous concurrency (like Coroutines for I/O), it lacks low-overhead, directive-based constructs for true hardware parallelism. When developers attempt to parallelize compute-bound mathematical loops using standard task-parallel libraries, they encounter significant runtime overhead such as lambda allocations and state-machine generation due to the abstraction layers, and face difficulties in ensuring readability, reliability and maintainability in implementing them.
  To address this, this project proposes Kotlin-MP, a native Kotlin Domain-Specific Language (DSL) paired with a custom Intermediate Representation (IR) transformer as a Kotlin compiler plugin. The DSL provides an intuitive, OpenMP-like syntax for developers to denote parallel regions, schedules, and critical sections. Instead of acting as a standard runtime wrapper, the IR transformer intercepts this DSL at compile-time and structurally rewrites the code. It lowers the parallelized sections directly into optimized \textit{java.util.concurrent.ForkJoinPool} tasks, automatically managing loop chunking, performing thread scheduling, and injecting hardware synchronization and mutual exclusion.

</details>


### [378] [Tile-Level Activation Overlap for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02521)
*Abhinav Jangda,Tyler Sorensen,Sebastian Burckhardt,Jianlan YE,Chaoyin Li,Atul Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了两种基于CUTLASS的SM90融合核函数，将SwiGLU激活函数与GeMM在tile级别融合，在H100上实现高达2.47倍加速，达到79.5%的BF16峰值利用率，且数值精度优于cuBLAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SwiGLU是现代大语言模型中主流的MLP激活函数，但其中间张量物化过程占用了MLP执行时间的9-37%，导致显著的内存开销和性能瓶颈。

Method: 提出了两种互补的CUTLASS-based SM90核函数：(1) Kernel-1利用Pingpong warp-specialized调度，在Gate累加器上重叠Swish计算与Up-tile加载；(2) Kernel-2通过自定义Epilogue Visitor Tree将SwiGLU与tile存储交错执行。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5模型(0.5B-72B)和NVIDIA H100上，核函数相比PyTorch实现最高2.47倍加速，将工作负载从内存受限转为计算受限，达到79.5%的BF16峰值利用率。torch.compile无法复现此融合（慢3-7倍），cuBLAS存在4.5-11%的数值不匹配而融合核函数零不匹配。

Conclusion: 验证了手写tile级别融合设计的必要性，证明通过精细的算子融合可以有效消除SwiGLU中间张量物化开销，实现更高的性能和数值精度。

Abstract: SwiGLU is the dominant MLP activation in modern large language models, yet its intermediate tensor materialization costs 9-37% of MLP execution time. We present two complementary CUTLASS-based SM90 kernels that fuse SwiGLU into GeMM at the tile level. Kernel-1 overlaps Swish computation on the Gate accumulator with Up-tile loading using the Pingpong warp-specialized schedule; Kernel-2 interleaves SwiGLU with tile stores via a custom Epilogue Visitor Tree. Evaluated on Qwen-2.5 models (0.5B-72B) on NVIDIA H100, our kernels achieve up to 2.47x speedup over PyTorch, shifting workloads from memory-bound to compute-bound and reaching 79.5% peak BF16 utilization. We demonstrate that torch.compile cannot replicate this fusion (3-7x slower than our kernels), validating the need for hand-crafted tile-level design. Our fused kernels are also numerically superior, achieving zero mismatches compared to 4.5-11% for cuBLAS.

</details>


### [379] [Multi-Turn Distributed Inference with Mixture of Experts for 6G Edge--Cloud Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02522)
*Bo Liu,Haiyuan Li,Yuelin Liu,Yulei Wu,Rasheed Hussain,Shadi Moazzeni,Dimitra Simeonidou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: StateFlow是一种针对6G边缘云网络中MoE架构的分布式推理策略，通过将持久KV状态与瞬态稀疏计算解耦，在保持KV状态局部性的同时实现弹性专家调度，实验表明在多轮推理中稳定对话并发量提升2倍以上，p95延迟降低53.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构在边缘云网络中推理时，KV状态局部性与弹性专家调度之间存在根本性矛盾：KV状态迁移开销大，而专家计算需要分散利用网络容量，多轮推理进一步放大了这一矛盾。

Method: 提出StateFlow分布式推理策略，核心思路是将持久KV状态固定在"粘性"服务站点以实现跨轮复用，同时联合优化专家调度和聚合放置位置，实现KV状态与稀疏计算的解耦。

Result: 在真实测试环境中，StateFlow相比分布式基线方案，稳定对话并发量提升2倍以上，多轮推理的p95延迟降低53.0%。

Conclusion: StateFlow通过解耦KV状态与专家计算，有效解决了边缘云网络中MoE推理的局部性与弹性矛盾，显著提升了多轮推理的性能和并发能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are increasingly deployed across 6G edge--cloud networks, where sparse activation reduces the computational footprint of each inference to only a fraction of the full expert set. However, MoE inference in edge-cloud networks creates a tension between KV state locality and elastic expert dispatch. KV state relocation incurs substantial transfer overhead, while expert computation benefits from spreading across the network to exploit available capacity. This tension is amplified in multi-turn inference, where each turn extends the KV state that must persist across the dialogue. To this end, we present StateFlow, a distributed inference policy that decouples persistent KV state from transient sparse computation. StateFlow pins KV state at a sticky serving site for cross-turn reuse and jointly optimizes expert dispatch and aggregation placement across the network. We further implement a real-world testbed with kernel-level network emulation and experimental results show that StateFlow sustains more than $2\times$ higher stable dialogue concurrency than the distributed baseline solutions and reduces turn-level p95 latency under multi-turn inference by 53.0\%.

</details>


### [380] [Performance evaluation of scheduling tasks in many-core systems utilizing processes and threads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04821)
*Mejgan Dedaj,Argyro Gailla,Theofanis Ioannou,Stamatia Kastrinaki,Hermione Kimpouropoulou,Dimitrios Kontodimos,Kleopatra Kontogianni,Sotirios Kontogiannis,Michail Panagiotidis Kannas,Anastasia Papouda,Anna Maria Sidiropoulou,George Tavridis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究评估了基于进程和基于线程的调度器在多核共享内存系统上的可扩展性，使用内存密集型的行式快速排序任务处理大型三维张量。实验表明，线程调度器性能最优，动态和引导式调度效果最好；进程调度器中，基于管道的设计可扩展性最强，小负载用一对一管道，大负载用多对多管道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估进程和线程两种调度范式在多核共享内存系统上处理内存密集型工作负载时的可扩展性，为异构工作负载的管理提供依据。

Method: 以内存密集型的行式快速排序为负载，在24核x86-64平台上分别评测进程调度器（有界繁衍、有界集体、三种管道式生产者-消费者调度器）和线程调度器（静态、动态、引导、分块、分块窃取、自适应块、AIMD自适应调度）。

Result: 线程调度器整体性能最优，动态和引导式调度实际效果最好。进程调度器中管道式设计可扩展性最强，小负载一对一管道最优，大负载多对多管道更优。

Conclusion: 轻量级线程调度是共享内存行排序的最佳选择；AIMD/自适应调度和管道式进程调度在争用感知执行、显式进程间协调和分布式风格异构工作负载管理中仍有价值。

Abstract: This study assesses the scalability of process-based and thread-based schedulers for many-core shared-memory systems using a memory-intensive row-wise quick-sort workload on large three-dimensional tensors. The process-based evaluation considers bounded prolific, bounded collective, and three pipe-based producer-consumer schedulers: one-to-one, one-to-many, and many-to-many. These pipe schedulers dynamically stream task identifiers to worker processes, exchanging increased inter-process communication overhead for enhanced runtime load balancing and flexible chunk-based task dispatching. The thread-based evaluation examines static, dynamic, guided, chunk-based, chunk-stealing, adaptive chunk, and AIMD adaptive scheduling strategies. The AIMD scheduler employs an additive-increase multiplicative-decrease policy inspired by TCP congestion control, utilizing an exponentially weighted moving average (EWMA) of CPU utilization to regulate a contention window that limits the number of concurrently active chunks. The adaptive chunk scheduler further modifies chunk size based on observed per-thread execution speed. Experimental results on a 24-core x86-64 platform indicate that thread schedulers deliver the highest overall performance, with dynamic and guided scheduling yielding the most favorable practical outcomes. Among process schedulers, pipe-based designs demonstrate the strongest scalability, with one-to-one pipes excelling for smaller workloads and many-to-many pipes preferred for larger workloads. In summary, lightweight thread scheduling is optimal for shared-memory row sorting, while AIMD/adaptive scheduling and pipe-based process scheduling remain valuable for contention-aware execution, explicit inter-process coordination, and distributed-style heterogeneous workload management.

</details>


### [381] [Edge-Deployable LLM Fine-Tuning on a Single GPU for Telecom Network Troubleshooting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02523)
*Chenhua Shi,Bhavika Jalli,John Zou,Gregor Macdonald,Wanlu Lei,Mridul Jain,Joji Philip*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文在电信边缘场景下，针对单GPU加速器使用Unsloth框架对LLM进行微调的系统性性能分析研究，探讨了最大序列长度、内存利用率、LoRA秩、生成数量等参数对训练稳定性和资源效率的影响，并比较了推理型与非推理型模型架构在SFT和RFT中的行为差异，为边缘环境下的资源感知型LLM微调提供了实用配置指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电信边缘站点故障排查需要低延迟模型响应和本地化模型适配，但受限于边缘站点有限的功耗、冷却、空间和GPU预算，以及RAN流量导致的GPU利用率低下和ROI不佳等问题，单GPU微调成为可扩展边缘AI部署的实用需求。

Method: 使用Unsloth框架在单一边缘级加速器上进行LLM微调的GPU性能分析，系统研究最大序列长度、GPU内存利用率、LoRA秩、生成数量等参数的影响，并分析KV缓存使用、激活内存开销及编译器优化下的运行时稳定性，对比推理型与非推理型模型在SFT和RFT中的差异。实验采用电信故障排查数据集（问答对+top-3检索文档）。

Result: 提供了电信边缘环境中稳定、高效、资源感知的LLM微调实用配置指南，揭示了不同模型架构在SFT和RFT中的行为差异，以及各参数对训练稳定性和资源效率的具体影响。

Conclusion: 单GPU微调是电信边缘场景下可行的部署策略，通过合理配置序列长度、LoRA秩等参数可在资源受限条件下实现稳定的模型微调，不同架构模型在微调过程中表现出的差异需要通过针对性配置加以优化。

Abstract: Telecom troubleshooting at edge sites requires low-latency model responses and localized model adaptation to satisfy operational and data sovereignty requirements. However, deploying large language models (LLMs) at telecom edge sites is constrained by limited power, cooling, space, and weight budgets for GPU infrastructure. These challenges are further amplified by human-patterned Radio Access Network (RAN) traffic that often results in low GPU utilization and poor return on investment, as well as by architectural mismatches between deterministic ASIC-based telecom processing and GPU-oriented AI workloads. Consequently, single-GPU fine-tuning becomes a practical requirement for scalable edge AI deployment rather than merely a resource limitation. This paper presents a GPU profiling study of LLM fine-tuning using the Unsloth framework on a single edge-class accelerator. We systematically analyze the effects of maximum sequence length, GPU memory utilization, Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) rank, and generation count on training stability and resource efficiency. We further investigate trade-offs in KV cache usage, activation memory overhead, and runtime stability under inductor compilation. In addition, we show that reasoning and non-reasoning model architectures exhibit substantially different behaviors during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) because of differences in chat template structures, reasoning tags, and control flags. Experiments are conducted on a telecom troubleshooting dataset consisting of question-answer pairs augmented with top-3 retrieved contextual documents. The results provide practical configuration guidelines for stable, efficient, and resource-aware LLM fine-tuning in telecom edge environments.

</details>


### [382] [TARE: Tail Aware Evaluation of HPC Job Runtime Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04935)
*Haili Xiao,Can Wu,Shasha Lu,Xiaoning Wang,Yining Zhao,Rong He*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向HPC调度尾延迟的作业运行时预测评估方法（GeoAccuracy+分位数分析），发现用户提交的预估（UserReq）在top decile表现最佳，并据此设计混合调度策略（XGBoost+UserReq），在线回放将平均等待时间降低8%，回填作业增加50%-115%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC作业运行时预测评估多采用均值指标，但在重尾负载下少量作业主导资源消耗，均值误导评估结果；需要一种聚焦尾部的评估方法来更好地刻画预测质量与调度性能的关系。

Method: 1) 提出尾部聚焦的评估框架：GeoAccuracy（资源使用加权）、十分位数分析和滚动分割分析；2) 在NREL Eagle和ALCF Mira/Intrepid三个生产数据集上对比XGBoost、Last2和UserReq；3) 基于评估发现设计混合调度策略：proxy_cost判定后，top decile使用UserReq，其余使用XGBoost；4) 在线回放验证。

Result: 1) GeoAccuracy比MeanAccuracy更清晰区分方法优劣，揭示UserReq在尾部的优势；2) UserReq在top decile取得最高GeoAccuracy和最低低估率，跨数据集一致；3) 混合策略在线回放：平均等待时间降低8%，回填作业增加50%-115%。

Conclusion: 尾部聚焦的离线评估能更好地表征与调度相关的预测质量，并有效指导调度策略设计。

Abstract: Runtime estimates affect reservation quality, backfilling opportunities, and queue delay in HPC schedulers. Under heavy tailed workloads, however, averaging over jobs can misrepresent scheduling impact because a small fraction of jobs dominates resource usage. This paper presents an empirical evaluation methodology for HPC job runtime prediction that focuses on the tail, combining GeoAccuracy weighted by resource usage with decile and split analyses. Using production traces from NREL Eagle and ALCF Mira/Intrepid, we compare XGBoost and Last2 against the user provided walltime estimate at submission (UserReq). Across all three datasets, evaluation focused on the tail changes the offline conclusion: MeanAccuracy keeps the methods relatively close, whereas GeoAccuracy reveals clearer separation and makes UserReq's strength in the upper tail visible. In the top decile, UserReq achieves the highest GeoAccuracy and lowest underestimation rate on all three datasets, and this pattern remains stable across rolling splits. We then translate this signal into a simple hybrid scheduling policy that keeps XGBoost for most jobs and routes the top decile by proxy_cost at submission to UserReq. Online replay on four production queues reduces mean wait time by up to 8% and increases backfilled jobs by 50% to 115%. These results show that offline evaluation focused on the tail better characterizes prediction quality relevant to scheduling and informs scheduling policy design.

</details>


### [383] [SWIFT: Spatio-temporal Wavelet Integrated Forecasting Framework for Workload Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02524)
*Zeyuan Ding,Lingfeng Zheng,Dian Ding,Guangtao Xue*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出SWIFT框架，一个纯卷积架构，通过可学习级联小波路径(Learnable Cascaded Wavelet Path)将传统固定小波基转化为自适应卷积算子，配合多变量交互模块(Multivariate Interaction Module)建模变量间空间依赖和特征内交互，实现高精度云工作负载预测，达到SOTA精度且具有线性复杂度O(L)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云工作负载预测对资源管理至关重要，但工作负载具有高波动性和突发性；传统小波方法虽能保留时间局部性，但固定基难以处理复杂模式，且孤立处理各变量忽略了关键的空间依赖关系。

Method: 提出SWIFT纯卷积框架：(1) 可学习级联小波路径(LCWP)，将传统固定小波基重构为可学习卷积算子，实现数据驱动的自适应特征剥离；(2) 多变量交互模块(MIM)，顺序建模变量间空间交互和变量内特征交互，以稳定和细化噪声工作负载状态。

Result: SWIFT达到SOTA精度，具有线性O(L)复杂度，预测误差最高降低31.04%，延迟降低79.74%。

Conclusion: 可学习级联小波路径结合多变量交互模块的纯卷积架构能有效应对云工作负载的高波动性和突发性，在精度和效率上均显著超越现有方法。

Abstract: Accurate cloud workload forecasting is pivotal for efficient resource management but remains challenging as workloads are highly volatile and prone to sudden bursts. Although wavelets preserve temporal locality, rigid fixed bases struggle with complex patterns and isolated processing neglects critical spatial dependencies. To address this, we propose SWIFT, a pure convolutional framework designed for high-efficiency workload forecasting. We introduce a Learnable Cascaded Wavelet Path that reformulates the traditional fixed wavelet bases into adaptive convolutional operators, enabling precise, data-driven feature peeling. Complementing this, our Multivariate Interaction Module sequentially models inter-variable spatial and intra-variable feature interactions to stabilize and refine noisy workload states. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIFT achieves SOTA accuracy with linear O(L) complexity, reducing prediction error by up to 31.04% while cutting latency by 79.74%.

</details>


### [384] [PEEK: Predictive Queue-Informed KV Cache Management for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02525)
*Bing Xie,Zhipeng Wang,Masahiro Tanaka,Zheng Zhen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PEEK是一个轻量级调度和驱逐框架，通过增量基数树在等待队列中暴露前缀共享集群，采用双游走匹配、簇先驱优先调度、协同驱逐钩子和多车道步幅调度器，在SGLang和vLLM上实现最多3.0×缓存命中率提升、7.9×TTFT降低和6.7×端到端延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务引擎未能有效利用等待请求之间的前缀共享结构，导致KV缓存利用率低、调度效率差，从而增加请求延迟并降低吞吐量。

Method: 1) 在待处理队列上维护增量基数树，暴露前缀共享集群；2) 双游走算法将树与引擎前缀缓存匹配，为每个等待请求找到最长前缀匹配；3) 簇先驱优先调度，使兄弟请求继承刚缓存的前缀；4) 协同驱逐钩子保护与排队请求相关的祖先块；5) 多车道步幅调度器限制饥饿现象。

Result: 在SGLang和vLLM上，五个工作负载（最多4×H100, DP=2, TP=2）中，相比各自最强基线，PEEK实现：最多3.0×/2.6×缓存命中率提升，7.9×/7.1×TTFT降低，6.7×/5.5×端到端延迟降低，3.6×/4.5×吞吐量提升；无可利用前缀结构的工作负载上性能与基线持平。

Conclusion: PEEK通过暴露和利用请求间前缀共享结构，在在线LLM服务中显著提升缓存命中率、降低延迟并提高吞吐量，且随KV缓存压力和推理并行度扩展时优势持续保持。

Abstract: We present PEEK, a lightweight scheduling and eviction framework for both online (streaming) and offline (batch) LLM serving; this paper focuses on the online regime. PEEK maintains an incremental radix tree over the pending queue, exposing
  prefix-sharing clusters no existing engine surfaces. A low-overhead dual-walk matches the tree against the engine's prefix cache to yield longest-prefix-match for every waiting request; PEEK then admits cluster pioneers first so siblings
  inherit the freshly cached prefix, a co-designed eviction hook protects blocks ancestral to queued demand, and a multi-lane stride scheduler bounds starvation. On SGLang and vLLM across five workloads up to 4$\times$H100 (DP=2 over TP=2),
  PEEK delivers up to 3.0$\times$/2.6$\times$ cache hit, 7.9$\times$/7.1$\times$ TTFT, 6.7$\times$/5.5$\times$ E2E, and 3.6$\times$/4.5$\times$ throughput gains over each engine's strongest stock baseline (SGLang/vLLM), while matching
  baselines within noise on workloads with no exploitable prefix structure. Wins hold as KV-cache pressure and inference parallelism scale.

</details>


### [385] [Operational Memory Architecture for Kubernetes: Evidence Horizon Taxonomy and Extended Causal Pattern Preservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02528)
*Shamsher Khan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文识别了Kubernetes集群中五种证据边界（H1-H5），这些机制导致诊断性操作事件被永久丢弃。为应对这一问题，论文扩展了操作记忆架构（OMA），新增两个因果模式（P004）、两个Go监视器（EventWatcher, EphemeralWatcher）和两个存储表（scheduler_events, ephemeral_exits），并在Minikube和AKS上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kubernetes原生事件保留模型会按照确定性调度规则系统性地丢弃最具诊断价值的上下文信息，导致关键运维证据永久不可恢复，严重影响集群故障诊断能力。

Method: 通过形式化定义五种证据边界（H1-H5），扩展操作记忆架构（OMA），新增归因模式P004（调度决策溯源）、两个Go监视器（EventWatcher, EphemeralWatcher）和两个存储表（scheduler_events, ephemeral_exits），实现跨边界因果链捕获。

Result: 在Minikube（3节点）和AKS 1.32.10上完成验证。原始30轮统计延迟分析（242条边，周期内平均0.702ms）和压力评估（20个pod时2.86事件/秒，8.8MB RAM）被延续，并补充了H2、H3、H5的结果。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效跨越五种证据边界，恢复被Kubernetes原生机制丢弃的诊断上下文，首次实现了从调度器证据到下游OOMKill故障的跨边界因果链关联。

Abstract: Kubernetes clusters generate rich operational events during pod lifecycle transitions, yet the platform's native event retention model systematically discards the most diagnostically valuable context through multiple evidence destruction mechanisms operating on deterministic schedules. We formalize these mechanisms as an evidence horizon taxonomy: five distinct boundaries after which specific categories of diagnostic context become permanently unrecoverable from the Kubernetes API. H1(LastTerminationState rotation, ~90s) destroys container failure forensics; H2 (scheduler event pruning, 1hr/1000-event cluster limit) destroys placement rationale; H3 (ephemeral container exit, immediate) destroys debug session context; H4 (kubelet reconciliation gap) destroys in-memory operational state; and H5 (scrape-interval blind spot) renders sub-interval pod lifetimes invisible to poll-based observability tools.
  This paper extends the Operational Memory Architecture (OMA) to address the full evidence horizon taxonomy. Two new causal patterns are defined: P004 (Scheduler Decision Provenance) captures FailedScheduling predicate failures before kube-apiserver TTL pruning and demonstrates the first cross-horizon causal chain linking scheduler evidence to downstream OOMKill failures.
  Two new Go watchers (EventWatcher, EphemeralWatcher) and two new storage tables (scheduler_events, ephemeral_exits) extend the original architecture. Validated on Minikube (3-node) and AKS 1.32.10. The original 30-run statistical latency analysis (242 edges, intra-cycle mean 0.702ms) and stress evaluation (2.86 events/sec at 20 pods, 8.8MB RAM) are carried forward and augmented with H2, H3, and H5 results.

</details>


### [386] [RepliCore: Reproducible Parallel Simulation under Asynchronous Browser Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02529)
*Anqing Chen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RepliCore提出了通过控制逻辑状态可见性而非执行重放来实现浏览器异步并行模拟的可复现性，在真实浏览器环境中跨不同工作配置和调度条件下产生比特级一致输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 浏览器模拟依赖异步运行时机制（事件循环、渲染回调、Web Workers），导致模拟进度取决于运行时时序和回调调度行为，使得大规模模拟难以复现。

Method: 将异步运行时进度与外部可观测的逻辑状态可见性分离，仅在逻辑状态稳定后才暴露运行时可见的模拟状态，防止渲染回调和异步任务在并行推进期间观察到瞬态中间逻辑状态。

Result: 在真实浏览器环境中，不同工作线程配置、调度条件和渲染频率下均产生比特级一致输出；消融实验表明放松关键执行约束后会出现系统性状态发散。

Conclusion: 通过受控的逻辑状态可见性稳定化，无需依赖执行重放或显式调度控制，即可实现可复现的异步并行模拟。

Abstract: Browser-based simulations execute over asynchronous runtime mechanisms including event loops, rendering callbacks, and independently scheduled Web Workers, causing simulation progression to depend on runtime timing and callback scheduling behavior. RepliCore addresses this problem by separating asynchronous runtime progression from externally observable logical-state visibility. Runtime-visible simulation states are exposed only after logical-state progression becomes externally stable, preventing asynchronous runtime activities from observing partially updated simulation progression. The framework prevents rendering callbacks and asynchronous runtime tasks from observing transient intermediate logical states during parallel progression. This organization maintains consistency between parallel and sequential execution. Based on this model, we implement RepliCore, a browser-oriented deterministic parallel simulation framework for reproducible large-scale simulation under asynchronous browser runtimes. Experiments in real browser environments produce bitwise-identical outputs across varying worker configurations, scheduling conditions, and rendering frequencies while remaining practical for large-scale browser-oriented workloads. Additional ablation experiments show systematic state divergence after relaxing key execution constraints. These results indicate that reproducible asynchronous parallel simulation can be achieved through controlled logical-state visibility stabilization without relying on execution replay or explicit schedule control.

</details>


### [387] [ETALE: Evolving Topology with Accelerated Lock-free Execution for Dynamic Graph ANN Search on GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02543)
*Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: ETALE是首个支持流式插入和删除且无需全局重建的GPU原生图ANN索引，通过无锁写时复制石板图结构实现高效动态更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的GPU图ANN索引为静态集合设计，更新时需要重建受影响窗口；而支持增量更新的动态图索引运行在CPU上，无法利用GPU的并行能力。因此需要一种GPU原生且支持高效动态更新的图ANN索引。

Method: 提出ETALE索引，核心是一个无锁的写时复制(copy-on-write)石板图(slab graph)，其删除状态和邻接关系共享单个原子发布的字，从而实现可证明的删除单调性不变性，以及累积删除次数的次线性GPU内存有界回收。

Result: 在五个多样化多模态数据集上，与Tagore(GPU静态)、DIGRA(CPU动态)、CAGRA(GPU静态)和HNSW(CPU动态)四个SOTA索引对比。在持续变更下，ETALE每轮数百毫秒即可维持召回率>0.95，比CAGRA的逐窗口重建快4.8-8.8倍，比Tagore快1.8-2.5倍，比CPU动态索引快3.3-147倍。内存占用有界，而仅使用墓碑的系统会无限增长。

Conclusion: ETALE是首个真正GPU原生支持流式插入和删除的图ANN索引，在不牺牲召回率的前提下显著提升动态更新效率，且内存占用可控。

Abstract: Graph-based approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) indexes on the GPU are originally built for static collections and must reconstruct the affected window to absorb any update, while the dynamic graph indexes that update incrementally run on the CPU and cannot exploit GPU parallelism. This paper presents a new ANN index, namely ETALE (Evolving Topology with Accelerated Lock-free Execution), which is among the first GPU-native graph ANN indexes to support streaming insertion and deletion without a global rebuild. The core of ETALE is a lock-free copy-on-write slab graph whose deletion state and adjacency share a single atomically published word, which yields a provable deletion-monotonicity invariant together with a bounded reclaim of GPU memory that is sublinear in accumulated deletions. We have implemented ETALE in CUDA and evaluate it on five diverse multimodal datasets against four state-of-the-art indexes: Tagore (GPU static, SIGMOD'26), DIGRA (CPU dynamic, SIGMOD'26), CAGRA (GPU static, ICDE'24), and HNSW (CPU dynamic, TPAMI'20). Under continuous churn, ETALE maintains the index in hundreds of milliseconds per round at recall above $0.95$, which is $4.8$--$8.8\times$ faster than CAGRA's per-window rebuild, $1.8$--$2.5\times$ faster than the more recent Tagore, and $3.3$--$147\times$ faster than the CPU dynamic indexes. In addition, the memory footprint of ETALE stays bounded where tombstone-only systems grow indefinitely.

</details>


### [388] [Not Every Sync Is Safe: Calibrated DiLoCo Scheduling for Shared AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02544)
*Maxwell Twelftree,David Lemphers,An-chi He,Yue Yang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文指出现有DiLoCo调度研究缺乏“匹配随机延迟”这一关键对照基线，导致声称的收益无法归因于窗口选择。作者提出WA-DiLoCo分数控制器和校准协议，在突发负载下结合EWMA预测可显著降低SLO违规率，并给出了可部署的评估协议建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DiLoCo风格训练的调度研究在评估工作负载感知策略时，缺少匹配随机延迟这一控制基线（继承控制器同步预算但不可部署），导致声称的性能提升无法被归因于窗口选择策略本身。这一遗漏使得许多研究结果的有效性存疑。

Method: 提出1）WA-DiLoCo：基于分数的在线控制器，权衡学习者进度（learner progress）与集群压力（fleet pressure）；2）校准协议：确定何时可以击败匹配随机延迟；3）在突发负载下加入单步EWMA突发预测；4）提出可部署的评估协议：报告真实sidecar效应量迁移、无同步负载匹配、以及匹配随机延迟的上界。

Result: 在突发负载下，加入EWMA预测的在线控制器在真实vLLM sidecar回放中SLO违规率从6.54%降至5.09%（8/10种子，p=0.021）；离线Calibrated-WA（不可部署边界）显示剩余空间为4.45%对6.26%。匹配随机延迟在大多数无预测策略中表现持平或更好。

Conclusion: 匹配随机延迟是评估调度策略时不可省略的关键基线；在突发负载下结合EWMA预测可以超越该基线；未来研究应遵循提出的评估协议以保障结论的可信度。

Abstract: DiLoCo-style training reduces communication by letting learner islands train locally before occasional outer synchronization, making it attractive for fragmented industrial AI fleets where training shares hardware with latency-sensitive serving. The question for such fleets is when an outer merge is worth its system cost, and whether choosing \emph{which} windows to defer matters at all. Existing scheduling studies evaluate workload-aware policies against fixed-period baselines, but most omit the control that isolates timing from budget: matched random deferral, which inherits the controller's synchronization budget but is not itself deployable. This omission is consequential: across controlled stress tests and real vLLM sidecar replays, matched random ties or beats every forecast-free policy we test, so gains reported against weaker baselines cannot be attributed to window choice. We fill this gap with Workload-Aware DiLoCo (WA-DiLoCo), a score-based controller that weighs learner progress against fleet pressure, and a calibration protocol that determines when matched random can be beaten, then demonstrate that it can. In the bursty regime where calibration exposes request-overlap structure, adding a one-step EWMA burst forecast to the online controller beats matched random in real vLLM sidecar replay, reducing SLO violations from 6.54\% to 5.09\% (8 of 10 seeds, $p=0.021$); offline Calibrated-WA, a non-deployable bound, shows the remaining headroom at 4.45\% versus 6.26\%. The deployable lesson remains the protocol: report real-sidecar effect-size transfer, a no-sync load match, and a matched-random envelope before claiming serving-SLO improvement.

</details>


### [389] [MLSYSIM: First-Principles Infrastructure Modeling for Machine Learning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02558)
*Vijay Janapa Reddi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: MLSYSIM是一个基于第一性原理的分析框架，通过需求-供给抽象和维度严格的Python引擎，实现对从微瓦级微控制器到吉瓦级数据中心的全栈ML系统架构推理，在亚秒级时间内完成设计空间探索并识别22类系统瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临两难：经验式剖析需要目标硬件在手，周期精确仿真耗费数小时每配置，缺乏能够快速进行全栈架构推理的工具，导致跨尺度（从亚毫瓦微控制器到多吉瓦数据中心）的ML系统推理极为困难。

Method: 基于第一性原理构建维度严格的Python分析引擎，采用需求-供给抽象解耦计算需求与硅供给及环境上下文，运行时强制单位完整性防止静默转换错误，所有输入来自带类型和来源追踪的注册表，并构建了由22类"系统墙"和28个可组合模型/求解器组成的分类体系。

Result: 实现了亚秒级设计空间探索，能够识别绑定约束（系统瓶颈）并综合出跨越整个ML系统生命周期的理想硬件规格。

Conclusion: MLSYSIM填补了快速全栈架构推理工具的空白，通过严格的第一性原理建模和维度系统，为跨尺度的ML系统基础设施设计提供了可行的分析工具。

Abstract: As machine learning shifts from laboratory curiosity to critical infrastructure, the systems that sustain it span an extraordinary range, from sub-milliwatt microcontrollers to multi-gigawatt datacenter fleets. Reasoning across this range is hard: empirical profiling requires the target hardware in hand, while cycle-accurate simulation costs hours per configuration, leaving no tool for rapid, full-stack architectural reasoning. We present MLSYSIM (Machine Learning Systems Infrastructure Modeling), a first-principles analytical framework that formalizes the "physics of systems" into a dimensionally-strict Python engine. MLSysim is built on a demand-supply abstraction that decouples computational demand from silicon supply and environmental context, and it enforces unit integrity at runtime so the silent conversion errors that plague ad-hoc modeling cannot occur. Every input is drawn from a typed, provenance-tracked registry, so no number enters an analysis without a documented source.
